640	
641	## 5. 渲染引擎设计
642	
643	### 5.1 窗口类型注册机制
644	
645	```python
646	# dashboard/registry.py
647	
648	from typing import Callable, Protocol
649	from dataclasses import dataclass
650	
651	@dataclass
652	class PanelContext:
653	    """渲染上下文，传递给每个panel渲染函数"""
654	    panel_config: dict          # 该panel的配置（含data_binding, options）
655	    data: dict                  # 完整的dashboard_data
656	    resolved_data: dict         # data_binding解析后的数据
657	    panel_id: str              # panel唯一ID
658	
659	@dataclass
660	class PanelRenderResult:
661	    """渲染结果"""
662	    html_content: str              # panel body内的HTML（div容器或table）
663	    echarts_option: dict | None   # ECharts配置（非图表类型为None）
664	
665	class PanelRenderer(Protocol):
666	    """窗口渲染器协议"""
667	    def __call__(self, ctx: PanelContext) -> PanelRenderResult:
668	        """返回该panel的渲染结果"""
669	        ...
670	
671	# 全局注册表
672	_PANEL_REGISTRY: dict[str, PanelRenderer] = {}
673	
674	def register_panel(panel_type: str):
675	    """
676	    装饰器：注册一个窗口类型渲染器。
677	    
678	    用法：
679	        @register_panel("line_chart")
680	        def render_line_chart(ctx: PanelContext) -> PanelRenderResult:
681	            ...
682	    """
683	    def decorator(func: PanelRenderer) -> PanelRenderer:
684	        if panel_type in _PANEL_REGISTRY:
685	            raise ValueError(f"Panel type '{panel_type}' already registered")
686	        _PANEL_REGISTRY[panel_type] = func
687	        return func
688	    return decorator
689	
690	def get_renderer(panel_type: str) -> PanelRenderer:
691	    """获取已注册的渲染器，未注册则抛出明确错误"""
692	    if panel_type not in _PANEL_REGISTRY:
693	        available = ", ".join(sorted(_PANEL_REGISTRY.keys()))
694	        raise KeyError(
695	            f"Unknown panel type '{panel_type}'. Available: {available}"
696	        )
697	    return _PANEL_REGISTRY[panel_type]
698	
699	def validate_config_panels(panels: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
700	    """校验配置中所有panel的type是否已注册，返回错误列表"""
701	    errors = []
702	    for p in panels:
703	        if p["type"] not in _PANEL_REGISTRY:
704	            errors.append(f"Panel '{p['id']}': unknown type '{p['type']}'")
705	    return errors
706	
707	def wrap_echarts_div(panel_id: str, option: dict, height: str = "100%") -> PanelRenderResult:
708	    """将ECharts option包装为div + option结构"""
709	    html = f'<div id="chart-{panel_id}" style="width:100%;height:{height};"></div>'
710	    return PanelRenderResult(html_content=html, echarts_option=option)
711	```
712	
713	### 5.2 各窗口类型渲染逻辑
714	
715	#### 5.2.1 line_chart（多模型预测曲线）
716	
717	```python
718	@register_panel("line_chart")
719	def render_line_chart(ctx: PanelContext) -> PanelRenderResult:
720	    """
721	    生成ECharts折线图配置。
722	    
723	    逻辑：
724	    1. 从resolved_data获取所有模型的y_pred
725	    2. 按rank_by指标排序，取top_n个模型
726	    3. y_test从resolved_data["y_test"]获取（来自__y_test__字段）
727	    4. 生成ECharts option JSON
728	    """
729	    option = {
730	        "tooltip": {"trigger": "axis"},
731	        "legend": {"data": model_names + ["实际值"], "textStyle": {"color": "#ccc"}},
732	        "xAxis": {"type": "category", "data": time_labels},
733	        "yAxis": {"type": "value", "name": "需求量"},
734	        "series": [
735	            {"name": model, "type": "line", "data": y_pred, "smooth": True},
736	            {"name": "实际值", "type": "line", "data": y_test,
737	             "lineStyle": {"type": "dashed"}, "symbol": "circle", "symbolSize": 6}
738	        ]
739	    }
740	    return wrap_echarts_div(ctx.panel_id, option)
741	```
742	
743	#### 5.2.2 rank_table（指标排名表格）
744	
745	```python
746	@register_panel("rank_table")
747	def render_rank_table(ctx: PanelContext) -> PanelRenderResult:
748	    """
749	    生成HTML表格（非ECharts）。
750	    
751	    逻辑：
752	    1. 按group_by聚合（model维度：对每个模型取所有物资的指标均值）
753	    2. 按metric排序（desc/asc）
754	    3. 取top_n行
755	    4. 生成带样式的HTML table，前3名高亮
756	    """
757	    # 输出纯HTML table，CSS类控制暗色主题样式
758	    return PanelRenderResult(html_content=table_html, echarts_option=None)
759	```
760	
761	#### 5.2.3 bar_chart（指标对比柱状图）
762	
763	```python
764	@register_panel("bar_chart")
765	def render_bar_chart(ctx: PanelContext) -> PanelRenderResult:
766	    """
767	    生成ECharts柱状图。
768	    
769	    支持：
770	    - horizontal: 横向/纵向
771	    - sort: 排序方向
772	    - show_value: 柱顶/柱侧显示数值
773	    """
774	    option = {
775	        "tooltip": {"trigger": "axis"},
776	        "xAxis": {"type": "category" if not horizontal else "value", "data": categories},
777	        "yAxis": {"type": "value" if not horizontal else "category", "data": categories if horizontal else None},
778	        "series": [{
779	            "type": "bar",
780	            "data": values,
781	            "itemStyle": {"color": color},
782	            "label": {"show": show_value, "position": "top" if not horizontal else "right"}
783	        }]
784	    }
785	    return wrap_echarts_div(ctx.panel_id, option)
786	```
787	
788	#### 5.2.4 markdown_panel（AI总结/文本）
789	
790	```python
791	@register_panel("markdown_panel")
792	def render_markdown_panel(ctx: PanelContext) -> PanelRenderResult:
793	    """
794	    渲染Markdown文本为HTML。
795	    
796	    逻辑：
797	    1. 从resolved_data["content"]获取Markdown文本
798	    2. 使用简单的Markdown→HTML转换（内置轻量解析，不引入额外依赖）
799	       - 支持：标题、加粗、列表、换行
800	    3. 包裹在带滚动条的div中
801	    """
802	    return PanelRenderResult(html_content=html_div, echarts_option=None)
803	```
804	
805	#### 5.2.5 gauge（仪表盘）
806	
807	```python
808	@register_panel("gauge")
809	def render_gauge(ctx: PanelContext) -> PanelRenderResult:
810	    """
811	    生成ECharts仪表盘。
812	    
813	    逻辑：
814	    1. 取resolved_data["value"]作为当前值
815	    2. 根据thresholds设置颜色分段
816	    3. inverse=true时（如sMAPE，越低越好），颜色反转
817	    """
818	    option = {
819	        "series": [{
820	            "type": "gauge",
821	            "min": min_val, "max": max_val,
822	            "data": [{"value": value, "name": label}],
823	            "axisLine": {"lineStyle": {"color": threshold_colors}},
824	            "detail": {"formatter": "{value}"},
825	            "title": {"color": "#ccc"}
826	        }]
827	    }
828	    return wrap_echarts_div(ctx.panel_id, option)
829	```
830	
831	#### 5.2.6 heatmap（物资×模型热力图）
832	
833	```python
834	@register_panel("heatmap")
835	def render_heatmap(ctx: PanelContext) -> PanelRenderResult:
836	    """
837	    生成ECharts热力图。
838	    
839	    data_binding字段说明：
840	    - x_axis: 路径表达式，解析为模型名称列表
841	    - y_axis: 路径表达式，解析为物资名称列表
842	    - metric: 控制参数（非路径表达式），指定从metrics中取哪个指标
843	    
844	    逻辑：
845	    1. x_axis = models, y_axis = materials
846	    2. 遍历 results[material][model].metrics[metric] 构建 [x_idx, y_idx, value] 数组
847	       （跳过__y_test__等非模型key）
848	    3. visualMap 映射颜色
849	    """
850	    metric = ctx.resolved_data["metric"]  # 控制参数，直接透传的字符串
851	    data_points = []
852	    for y_idx, material in enumerate(materials):
853	        for x_idx, model in enumerate(models):
854	            val = data["results"][material][model]["metrics"][metric]
855	            data_points.append([x_idx, y_idx, round(val, 3)])
856	
857	    option = {
858	        "tooltip": {"position": "top"},
859	        "xAxis": {"type": "category", "data": models, "axisLabel": {"rotate": 30}},
860	        "yAxis": {"type": "category", "data": materials},
861	        "visualMap": {
862	            "min": min_val, "max": max_val,
863	            "inRange": {"color": color_range},
864	            "calculable": True
865	        },
866	        "series": [{
867	            "type": "heatmap",
868	            "data": data_points,
869	            "label": {"show": show_value}
870	        }]
871	    }
872	    return wrap_echarts_div(ctx.panel_id, option)
873	```
874	
875	### 5.3 Jinja2模板结构
876	
877	```
878	dashboard/templates/
879	├── base.html              # 主模板：HTML骨架 + CSS + JS初始化
880	├── macros.html            # 可复用宏：panel_container, echarts_init
881	└── partials/
882	    └── panel_wrapper.html # 单个panel的div容器
883	```
884	
885	**base.html 核心结构**：
886	
887	```html
888	<!DOCTYPE html>
889	<html lang="zh-CN">
890	<head>
891	    <meta charset="UTF-8">
892	    <title>{{ title }}</title>
893	    <style>
894	        :root {
895	            --bg-primary: #181b1f;
896	            --bg-panel: #212427;
897	            --border-color: #2c3033;
898	            --text-primary: #d8d9da;
899	            --text-secondary: #8e8e8e;
900	            --accent-blue: #3274d9;
901	        }
902	        body {
903	            background: var(--bg-primary);
904	            color: var(--text-primary);
905	            margin: 0;
906	            font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto,
907	                         "Helvetica Neue", Arial, "Noto Sans SC", sans-serif,
908	                         "Apple Color Emoji", "Segoe UI Emoji";
909	        }
910	        .dashboard-header { padding: 20px 24px 8px; }
911	        .dashboard-grid {
912	            display: grid;
913	            grid-template-columns: repeat({{ layout.columns }}, 1fr);
914	            grid-auto-rows: {{ layout.row_height }}px;
915	            gap: {{ layout.gap }}px;
916	            padding: {{ layout.padding }}px;
917	        }
918	        .panel {
919	            background: var(--bg-panel);
920	            border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
921	            border-radius: 4px;
922	            padding: 12px;
923	            overflow: hidden;
924	        }
925	        .panel-title { font-size: 13px; color: var(--text-secondary); margin-bottom: 8px; }
926	        .panel-error {
927	            color: #f5222d;
928	            font-size: 12px;
929	            padding: 8px;
930	            border: 1px dashed #f5222d;
931	            border-radius: 4px;
932	        }
933	    </style>
934	</head>
935	<body>
936	    <div class="dashboard-header">
937	        <h1>{{ title }}</h1>
938	        <p>{{ subtitle }} · 生成时间：{{ generated_at }}</p>
939	    </div>
940	    <div class="dashboard-grid">
941	        {% for panel in panels %}
942	        <div class="panel" style="grid-column: {{ panel.grid.col }} / span {{ panel.grid.col_span }}; grid-row: {{ panel.grid.row }} / span {{ panel.grid.row_span }};">
943	            <div class="panel-title">{{ panel.title }}</div>
944	            <div class="panel-body" id="panel-body-{{ panel.id }}">
945	                {{ panel.html_content }}
946	            </div>
947	        </div>
948	        {% endfor %}
949	    </div>
950	
951	    <!-- ECharts 内联 -->
952	    <script>{{ echarts_source }}</script>
953	
954	    <!-- 各panel的初始化脚本（统一resize监听） -->
955	    <script>
956	    (function() {
957	        var charts = [];
958	
959	        {% for panel in panels %}
960	        {% if panel.echarts_option %}
961	        (function() {
962	            try {
963	                var el = document.getElementById('chart-{{ panel.id }}');
964	                if (!el) return;
965	                var chart = echarts.init(el);
966	                chart.setOption({{ panel.echarts_option | tojson }});
967	                charts.push(chart);
968	            } catch (e) {
969	                console.error('[Dashboard] Panel "{{ panel.id }}" 初始化失败:', e);
970	                var body = document.getElementById('panel-body-{{ panel.id }}');
971	                if (body) body.innerHTML = '<div class="panel-error">图表渲染失败: ' + e.message + '</div>';
972	            }
973	        })();
974	        {% endif %}
975	        {% endfor %}
976	
977	        /* 统一resize监听器 */
978	        window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
979	            for (var i = 0; i < charts.length; i++) {
980	                charts[i].resize();
981	            }
982	        });
983	
984	        /* minmax响应式：窗口尺寸变化时重新计算 */
985	        var resizeTimer;
986	        window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
987	            clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
988	            resizeTimer = setTimeout(function() {
989	                for (var i = 0; i < charts.length; i++) {
990	                    charts[i].resize();
991	                }
992	            }, 150);
993	        });
994	    })();
995	    </script>
996	</body>
997	</html>
998	```
999	
1000	**设计说明**：
1001	- **字体**：使用系统字体栈（-apple-system, Segoe UI, Roboto等），不引用Web字体'Inter'，确保离线环境正常显示
1002	- **Panel定位**：使用inline style直接输出grid定位，无需动态生成CSS类名
1003	- **ECharts容错**：每个IIFE内加try/catch，初始化失败时显示错误信息而非白屏
1004	- **统一resize**：所有chart实例收集到数组，统一一个resize监听器，避免重复绑定
1005	- **minmax响应式**：debounce处理resize事件，防止频繁触发
1006	
1007	### 5.4 ECharts配置生成逻辑
1008	
1009	每个渲染器返回统一的`PanelRenderResult`结构：
1010	
1011	```python
1012	@dataclass
1013	class PanelRenderResult:
1014	    html_content: str              # panel body内的HTML（div容器或table）
1015	    echarts_option: dict | None   # ECharts配置（非图表类型为None）
1016	
1017	def wrap_echarts_div(panel_id: str, option: dict, height: str = "100%") -> PanelRenderResult:
1018	    """将ECharts option包装为div + option结构"""
1019	    html = f'<div id="chart-{panel_id}" style="width:100%;height:{height};"></div>'
1020	    return PanelRenderResult(html_content=html, echarts_option=option)
1021	```
1022	
1023	### 5.5 ECharts定制构建建议
1024	
1025	当前方案使用ECharts完整包（~1MB），如需减小体积，可采用定制构建：
1026	
1027	```bash
1028	# 仅包含本项目用到的图表类型
1029	npx echarts-build \
1030	  --charts line,bar,gauge,heatmap \
1031	  --components tooltip,legend,grid,visualMap \
1032	  --output dashboard/static/echarts.custom.min.js
1033	```
1034	
1035	定制构建后体积约400KB，减少60%。此为可选优化，不影响功能完整性。
1036	
1037	---
1038	
1039	## 6. AI总结规则引擎
1040	
1041	### 6.1 规则定义方式
1042	
1043	```python
1044	from string import Template
1045	
1046	@dataclass
1047	class SummaryRule:
1048	    """总结规则定义"""
1049	    id: str                           # 规则唯一标识
1050	    name: str                         # 规则名称（用于日志）
1051	    priority: int                     # 优先级（越小越先输出）
1052	    condition: Callable[[dict], bool] # 触发条件，输入dashboard_data
1053	    template: str                     # 输出模板，使用string.Template的$var语法
1054	    extractor: Callable[[dict], dict] # 从data中提取模板变量
1055	
1056	# 规则注册
1057	_SUMMARY_RULES: list[SummaryRule] = []
1058	
1059	def register_rule(rule: SummaryRule):
1060	    _SUMMARY_RULES.append(rule)
1061	    _SUMMARY_RULES.sort(key=lambda r: r.priority)
1062	```
1063	
1064	**模板格式化**：使用`string.Template`（`$var`语法）替代`str.format()`，避免与JSON/Markdown中的花括号冲突：
1065	
1066	```python
1067	# 示例规则
1068	register_rule(SummaryRule(
1069	    id="best_model",
1070	    name="最佳模型",
1071	    priority=10,
1072	    condition=lambda data: True,
1073	    template="综合R²表现，**${model}**以均值${value}位列第一，在${win_count}/${total}种物资上取得最优。",
1074	    extractor=lambda data: {
1075	        "model": data["aggregations"]["overall_best"]["model"],
1076	        "value": f"{data['aggregations']['overall_best']['R2']:.2f}",
1077	        "win_count": ...,
1078	        "total": len(data["meta"]["materials"])
1079	    }
1080	))
1081	```
1082	
1083	### 6.2 内置规则列表
1084	
1085	| 规则ID | 名称 | 触发条件 | 输出示例 |
1086	|--------|------|----------|----------|
1087	| `best_model` | 最佳模型 | 始终触发 | "综合R²表现，**Transformer**以均值0.94位列第一，在4/5种物资上取得最优。" |
1088	| `worst_material` | 最难预测物资 | 始终触发 | "**交流支柱绝缘子**预测难度最大，最佳模型R²仅为0.72，建议增加特征或调整预测策略。" |
1089	| `anomaly_detect` | 异常检测 | 存在R²<0.5的物资×模型组合 | "检测到异常：**Linear**在**电抗器保护**上R²=0.23，远低于均值，可能存在数据质量问题。" |
1090	| `zero_demand` | 零需求特征 | zero_acc < 0.8 | "注意：**交流避雷器**存在大量零需求月份，**GRU**的零值准确率仅67%，建议使用零膨胀模型。" |
1091	| `model_consensus` | 模型共识 | Top3模型在某物资上预测序列Pearson相关系数 > 0.95 | "**断路器保护**的Top3模型预测趋势高度一致（Pearson > 0.95）：下半年需求上升约15%。" |
1092	| `overall_quality` | 整体质量评估 | 始终触发 | "整体预测质量：R²均值0.87（良好），sMAPE均值8.3%（可接受），75/75组合R²>0.6。" |
1093	
1094	**model_consensus 数学定义**：
1095	
1096	对于某物资的Top3模型（按R²排序），取其y_pred序列两两计算Pearson相关系数：
1097	
1098	```python
1099	import numpy as np
1100	
1101	def check_consensus(y_preds: list[list[float]], threshold: float = 0.95) -> bool:
1102	    """
1103	    判断Top3模型预测趋势是否一致。
1104	    
1105	    条件：任意两个模型的y_pred序列的Pearson相关系数均 > threshold。
1106	    """
1107	    for i in range(len(y_preds)):
1108	        for j in range(i + 1, len(y_preds)):
1109	            corr = np.corrcoef(y_preds[i], y_preds[j])[0, 1]
1110	            if corr <= threshold:
1111	                return False
1112	    return True
1113	```
1114	
1115	**动态物资遍历要求**：`model_consensus` 规则必须遍历 `data["meta"]["materials"]` 中的所有物资，对每个物资独立执行共识检测，仅输出存在共识的物资。物资增减时自动适配，无需修改规则代码：
1116	
1117	```python
1118	@SummaryEngine.rule
1119	def model_consensus_rule(data: dict) -> str | None:
1120	    materials = data["meta"]["materials"]  # 动态获取
1121	    consensus_materials = []
1122	    for material in materials:
1123	        results = data["results"][material]
1124	        # 按R2排序取Top3
1125	        top3 = sorted(results.items(), key=lambda x: x[1]["metrics"]["R2"], reverse=True)[:3]
1126	        y_preds = [r[1]["y_pred"] for r in top3]
1127	        if check_consensus(y_preds):
1128	            consensus_materials.append(material)
1129	    if not consensus_materials:
1130	        return None
1131	    return f"**{'、'.join(consensus_materials)}**的Top3模型预测趋势高度一致（Pearson > 0.95）。"
1132	```
1133	
1134	### 6.3 输出格式
1135	
1136	```python
1137	class SummaryEngine:
1138	    def generate(self, data: dict, mode: str = "rule") -> str:
1139	        """
1140	        生成AI总结文本。
1141	        
1142	        Args:
1143	            data: dashboard_data完整结构
1144	            mode: "rule"=规则引擎（人运行时），"agent"=预留agent填充接口
1145	        
1146	        Returns:
1147	            Markdown格式的总结文本
1148	            
1149	        Raises:
1150	            ValueError: mode不是"rule"或"agent"时抛出
1151	        """
1152	        if mode == "rule":
1153	            return self._rule_based_generate(data)
1154	        elif mode == "agent":
1155	            return self._agent_placeholder(data)
1156	        else:
1157	            raise ValueError(f"Unknown summary mode: '{mode}'. Expected 'rule' or 'agent'.")
1158	
1159	    def _rule_based_generate(self, data: dict) -> str:
1160	        sections = []
1161	        for rule in _SUMMARY_RULES:
1162	            if rule.condition(data):
1163	                variables = rule.extractor(data)
1164	                text = Template(rule.template).safe_substitute(variables)
1165	                sections.append(text)
1166	        return "\n\n".join(sections)
1167	
1168	    def _agent_placeholder(self, data: dict) -> str:
1169	        """agent模式：输出结构化数据供外部agent消费"""
1170	        return json.dumps({
1171	            "task": "generate_summary",
1172	            "data_summary": {
1173	                "materials": data["meta"]["materials"],
1174	                "overall_r2_mean": ...,
1175	                "anomalies": [...],
1176	            },
1177	            "instruction": "请基于以上数据生成200字以内的预测效果总结"
1178	        }, ensure_ascii=False)
1179	```
1180	
1181	### 6.4 总结上下文层（__context__）
1182	
1183	AI总结文本作为独立的context层存储，与业务数据分离：
1184	
1185	```python
1186	# Builder中的处理
1187	context = {
1188	    "summary": SummaryEngine().generate(data, mode="rule"),
1189	    "generated_at": data["generated_at"],
1190	}
1191	
1192	# 配置中通过 $.__context__.summary 引用
1193	# data_binding: {"content": "$.__context__.summary"}
1194	```
1195	
1196	**设计理由**：
1197	- `__context__`是渲染时注入的上下文层，不属于业务数据（results/aggregations）
1198	- 避免在dashboard_data中混入非数据字段
1199	- DataBindingResolver解析时，`$`根节点包含`data`和`__context__`两个顶层key
1200	
1201	### 6.5 Agent模式数据接口
1202	
1203	当以agent模式运行时，`SummaryEngine`输出一个JSON结构，外部agent可：
1204	
1205	1. 读取该payload
1206	2. 调用LLM生成自然语言总结
1207	3. 将结果写回context层
1208	4. 再调用`DashboardBuilder.render()`完成渲染
1209	
1210	```python
1211	def get_agent_payload(data: dict) -> dict:
1212	    """提取供agent使用的精简数据"""
1213	    return {
1214	        "overall_metrics": data["aggregations"]["model_avg_metrics"],
1215	        "best_per_material": data["aggregations"]["best_model_per_material"],
1216	        "anomalies": detect_anomalies(data),
1217	        "zero_demand_materials": [...],
1218	        "forecast_trend": {...}
1219	    }
1220	```
1221

1264	### 7.2 各文件职责

1285	## 8. 集成方案
1286	
1287	### 8.1 main.py修改位置
1288	
1289	```python
1290	# ============ main.py 修改示意（5个插入点，约15行） ============
1291	
1292	# 【插入点1】文件头部，安全导入（4行）
1293	try:
1294	    from dashboard import DashboardBuilder, ResultCollector
1295	    DASHBOARD_AVAILABLE = True
1296	except ImportError as e:
1297	    DASHBOARD_AVAILABLE = False
1298	    print(f"[Warning] Dashboard模块不可用: {e}")
1299	
1300	# 【插入点2】argparse部分，新增参数（4行）
1301	parser.add_argument('--no-dashboard', action='store_true', help='禁用看板输出')
1302	parser.add_argument('--dashboard-config', default=None, help='看板配置文件路径')
1303	parser.add_argument('--dashboard-output', default=None, help='看板输出路径')
1304	parser.add_argument('--dashboard-debug', action='store_true', help='输出调试数据')
1305	
1306	# 【插入点3】预测主循环开始前（3行）
1307	collector = ResultCollector() if DASHBOARD_AVAILABLE else None
1308	if collector:
1309	    collector.set_meta(time_labels=["1月", "2月", "3月", "4月", "5月", "6月",
1310	                                     "7月", "8月", "9月", "10月", "11月", "12月"],
1311	                       forecast_horizon=12)
1312	
1313	# 【插入点4】在每个模型预测完成后（2行）
1314	if collector:
1315	    collector.collect(material, model_name, all_results[material][model_name])
1316	
1317	# 【插入点5】全部预测完成后（4行）
1318	if collector and not args.no_dashboard:
1319	    DashboardBuilder(
1320	        collector=collector,
1321	        config_path=args.dashboard_config,
1322	        output_path=args.dashboard_output,
1323	        debug=args.dashboard_debug
1324	    ).build()
1325	```
1326	
1327	### 8.2 三输出共享数据
1328	
1329	```
1330	                    all_results (main.py原有)
1331	                         │
1332	          ┌──────────────┼──────────────┐
1333	          ▼              ▼              ▼
1334	    PNG绘图逻辑     日志输出逻辑    collector.collect()
1335	   （现有不变）    （现有不变）         │
1336	                                      ▼
1337	                              DashboardBuilder
1338	                              （新增，独立）
1339	```
1340	
1341	**关键设计**：
1342	- `collector.collect()`接收的是与`all_results`相同的dict引用，不复制数据
1343	- PNG绘图和日志输出完全不感知dashboard模块的存在
1344	- 安全导入：dashboard模块导入失败（如缺少jinja2）时，`DASHBOARD_AVAILABLE=False`，主流程完全不受影响
1345	- `collector.collect()`内部有try/except保护，即使序列化异常也不中断预测循环
1346	
1347	### 8.3 命令行参数扩展
1348	
1349	| 参数 | 类型 | 默认值 | 说明 |
1350	|------|------|--------|------|
1351	| `--no-dashboard` | flag | False（默认生成看板） | 禁用看板生成 |
1352	| `--dashboard-config` | str | None（使用模块内默认路径） | 指定配置文件 |
1353	| `--dashboard-output` | str | None（使用默认output/dashboard.html） | 指定输出路径 |
1354	| `--dashboard-debug` | flag | False | 同时输出dashboard_data.json用于调试 |
1355	
1356	**参数传递**：所有命令行参数完整传递给DashboardBuilder构造函数：
1357	
1358	```python
1359	DashboardBuilder(
1360	    collector=collector,
1361	    config_path=args.dashboard_config,   # None时使用默认
1362	    output_path=args.dashboard_output,   # None时使用默认
1363	    debug=args.dashboard_debug
1364	).build()
1365	```

1368	
1369	## 9. 关键代码设计
1370	
1371	### 9.1 DashboardBuilder（主构建器）
1372	
1373	```python
1374	# dashboard/builder.py
1375	
1376	import os
1377	from pathlib import Path
1378	
1379	# 模块根目录（解决非项目根目录运行时相对路径失败问题）
1380	_MODULE_DIR = Path(__file__).parent
1381	
1382	class DashboardBuilder:
1383	    """看板构建器 - 编排整体流程"""
1384	
1385	    DEFAULT_CONFIG = _MODULE_DIR / "dashboard_config.json"
1386	    DEFAULT_OUTPUT = Path("output") / "dashboard.html"
1387	
1388	    def __init__(
1389	        self,
1390	        collector: ResultCollector,
1391	        config_path: str | None = None,
1392	        output_path: str | None = None,
1393	        debug: bool = False
1394	    ) -> None:
1395	        self.collector = collector
1396	        self.config_path = Path(config_path) if config_path else self.DEFAULT_CONFIG
1397	        self.output_path = Path(output_path) if output_path else self.DEFAULT_OUTPUT
1398	        self.debug = debug
1399	
1400	    def build(self) -> str:
1401	        """
1402	        执行完整构建流程，返回输出文件路径。
1403	        
1404	        流程：
1405	        0. 前置检查（数据非空）
1406	        1. 数据校验
1407	        2. 加载并校验配置
1408	        3. 序列化数据
1409	        4. 生成AI总结（存入context层）
1410	        5. 展开repeat配置
1411	        6. 渲染所有panel（每个独立try/except）
1412	        7. Jinja2模板渲染
1413	        8. 写入HTML文件
1414	        """
1415	        # Step 0: 前置检查 - collector收到空数据时提前退出
1416	        if not self.collector.materials:
1417	            print("[Dashboard] 警告：无预测数据，跳过看板生成")
1418	            return ""
1419	        
1420	        # Step 1: 数据校验
1421	        warnings = self.collector.validate()
1422	        for w in warnings:
1423	            print(f"[Dashboard] 数据警告: {w}")
1424	        
1425	        # Step 2: 配置
1426	        config = ConfigLoader(str(self.config_path)).load()
1427	        
1428	        # Step 3: 数据
1429	        data = self.collector.to_dashboard_data()
1430	        
1431	        # Step 4: AI总结 → 独立context层
1432	        context = {
1433	            "summary": SummaryEngine().generate(data, mode="rule"),
1434	            "generated_at": data["generated_at"],
1435	        }
1436	        
1437	        # Step 5: 展开repeat配置（传入layout用于自动布局计算）
1438	        panels_config = self._expand_repeats(config["panels"], data, config["layout"])
1439	        
1440	        # Step 6: 渲染panels（每个panel独立try/except降级）
1441	        rendered_panels = []
1442	        for panel_cfg in panels_config:
1443	            try:
1444	                renderer = get_renderer(panel_cfg["type"])
1445	                resolved = DataBindingResolver(data, context).resolve(
1446	                    panel_cfg.get("data_binding", {})
1447	                )
1448	                ctx = PanelContext(
1449	                    panel_config=panel_cfg,
1450	                    data=data,
1451	                    resolved_data=resolved,
1452	                    panel_id=panel_cfg["id"]
1453	                )
1454	                result = renderer(ctx)
1455	                rendered_panels.append({
1456	                    **panel_cfg,
1457	                    "html_content": result.html_content,
1458	                    "echarts_option": result.echarts_option
1459	                })
1460	            except Exception as e:
1461	                # 降级：显示错误信息，不影响其他panel
1462	                rendered_panels.append({
1463	                    **panel_cfg,
1464	                    "html_content": f'<div class="panel-error">渲染失败: {e}</div>',
1465	                    "echarts_option": None
1466	                })
1467	                print(f"[Dashboard] Panel '{panel_cfg['id']}' 渲染失败: {e}")
1468	        
1469	        # Step 7: Jinja2渲染
1470	        html = self._render_template(config, rendered_panels, data, context)
1471	        
1472	        # Step 8: 输出（确保目录存在）
1473	        output_dir = self.output_path.parent
1474	        os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
1475	        
1476	        with open(self.output_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
1477	            f.write(html)
1478	        
1479	        if self.debug:
1480	            debug_path = output_dir / "dashboard_data.json"
1481	            self._dump_debug_data(data, debug_path)
1482	        
1483	        print(f"[Dashboard] 看板已生成: {self.output_path}")
1484	        return str(self.output_path)
1485	
1486	    def _expand_repeats(self, panels: list[dict], data: dict, layout: dict) -> list[dict]:
1487	        """
1488	        展开含repeat配置的panel为多个实例。
1489	        
1490	        自动布局算法：
1491	        1. 先扫描所有非repeat panel，计算已占用的最大行号（max_fixed_row）
1492	        2. repeat panel的auto行从 max_fixed_row + 1 开始
1493	        3. 根据 layout.columns / col_span 计算每行可放置的panel数（items_per_row）
1494	        4. 按顺序分配 row 和 col
1495	        5. repeat展开后，后续非repeat panel的行号自动偏移（避免重叠）
1496	        """
1497	        columns = layout.get("columns", 12)
1498	        
1499	        # Phase 1: 计算固定panel占用的最大行号
1500	        max_fixed_row = 0
1501	        for panel in panels:
1502	            if "repeat" not in panel:
1503	                grid = panel["grid"]
1504	                panel_end_row = grid["row"] + grid["row_span"] - 1
1505	                max_fixed_row = max(max_fixed_row, panel_end_row)
1506	        
1507	        # Phase 2: 展开repeat panels
1508	        expanded = []
1509	        auto_row_cursor = max_fixed_row + 1  # auto区域起始行
1510	        
1511	        for panel in panels:
1512	            if "repeat" not in panel:
1513	                # 非repeat panel：如果行号在auto区域之后，需要偏移
1514	                expanded.append(panel)
1515	                continue
1516	            
1517	            repeat_cfg = panel["repeat"]
1518	            items = DataBindingResolver(data, {}).resolve_path(repeat_cfg["foreach"])
1519	            var_name = repeat_cfg["as"]
1520	            id_suffix_key = repeat_cfg.get("id_suffix", "idx")
1521	            col_span = panel["grid"]["col_span"]
1522	            row_span = panel["grid"]["row_span"]
1523	            items_per_row = max(1, columns // col_span)
1524	            
1525	            for idx, item in enumerate(items):
1526	                instance = self._substitute_template(panel, var_name, item, idx, id_suffix_key)
1527	                if instance["grid"].get("row") == "auto":
1528	                    row_offset = (idx // items_per_row) * row_span
1529	                    col_offset = (idx % items_per_row) * col_span
1530	                    instance["grid"]["row"] = auto_row_cursor + row_offset
1531	                    instance["grid"]["col"] = 1 + col_offset
1532	                expanded.append(instance)
1533	            
1534	            # 更新auto_row_cursor：本批repeat panels占用后的下一行
1535	            total_rows_used = ((len(items) - 1) // items_per_row + 1) * row_span
1536	            auto_row_cursor += total_rows_used
1537	        
1538	        return expanded
1539	
1540	    def _substitute_template(self, panel: dict, var_name: str, value: str,
1541	                              idx: int, id_suffix_key: str) -> dict:
1542	        """递归替换配置中的${var}占位符"""
1543	        import copy
1544	        result = copy.deepcopy(panel)
1545	        # 替换id
1546	        result["id"] = result["id"].replace(f"${{{id_suffix_key}}}", str(idx))
1547	        # 替换title
1548	        result["title"] = result["title"].replace(f"${{{var_name}}}", value)
1549	        # 替换data_binding中的表达式
1550	        for key, expr in result.get("data_binding", {}).items():
1551	            if isinstance(expr, str):
1552	                result["data_binding"][key] = expr.replace(f"${{{var_name}}}", value)
1553	        # 移除repeat字段
1554	        result.pop("repeat", None)
1555	        return result
1556	
1557	    def _render_template(self, config, panels, data, context) -> str:
1558	        """加载Jinja2模板并渲染"""
1559	        import jinja2
1560	        env = jinja2.Environment(
1561	            loader=jinja2.FileSystemLoader(str(_MODULE_DIR / "templates")),
1562	            autoescape=False
1563	        )
1564	        template = env.get_template("base.html")
1565	        
1566	        # 读取ECharts源码用于内联（指定编码）
1567	        echarts_path = _MODULE_DIR / "static" / "echarts.min.js"
1568	        with open(echarts_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
1569	            echarts_source = f.read()
1570	        
1571	        return template.render(
1572	            title=config["title"],
1573	            subtitle=config["subtitle"],
1574	            layout=config["layout"],
1575	            panels=panels,
1576	            echarts_source=echarts_source,
1577	            generated_at=context["generated_at"]
1578	        )
1579	
1580	    def _dump_debug_data(self, data: dict, path: Path) -> None:
1581	        """输出调试用JSON数据"""
1582	        with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
1583	            json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, cls=NumpyEncoder)
1584	```
1585	
1586	### 9.2 DataBindingResolver（数据绑定解析器）
1587	
1588	```python
1589	# dashboard/data_binding.py
1590	
1591	from typing import Any
1592	
1593	class DataBindingResolver:
1594	    """解析数据绑定表达式，从dashboard_data和context中提取数据"""
1595	
1596	    def __init__(self, data: dict, context: dict | None = None) -> None:
1597	        # $ 根节点包含业务数据和context层
1598	        self._root = {**data, "__context__": context or {}}
1599	
1600	    def resolve(self, bindings: dict) -> dict:
1601	        """
1602	        解析一组data_binding，返回解析后的数据字典。
1603	        
1604	        对于非表达式值（如固定字符串"R2"、数字5），直接透传。
1605	        对于以"$."开头的表达式，执行解析。
1606	        """
1607	        result = {}
1608	        for key, expr in bindings.items():
1609	            if isinstance(expr, str) and expr.startswith("$."):
1610	                result[key] = self._evaluate(expr)
1611	            else:
1612	                result[key] = expr  # 控制参数直接透传
1613	        return result
1614	
1615	    def resolve_path(self, expr: str) -> Any:
1616	        """公开方法：解析单个路径表达式（供repeat展开使用）"""
1617	        return self._evaluate(expr)
1618	
1619	    def _evaluate(self, expr: str) -> Any:
1620	        """
1621	        解析单个表达式。
1622	        
1623	        示例：
1624	            "$.results.交流避雷器.*.y_pred" → {"LSTM": [...], "GRU": [...], ...}
1625	            "$.aggregations.model_avg_metrics.*.R2 | mean" → 0.87
1626	            "$.__context__.summary" → "综合R²表现..."
1627	        """
1628	        # 分离路径和管道
1629	        parts = expr.split("|")
1630	        path = parts[0].strip()
1631	        pipes = [p.strip() for p in parts[1:]]
1632	        
1633	        # 解析路径
1634	        value = self._resolve_path(path)
1635	        
1636	        # 应用管道
1637	        for pipe in pipes:
1638	            value = self._apply_pipe(value, pipe)
1639	        
1640	        return value
1641	
1642	    def _resolve_path(self, path: str) -> Any:
1643	        """
1644	        解析$.a.b.*.c形式的路径。
1645	        
1646	        遇到*时，对当前dict的每个子value递归应用剩余segments。
1647	        """
1648	        segments = path.removeprefix("$.").split(".")
1649	        return self._resolve_segments(self._root, segments)
1650	
1651	    def _resolve_segments(self, current: Any, segments: list[str]) -> Any:
1652	        """递归解析路径segments"""
1653	        if not segments:
1654	            return current
1655	        
1656	        seg = segments[0]
1657	        remaining = segments[1:]
1658	        
1659	        if seg == "*":
1660	            # 通配：对当前dict的每个value递归应用剩余segments
1661	            if not isinstance(current, dict):
1662	                raise ValueError(f"Cannot apply '*' to non-dict type: {type(current)}")
1663	            if not remaining:
1664	                # *是最后一个segment，返回所有value
1665	                return {k: v for k, v in current.items() if not k.startswith("__")}
1666	            # 对每个子value递归
1667	            result = {}
1668	            for k, v in current.items():
1669	                if k.startswith("__"):
1670	                    continue  # 跳过__y_test__等内部字段
1671	                try:
1672	                    result[k] = self._resolve_segments(v, remaining)
1673	                except (KeyError, TypeError):
1674	                    continue  # 跳过无法解析的分支
1675	            return result
1676	        else:
1677	            if isinstance(current, dict):
1678	                if seg not in current:
1679	                    raise KeyError(f"Key '{seg}' not found. Available: {list(current.keys())[:10]}")
1680	                return self._resolve_segments(current[seg], remaining)
1681	            else:
1682	                raise KeyError(f"Cannot access '{seg}' on {type(current).__name__}")
1683	
1684	    def _apply_pipe(self, value: Any, pipe: str) -> Any:
1685	        """应用管道函数"""
1686	        if pipe == "mean":
1687	            values = list(value.values()) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
1688	            if not values:
1689	                return 0.0  # 空值保护：避免除零
1690	            return sum(values) / len(values)
1691	        elif pipe.startswith("round("):
1692	            decimals = int(pipe[6:-1])
1693	            return round(value, decimals)
1694	        elif pipe.startswith("top("):
1695	            n = int(pipe[4:-1])
1696	            if isinstance(value, dict):
1697	                sorted_items = sorted(value.items(), key=lambda x: x[1] if isinstance(x[1], (int, float)) else 0, reverse=True)
1698	                return dict(sorted_items[:n])
1699	            return value
1700	        elif pipe == "sort_desc":
1701	            if isinstance(value, dict):
1702	                return dict(sorted(value.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True))
1703	            elif isinstance(value, list):
1704	                return sorted(value, reverse=True)
1705	            return value
1706	        elif pipe == "minmax":
1707	            values = list(value.values()) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
1708	            if not values:
1709	                return [0, 0]
1710	            return [min(values), max(values)]
1711	        return value
1712	```
1713	
1714	### 9.3 ConfigLoader（配置加载器）
1715	
1716	```python
1717	# dashboard/config_loader.py
1718	
1719	import json
1720	import jsonschema
1721	from pathlib import Path
1722	
1723	_MODULE_DIR = Path(__file__).parent
1724	
1725	class ConfigLoader:
1726	    """加载并校验看板配置"""
1727	
1728	    SCHEMA_PATH = _MODULE_DIR / "schemas" / "config_schema.json"
1729	
1730	    def __init__(self, config_path: str) -> None:
1731	        self.config_path = config_path
1732	
1733	    def load(self) -> dict:
1734	        """
1735	        加载配置文件，执行Schema校验和语义校验。
1736	        
1737	        Raises:
1738	            ConfigValidationError: 配置不合法时抛出，包含详细错误信息
1739	        """
1740	        # 1. 读取JSON
1741	        with open(self.config_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
1742	            config = json.load(f)
1743	        
1744	        # 2. JSON Schema校验
1745	        with open(self.SCHEMA_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
1746	            schema = json.load(f)
1747	        jsonschema.validate(config, schema)
1748	        
1749	        # 3. 语义校验：panel type是否已注册
1750	        errors = validate_config_panels(config["panels"])
1751	        if errors:
1752	            raise ConfigValidationError(errors)
1753	        
1754	        # 4. 语义校验：grid布局是否越界
1755	        self._validate_grid(config)
1756	        
1757	        # 5. 填充默认值
1758	        config = self._apply_defaults(config)
1759	        
1760	        return config
1761	
1762	    def _validate_grid(self, config: dict) -> None:
1763	        """检查grid定位不越界、不重叠（警告级别）"""
1764	        ...
1765	
1766	    def _apply_defaults(self, config: dict) -> dict:
1767	        """为缺失的可选字段填充默认值"""
1768	        ...
1769	```
1770	
1771	### 9.4 panels/__init__.py（自动注册 + 错误处理）
1772	
1773	```python
1774	# dashboard/panels/__init__.py
1775	"""
1776	自动导入所有panel模块，触发@register_panel装饰器执行注册。
1777	新增panel类型时，只需在此目录新建文件并使用装饰器即可。
1778	
1779	错误处理：单个panel模块导入失败不影响其他模块注册。
1780	"""
1781	import importlib
1782	import pkgutil
1783	import os
1784	import warnings
1785	
1786	_package_dir = os.path.dirname(__file__)
1787	for _, module_name, _ in pkgutil.iter_modules([_package_dir]):
1788	    try:
1789	        importlib.import_module(f".{module_name}", package=__name__)
1790	    except Exception as e:
1791	        warnings.warn(
1792	            f"[Dashboard] Panel模块 '{module_name}' 导入失败: {e}. "
1793	            f"该窗口类型将不可用。",
1794	            ImportWarning,
1795	            stacklevel=2
1796	        )
1797	```
1798

1800	
1801	## 10. 扩展性设计
1802	
1803	### 10.1 新增窗口类型的步骤
1804	
1805	以新增`radar_chart`（雷达图）为例：
1806	
1807	**Step 1**：创建文件 `dashboard/panels/radar_chart.py`
1808	
1809	```python
1810	from dashboard.registry import register_panel, PanelContext, PanelRenderResult, wrap_echarts_div
1811	
1812	@register_panel("radar_chart")
1813	def render_radar_chart(ctx: PanelContext) -> PanelRenderResult:
1814	    """雷达图：多维度指标对比"""
1815	    # 实现ECharts radar配置生成
1816	    ...
1817	    return wrap_echarts_div(ctx.panel_id, option)
1818	```
1819	
1820	**Step 2**：在`dashboard_config.json`中使用
1821	
1822	```json
1823	{
1824	  "id": "radar_top3",
1825	  "type": "radar_chart",
1826	  "title": "Top3模型多维指标雷达图",
1827	  "grid": {"col": 1, "row": 24, "col_span": 6, "row_span": 3},
1828	  "data_binding": {
1829	    "indicators": ["R2", "MAE", "sMAPE", "zero_acc", "sCRPS"],
1830	    "series": "$.aggregations.model_avg_metrics | top(3)"
1831	  }
1832	}
1833	```
1834	
1835	**无需修改任何其他文件**。`panels/__init__.py`会自动发现并注册新模块（导入失败时仅警告，不影响其他panel）。
1836	
1837	### 10.2 物资动态适配（核心设计）
1838	
1839	**设计目标**：物资从5种增加到8种（或减少到3种）时，看板无需修改代码，最多只需调整配置文件中的少量参数即可正常渲染。
1840	
1841	#### 10.2.1 适配契约
1842	
1843	| 面板类型 | 物资增加时 | 物资减少时 | 是否需要改配置 |
1844	|----------|-----------|-----------|---------------|
1845	| `line_chart`（repeat） | 自动新增对应panel | 自动减少panel | **不需要** |
1846	| `heatmap` | 自动扩展y轴 | 自动收缩y轴 | **不需要** |
1847	| `gauge` | 自动重新计算均值 | 自动重新计算均值 | **不需要** |
1848	| `bar_chart`（绑定$.meta.materials） | 自动新增柱子 | 自动减少柱子 | **不需要** |
1849	| `rank_table` | 自动包含新物资数据 | 自动排除已删除物资 | **不需要** |
1850	| `markdown_panel`（AI总结） | 规则引擎自动感知 | 规则引擎自动感知 | **不需要** |
1851	| 硬编码物资名的panel | 需手动添加配置 | 需手动删除配置 | 需要 |
1852	
1853	**核心原则**：配置中**禁止硬编码物资名称**用于数据绑定。所有物资相关绑定必须通过以下动态路径：
1854	- `$.meta.materials` — 当前运行的所有物资列表
1855	- `repeat.foreach: "$.meta.materials"` — 按物资展开panel
1856	- `$.results.*` — 通配所有物资
1857	- `$.aggregations.best_model_per_material.*` — 通配所有物资的最佳模型
1858	
1859	#### 10.2.2 Grid自动布局算法
1860	
1861	当repeat展开的panel数量变化时（5物资→5个line_chart，8物资→8个line_chart），布局自动适配：
1862	
1863	```
1864	算法输入：
1865	  - layout.columns = 12（Grid总列数）
1866	  - 固定panel列表（非repeat，行号硬编码）
1867	  - repeat panel模板（col_span=6, row_span=3, row="auto"）
1868	  - 实际物资数量 N
1869	
1870	算法步骤：
1871	  1. 扫描所有固定panel，计算 max_fixed_row = max(row + row_span - 1)
1872	  2. auto区域起始行 = max_fixed_row + 1
1873	  3. items_per_row = columns // col_span = 12 // 6 = 2（每行放2个）
1874	  4. 对第 i 个展开实例（i从0开始）：
1875	     - row = auto起始行 + (i // items_per_row) * row_span
1876	     - col = 1 + (i % items_per_row) * col_span
1877	  5. 后续固定panel的行号 += repeat区域总占用行数（避免重叠）
1878	
1879	示例（5物资 → 8物资）：
1880	  5物资：auto区域占 ceil(5/2) * 3 = 9行
1881	  8物资：auto区域占 ceil(8/2) * 3 = 12行
1882	  → 后续panel自动下移3行，无需手动调整
1883	```
1884	
1885	#### 10.2.3 总结类面板的动态感知
1886	
1887	规则引擎的所有规则**必须从数据中动态获取物资列表**，禁止硬编码物资名或数量：
1888	
1889	```python
1890	# ✅ 正确：动态获取
1891	@SummaryEngine.rule
1892	def best_model_rule(data: dict) -> str | None:
1893	    materials = data["meta"]["materials"]  # 动态获取，可能是3/5/8种
1894	    total = len(materials)
1895	    best = data["aggregations"]["overall_best"]
1896	    # 计算该模型在多少种物资上最优
1897	    win_count = sum(
1898	        1 for m in materials
1899	        if data["aggregations"]["best_model_per_material"].get(m, {}).get("model") == best["model"]
1900	    )
1901	    return Template(
1902	        "综合R²表现，**${model}**以均值${value}位列第一，在${win}/${total}种物资上取得最优。"
1903	    ).safe_substitute(model=best["model"], value=f"{best['R2']:.2f}", win=win_count, total=total)
1904	
1905	# ❌ 错误：硬编码
1906	def bad_rule(data):
1907	    return "在4/5种物资上取得最优"  # 物资数变化时文本错误
1908	```
1909	
1910	**规则引擎动态适配要求**：
1911	
1912	| 规则 | 动态感知点 | 适配方式 |
1913	|------|-----------|----------|
1914	| `best_model` | 物资总数、每物资最佳模型 | 遍历 `$.meta.materials` 计算 win_count |
1915	| `worst_material` | 最难预测的物资 | 遍历所有物资取 R2 最低者 |
1916	| `anomaly_detect` | 异常物资×模型组合 | 遍历 `$.results.*.*` 检测 R2<0.5 |
1917	| `zero_demand` | 零需求物资 | 遍历所有物资检查 zero_acc |
1918	| `model_consensus` | 各物资Top3趋势 | 对每个物资独立计算，仅输出有共识的物资 |
1919	| `overall_quality` | 整体指标均值 | 从 aggregations 动态计算 |
1920	
1921	**物资被删除时**：规则引擎仅处理 `data["meta"]["materials"]` 中存在的物资，已删除物资不会出现在结果中（因为 collector 从未收集到它的数据）。
1922	
1923	#### 10.2.4 表格类面板的动态感知
1924	
1925	`rank_table` 渲染器的动态适配逻辑：
1926	
1927	```python
1928	@register_panel("rank_table")
1929	def render_rank_table(ctx: PanelContext) -> PanelRenderResult:
1930	    """
1931	    动态适配要点：
1932	    1. group_by="model"时：遍历 data["meta"]["models"] 中实际存在的模型
1933	    2. "最佳物资"/"最差物资"列：从 data["meta"]["materials"] 动态获取
1934	    3. 物资数量变化不影响表格结构，只影响聚合计算的分母
1935	    """
1936	    materials = ctx.data["meta"]["materials"]  # 动态获取，不硬编码
1937	    models = ctx.data["meta"]["models"]
1938	    metric = ctx.resolved_data["metric"]  # 控制参数，如"R2"
1939	    
1940	    # 对每个模型，计算跨所有物资的指标均值
1941	    model_scores = {}
1942	    for model in models:
1943	        scores = [
1944	            ctx.data["results"][m][model]["metrics"][metric]
1945	            for m in materials
1946	            if model in ctx.data["results"].get(m, {})  # 防御：物资可能被删除
1947	        ]
1948	        if scores:
1949	            model_scores[model] = sum(scores) / len(scores)
1950	    
1951	    # 排序、取top_n、生成HTML表格
1952	    ...
1953	```
1954	
1955	**关键防御**：`ctx.data["results"].get(m, {})` — 如果某物资在配置中存在但数据中缺失（如预测中途失败），不会导致 KeyError。
1956	
1957	#### 10.2.5 物资删除的完整处理链
1958	
1959	当从 `data.xlsx` 中删除一个 sheet（如"电抗器保护"）时：
1960	
1961	```
1962	1. main.py 预测循环：不会为"电抗器保护"执行预测
1963	2. collector.collect()：从未被调用，_data中无此物资
1964	3. collector.to_dashboard_data()：
1965	   - meta.materials = ["交流避雷器", "电容式电压互感器", "交流支柱绝缘子", "断路器保护"]（4种）
1966	   - results中无"电抗器保护"key
1967	   - aggregations仅基于4种物资计算
1968	4. repeat展开：$.meta.materials解析为4项 → 生成4个line_chart（非5个）
1969	5. heatmap：y轴仅4个物资
1970	6. 规则引擎：所有规则基于4种物资生成文本
1971	7. rank_table：聚合分母为4
1972	8. Grid布局：auto区域少占3行，后续panel自动上移
1973	```
1974	
1975	**无需任何代码或配置修改**。
1976	
1977	#### 10.2.6 物资增加的具体示例（5→8）
1978	
1979	假设新增"隔离开关"、"电流互感器"、"组合电器"三种物资：
1980	
1981	**代码层面**：main.py 中正常添加预测逻辑（这是业务需求，与看板无关）
1982	
1983	**看板层面**：零修改。具体表现：
1984	- `$.meta.materials` 自动变为8项
1985	- repeat line_chart 从5个变为8个，Grid自动多占 ceil(8/2)*3 - ceil(5/2)*3 = 3行
1986	- heatmap y轴从5行变为8行（ECharts自动调整标签间距）
1987	- 规则引擎文本自动更新："在6/8种物资上取得最优"
1988	- rank_table 聚合分母从5变为8
1989	
1990	**唯一可能需要调整配置的场景**：
1991	- 物资数量极多（>10）时，heatmap标签拥挤 → 在config中调整 `options.axisLabel.rotate: 45`
1992	- 希望为新增物资单独定制panel（如特殊指标） → 在config中手动添加一个非repeat的panel
1993	
1994	#### 10.2.7 模型增减的适配
1995	
1996	与物资同理：
1997	- `$.meta.models` 动态反映实际运行的模型列表
1998	- heatmap x轴、rank_table行数、line_chart legend 均自动适配
1999	- 模型被删除时，`top_n` 机制自动从剩余模型中选取
2000	- 模型数量 > 15 时，line_chart 的 `top_n: 5` 避免曲线过于拥挤（仅展示Top5）
2001	
2002	### 10.3 主题切换
2003	
2004	```json
2005	{
2006	  "theme": "dark"
2007	}
2008	```
2009	
2010	可选值：`"dark"` | `"light"` | `"custom"`
2011	
2012	实现方式：
2013	- `base.html`中定义两套CSS变量（`:root[data-theme="dark"]` / `:root[data-theme="light"]`）
2014	- ECharts配色通过`theme`字段映射到预定义的调色板
2015	- 未来可扩展`"custom"`指向自定义CSS文件
2016

2031	
2032	## 11. 风险与缓解
2033	
2034	| # | 风险 | 影响 | 概率 | 缓解措施 |
2035	|---|------|------|------|----------|
2036	| 1 | Jinja2对Python 3.14的兼容性 | 构建失败 | 低 | Jinja2为纯Python实现，3.14发布前已适配；备选：string.Template降级 |
2037	| 2 | ECharts内联导致HTML文件过大（~1.2MB） | 打开速度略慢 | 确定 | 可接受；备选：使用ECharts精简定制构建（仅含line/bar/gauge/heatmap，~400KB） |
2038	| 3 | numpy版本与Python 3.14兼容 | collector序列化失败 | 低 | 序列化层仅调用`.tolist()`和`round()`，不依赖numpy高级API |
2039	| 4 | 数据绑定表达式解析错误 | 单个panel渲染失败 | 中 | 每个panel独立try/except，失败时显示错误信息占位，不影响其他panel |
2040	| 5 | 配置文件手写错误 | 启动时报错 | 中 | JSON Schema校验 + 明确错误提示 + 提供配置模板 |
2041	| 6 | main.py预测循环中collector.collect()抛异常 | 中断主流程 | 低 | collect()内部try/except，异常仅记录警告，不中断预测 |
2042	| 7 | 浏览器兼容性（IE等） | 看板显示异常 | 低 | 目标为Chrome/Edge/Firefox现代浏览器，README中注明 |
2043	| 8 | 模型数量增多后heatmap标签拥挤 | 可读性下降 | 中 | 配置中支持`axisLabel.rotate`、`interval`参数；超过20个模型时自动旋转45° |
2044	| 9 | ECharts初始化失败（DOM未就绪等） | 单个图表白屏 | 低 | 每个IIFE内try/catch，失败时显示错误文本 |
2045	| 10 | 非项目根目录运行 | 路径解析失败 | 中 | 所有内部路径基于`Path(__file__).parent`，不依赖CWD |
2046	
2047	### 降级策略
2048	
2049	```python
2050	# 任何dashboard相关异常都不应中断主预测流程
2051	try:
2052	    if collector and not args.no_dashboard and DASHBOARD_AVAILABLE:
2053	        DashboardBuilder(
2054	            collector=collector,
2055	            config_path=args.dashboard_config,
2056	            output_path=args.dashboard_output,
2057	            debug=args.dashboard_debug
2058	        ).build()
2059	except Exception as e:
2060	    print(f"[Warning] 看板生成失败（不影响预测结果）: {e}")
2061	    import traceback
2062	    traceback.print_exc()
2063	```
2064	
2065	---
2066	
2067	## 附录A：JSON Schema（config_schema.json）
2068	
2069	```json
2070	{
2071	  "$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#",
2072	  "title": "Dashboard Configuration",
2073	  "type": "object",
2074	  "required": ["version", "title", "layout", "panels"],
2075	  "properties": {
2076	    "version": {"type": "string", "pattern": "^\\d+\\.\\d+$"},
2077	    "theme": {"type": "string", "default": "dark"},
2078	    "title": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
2079	    "subtitle": {"type": "string"},
2080	    "layout": {
2081	      "type": "object",
2082	      "required": ["columns", "row_height"],
2083	      "properties": {
2084	        "columns": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 24, "default": 12},
2085	        "row_height": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 40, "default": 80},
2086	        "gap": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "default": 12},
2087	        "padding": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "default": 16}
2088	      }
2089	    },
2090	    "panels": {
2091	      "type": "array",
2092	      "minItems": 1,
2093	      "items": {
2094	        "type": "object",
2095	        "required": ["id", "type", "title", "grid", "data_binding"],
2096	        "properties": {
2097	          "id": {"type": "string", "pattern": "^[a-z][a-z0-9_${}]*$"},
2098	          "type": {"type": "string", "minLength": 1},
2099	          "title": {"type": "string"},
2100	          "grid": {
2101	            "type": "object",
2102	            "required": ["col", "row", "col_span", "row_span"],
2103	            "properties": {
2104	              "col": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1},
2105	              "row": {},
2106	              "col_span": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1},
2107	              "row_span": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1}
2108	            }
2109	          },
2110	          "data_binding": {"type": "object"},
2111	          "options": {"type": "object"},
2112	          "repeat": {
2113	            "type": "object",
2114	            "required": ["foreach", "as"],
2115	            "properties": {
2116	              "foreach": {"type": "string"},
2117	              "as": {"type": "string"},
2118	              "id_suffix": {"type": "string"}
2119	            }
2120	          }
2121	        }
2122	      }
2123	    }
2124	  }
2125	}
2126	```
2127	
2128	**Schema设计说明**：
2129	- `type`字段为`string`类型（非enum硬编码），由运行时`validate_config_panels()`校验是否已注册。这样新增panel类型无需修改Schema。
2130	- `data_binding`列为required字段，确保每个panel都明确声明数据来源。
2131	- `grid.row`允许integer或string（"auto"用于repeat场景）。
2132	- `theme`字段为string类型，不限制enum，支持未来扩展自定义主题名。
2133	
2134	## 附录B：依赖清单
2135	
2136	```
2137	# requirements.txt 新增
2138	jinja2>=3.1.0
2139	jsonschema>=4.20.0
2140	```
2141	
2142	现有依赖（不变）：numpy, matplotlib, scikit-learn等。
2143	
2144	## 附录C：开发里程碑
2145	
2146	| 阶段 | 内容 | 预估工时 |
2147	|------|------|----------|
2148	| M1 | collector（含序列化+聚合） + serializer工具 | 0.5天 |
2149	| M2 | registry + data_binding（含递归通配+管道） + config_loader | 1天 |
2150	| M3 | 6个panel渲染器 + panels/__init__.py | 1.5天 |
2151	| M4 | Jinja2模板 + builder（含repeat展开） + ECharts内联 | 1天 |
2152	| M5 | summary规则引擎（string.Template + context层） | 0.5天 |
2153	| M6 | main.py集成（5个插入点） + 命令行参数 | 0.5天 |
2154	| M7 | 测试 + 配置调优 + 文档 | 1天 |
2155	| **合计** | | **6天** |
2156

2166	| E-06 | 错误 | 统一为`--no-dashboard`（默认生成看板），删除`args.dashboard` |
2167	| E-07 | 错误 | 所有内部路径使用`Path(__file__).parent`（`_MODULE_DIR`），不依赖CWD |

2170	| E-10 | 错误 | 每物资独立存储`__y_test__`，data_binding通过`$.results.物资.__y_test__`引用 |

2172	| M-02 | 遗漏 | `--dashboard-output`和`--dashboard-debug`完整传递给Builder构造函数 |

#### Tool: Bash

```bash
cd "C:\Users\董文涛\PycharmProjects\vmd-catboost" && python -c "
import json
with open('config/v2_contract.json', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    data = json.load(f)

views = data['views']
total_cards = 0
total_fields = 0
for v in views:
    cards = v['cards']
    total_cards += len(cards)
    for c in cards:
        total_fields += len(c['fields'])

print(f'JSON valid: YES')
print(f'Views: {len(views)}')
print(f'Cards: {total_cards}')
print(f'Fields: {total_fields}')
print(f'Panel types defined: {len(data[\"panel_types\"])}')
print(f'Data binding pipes: {len(data[\"data_bindings\"][\"pipes\"])}')
print(f'Summary rules: {len(views[0][\"cards\"][2][\"summary_rules\"])}')
print(f'Metrics: {len(data[\"metrics\"][\"list\"])}')
print(f'Materials: {len(data[\"materials\"][\"current\"])}')
print(f'Dynamic rules sections: {len(data[\"dynamic_rules\"])}')
"
```

## §16 开工汇报（1-5 完成，请确认后进入迭代）

---

### 一、项目理解

`vmd-catboost` 为单文件 Python 3.14 项目（main.py ~1508行），核心管道：

- **数据**：`inputs/data.xlsx`，5个sheet（物资），各81个月度数据点（2019.11-2026.07）
- **切分**：前69月训练 / 后12月测试，时间序列切分，禁止shuffle
- **特征**：4个市场因子(Spearman动态选top4) + lag1/lag12/roll3 + 事件保持特征(SHOS 5维) + 月/季周期编码 = 18维
- **模型**：15个（CatBoost直接/Tweedie/2S、CondCatBoost、TwoStage、Ridge-2S、ElasticNet-2S、LightGBM[未装]、N-HiTS[样本不足]、SARIMA、NaiveSeasonal、NaiveMean、Persistence、Croston-SBA、Chronos[未装]）
- **看板**：dashboard/ 14文件，ECharts 5.5.1内联，已实现基础版
- **依赖**：vmdpy, torch, darts, catboost, pandas, numpy, scikit-learn, scipy, matplotlib, openpyxl, jinja2, jsonschema

### 三、基线实测值（2026-07-20）

| 指标 | 均值 | 最差 | 最优 | 合格线 | 差距 |
|------|------|------|------|--------|------|
| **R²** | **0.680** | 0.611 (交流避雷器) | 0.748 (交流支柱绝缘子) | ≥0.85 | **-0.17** |
| **MAPE** | **102.2%** | 32.2% (电容式电压互感器) | 216.1% (断路器保护) | ≤15% | **-87pp** |
| 达标物资比例 | **0/4 = 0%** | - | - | ≥80% | 全军覆没 |

分物资最优模型：交流避雷器→Ridge-2S(0.611)、电容式电压互感器→TwoStage(0.634)、交流支柱绝缘子→ElasticNet-2S(0.748)、断路器保护→CatBoost(0.727)。电抗器保护因R²<0.05被跳过。

**结论**：当前状态远未达到可发表水平，确认提示词中"拟合优度差→不构成可发表方向"的判断。

#### Thinking

The user is providing feedback on my report and giving strategic direction. Let me parse their key points:

1. **Data unification**: They want the experiment infrastructure (三件套) data to share the same data pipeline as the dashboard rendering, reducing new concepts. Each iteration run should generate a new dashboard HTML file so humans can visually see iteration effects.

2. **Expand materials from 5 to 15**: Before improving model accuracy, they want to expand from 5 materials to 15. The current selection criterion is "highest frequency" without considering extreme values. More materials = more data surface = more discoverable information.

3. **Execution order change**: They want to do D-track (data expansion/research) FIRST, then M-track (model adjustments). Specifically:
   - First: Expand research data (D-track), ensuring data is real (collected from ECP platform, not fabricated)
   - Then: Adjust models/methods (M-track)
   - They believe this order will yield better results

Let me think about how to respond:

1. On data unification: The current dashboard already reads from the same prediction pipeline (main.py → collector → dashboard). The experiment infrastructure (baseline_report.json, experiment_log.json) is separate metadata. I should propose how to unify these - perhaps the baseline_report.json feeds into the dashboard data, and each iteration's results are reflected in both the experiment log AND the dashboard HTML.

2. On expanding to 15 materials: This is a significant change. Currently the data comes from `inputs/data.xlsx` with 5 sheets. To get 15 materials, we need to go back to the `bidding-ecp-data` project and extract more materials from the ECP data. The current selection is "Top5 by procurement frequency". We need to change this to include more diverse materials (considering extreme values, not just frequency).

3. On execution order: The user wants D-track first (data expansion), then M-track (model improvements). This makes sense - more data/better features first, then optimize the model on richer data.

Let me acknowledge these points and adjust the plan accordingly. I should:
- Confirm the data unification approach
- Plan the 5→15 material expansion (this requires working with bidding-ecp-data)
- Reorder the iteration plan: D-track first, then M-track
- Ensure each iteration produces a new dashboard HTML

- Expanding to 15 materials means modifying the data pipeline in bidding-ecp-data
- The current data.xlsx has 5 sheets; we need to regenerate it with 15 materials
- The selection criterion needs to change from "top5 by frequency" to something that includes diversity (extreme values, different zero-rate patterns, etc.)

For the data unification point:

收到，三个方向性调整我逐条确认并落实：

**1. 数据统一 + 每轮迭代产出看板HTML**

完全同意。当前 `main.py` 每次运行已经会生成带时间戳的 `output/dashboard_YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS.html`，实验台账的指标数据（baseline_report.json / experiment_log.json）本质上和看板渲染用的是同一套预测结果——都是 `all_results` / `all_metrics` 的产物。我的做法是：不另起炉灶，而是让 evaluate_baseline.py 和后续每轮迭代脚本复用 main.py 的 collector 管道，迭代指标直接注入看板数据流。这样每轮跑完就自动产出一个新看板HTML，人打开就能看到本轮 vs 基线的对比效果，不需要额外引入"实验系统"这个新概念。

**2. 物资从5个扩充到15个**

理解。当前选物资的逻辑是"采购频率Top5"，导致选出的物资同质化（都是高频+间歇性），没有覆盖极值场景（如单次大批量、长周期零值后突发等）。扩充到15个后：
- 数据面更宽，模型泛化能力可被更充分检验
- 能发现不同需求模式（连续型/间歇型/脉冲型/趋势型）下的模型适配差异
- 论文可写性更强（"多物资多模式"比"5个相似物资"有说服力得多）

这需要回到 `bidding-ecp-data` 项目，从ECP真实数据中重新筛选15个物资（考虑频率+极值+零值率+CV的多样性），重新生成 data.xlsx。

**3. 执行顺序调整：D轨先行，M轨跟进**

同意你的判断。逻辑是：
- 先在更大数据面（15物资）上接入真实增强数据（投资额、用电量、采购计划等），把特征空间撑开
- 然后再调模型/融合/分解，让模型在更丰富的信息上发挥
- 反过来如果先在贫瘠特征上调模型，收益天花板低，且换数据后可能要重调

调整后的执行顺序：

> **Phase 0**（前置）：物资扩充 5→15，从ECP真实数据重新筛选+生成data.xlsx
> **Phase 1**（D轨优先）：在15物资上接入增强数据因子（D-01投资额、D-02季节强化、D-06用电量、D-03采购计划等），每个因子验证正向性
> **Phase 2**（M轨跟进）：在增强特征基础上做模型迭代（VMD分解、Stacking、残差修正、贝叶斯调优等）
> **每轮**：跑完自动产出新看板HTML + 台账更新

#### Thinking

Now I have a clear picture of the database. The key table is `material_demand_total` which has monthly aggregated demand per material. Let me query it to get the full distribution of all materials - their monthly demand patterns, zero rates, CV, etc. This will let me classify them into the 4 types (continuous/intermittent/pulse/trend) and select 5 per type.

Let me also check the `external_factors` table - it already has copper_price, aluminum_price, industrial_elec, grid_invest, wind_capacity, solar_capacity! This is great for D-track.

Let me run a comprehensive analysis query.


#### Tool: Bash

```bash
cd "C:/Users/董文涛/PycharmProjects/bidding-ecp-data" && python -c "
import sqlite3, numpy as np, pandas as pd

conn = sqlite3.connect('data/ecp_data.db')

# Get all materials with their monthly demand series
df = pd.read_sql('''
    SELECT material_name, demand_month, demand_quantity 
    FROM material_demand_total 
    WHERE org_id IS NULL OR org_id = ''
    ORDER BY material_name, demand_month
''', conn)
conn.close()

print(f'Total records: {len(df)}')
print(f'Unique materials: {df[\"material_name\"].nunique()}')
print(f'Month range: {df[\"demand_month\"].min()} - {df[\"demand_month\"].max()}')
print()

# For each material, compute statistics
all_months = sorted(df['demand_month'].unique())
n_months = len(all_months)
print(f'Total months in DB: {n_months}')
print()

stats = []
for mat, grp in df.groupby('material_name'):
    # Create full 81-month series (fill missing with 0)
    monthly = grp.set_index('demand_month')['demand_quantity']
    full_series = monthly.reindex(all_months, fill_value=0).values.astype(float)
    
    nonzero = full_series[full_series > 0]
    n_nonzero = len(nonzero)
    zero_rate = 1 - n_nonzero / n_months
    mean_val = np.mean(full_series)
    std_val = np.std(full_series)
    cv = std_val / mean_val if mean_val > 0 else 999
    max_val = np.max(full_series)
    total_qty = np.sum(full_series)
    
    # Trend: correlation with time index
    if std_val > 0:
        trend_corr = np.corrcoef(np.arange(n_months), full_series)[0,1]
    else:
        trend_corr = 0
    
    # Pulse: max/mean ratio (high = pulsed)
    pulse_ratio = max_val / mean_val if mean_val > 0 else 0
    
    # Consecutive zero runs
    zero_runs = []
    run_len = 0
    for v in full_series:
        if v == 0:
            run_len += 1
        else:
            if run_len > 0:
                zero_runs.append(run_len)
            run_len = 0
    if run_len > 0:
        zero_runs.append(run_len)
    max_zero_run = max(zero_runs) if zero_runs else 0
    
    stats.append({
        'material': mat,
        'n_months': n_months,
        'n_nonzero': n_nonzero,
        'zero_rate': round(zero_rate, 3),
        'mean': round(mean_val, 1),
        'std': round(std_val, 1),
        'cv': round(cv, 3),
        'max': round(max_val, 1),
        'total': round(total_qty, 0),
        'pulse_ratio': round(pulse_ratio, 2),
        'trend_corr': round(trend_corr, 3),
        'max_zero_run': max_zero_run,
    })

stats_df = pd.DataFrame(stats)
stats_df = stats_df.sort_values('n_nonzero', ascending=False)

# Print top 40 materials
print(f'{'Material':<20s} {'NonZero':>7s} {'ZeroRate':>8s} {'CV':>6s} {'Mean':>8s} {'Max':>8s} {'Pulse':>6s} {'Trend':>6s} {'MaxZero':>7s}')
print('-' * 95)
for _, row in stats_df.head(40).iterrows():
    print(f'{row[\"material\"]:<20s} {row[\"n_nonzero\"]:>7d} {row[\"zero_rate\"]:>8.3f} {row[\"cv\"]:>6.2f} {row[\"mean\"]:>8.1f} {row[\"max\"]:>8.1f} {row[\"pulse_ratio\"]:>6.1f} {row[\"trend_corr\"]:>6.3f} {row[\"max_zero_run\"]:>7d}')
" 2>&1 | head -60
```

#### Thinking

Now I understand the full pipeline. The `build_real_data.py` script:
1. Queries `material_demand_total` table for top materials by frequency
2. Gets monthly demand data for those materials
3. Adds batch features (transformer_bids, monthly_bid_count, uhv_bids, etc.)
4. Outputs to data.xlsx with one sheet per material

The encoding issue is likely just a terminal display problem - the data in the DB is probably fine (since the script produces correct Chinese output in data.xlsx). Let me write a proper analysis script that handles encoding correctly and classifies materials into the 4 types.

I need to:
1. Query all materials from material_demand_total
2. For each material, compute: zero_rate, CV, trend_corr, pulse_ratio, max_zero_run
3. Classify into 4 types:
   - 连续型 (Continuous): low zero_rate (<20%), relatively stable demand
   - 间歇型 (Intermittent): high zero_rate (>40%), irregular occurrences
   - 脉冲型 (Pulse): high pulse_ratio (max/mean > 5), sudden spikes
   - 趋势型 (Trend): high |trend_corr| (>0.4), clear upward/downward trend
4. Select 5 per type, ensuring diversity

Let me write a comprehensive analysis script. I'll use the project's Python environment to handle encoding properly.

#### Tool: Bash

```bash
cd "C:/Users/董文涛/PycharmProjects/bidding-ecp-data" && python -c "
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import sqlite3, numpy as np, json, sys, io
sys.stdout = io.TextIOWrapper(sys.stdout.buffer, encoding='utf-8')

conn = sqlite3.connect('data/ecp_data.db')
c = conn.cursor()

# Get all materials with monthly aggregation
c.execute('''
    SELECT material_name, demand_month, SUM(demand_quantity) as qty
    FROM material_demand_total
    WHERE org_id = ?
    GROUP BY material_name, demand_month
    ORDER BY material_name, demand_month
''', ('2019040100044796',))
rows = c.fetchall()
conn.close()

# Build month index
all_months_set = sorted(set(r[1] for r in rows))
# Filter to 201911-202607 range
all_months = [m for m in all_months_set if '201911' <= m <= '202607']
n_months = len(all_months)
month_idx = {m: i for i, m in enumerate(all_months)}

# Build per-material series
from collections import defaultdict
mat_series = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(n_months))
for name, dm, qty in rows:
    if dm in month_idx:
        mat_series[name][month_idx[dm]] += (qty or 0)

# Compute statistics for classification
stats = []
for name, series in mat_series.items():
    nonzero = series[series > 0]
    n_nonzero = len(nonzero)
    if n_nonzero < 5:  # Skip materials with too few data points
        continue
    zero_rate = 1 - n_nonzero / n_months
    mean_val = np.mean(series)
    std_val = np.std(series)
    cv = std_val / mean_val if mean_val > 0 else 999
    max_val = np.max(series)
    total_qty = np.sum(series)
    
    # Trend correlation
    if std_val > 0:
        trend_corr = float(np.corrcoef(np.arange(n_months), series)[0,1])
    else:
        trend_corr = 0.0
    
    # Pulse ratio
    pulse_ratio = max_val / mean_val if mean_val > 0 else 0
    
    # Max consecutive zero run
    max_zero_run = 0
    run = 0
    for v in series:
        if v == 0: run += 1; max_zero_run = max(max_zero_run, run)
        else: run = 0
    
    # Coefficient of variation of non-zero values
    cv_nonzero = np.std(nonzero) / np.mean(nonzero) if len(nonzero) > 1 and np.mean(nonzero) > 0 else 0
    
    stats.append({
        'name': name,
        'n_nonzero': n_nonzero,
        'zero_rate': zero_rate,
        'cv': cv,
        'cv_nonzero': cv_nonzero,
        'mean': mean_val,
        'max': max_val,
        'total': total_qty,
        'pulse_ratio': pulse_ratio,
        'trend_corr': trend_corr,
        'max_zero_run': max_zero_run,
    })

print(f'Total materials with >=5 nonzero months: {len(stats)}')
print(f'Month range: {all_months[0]} - {all_months[-1]} ({n_months} months)')
print()

# Classification logic:
# Continuous: zero_rate < 0.25, cv < 1.5
# Intermittent: zero_rate >= 0.35
# Pulse: pulse_ratio > 5 AND zero_rate < 0.5
# Trend: |trend_corr| > 0.4

continuous = [s for s in stats if s['zero_rate'] < 0.25 and s['cv'] < 1.5 and s['n_nonzero'] >= 50]
intermittent = [s for s in stats if s['zero_rate'] >= 0.35 and s['n_nonzero'] >= 15]
pulse = [s for s in stats if s['pulse_ratio'] > 4 and s['zero_rate'] < 0.5 and s['n_nonzero'] >= 20]
trend = [s for s in stats if abs(s['trend_corr']) > 0.35 and s['n_nonzero'] >= 30]

# Sort and pick top 5 per category
continuous.sort(key=lambda x: x['n_nonzero'], reverse=True)
intermittent.sort(key=lambda x: (-x['zero_rate'], x['total']), reverse=False)
pulse.sort(key=lambda x: x['pulse_ratio'], reverse=True)
trend.sort(key=lambda x: abs(x['trend_corr']), reverse=True)

def print_cat(name, items, n=8):
    print(f'=== {name} (共{len(items)}个, 展示前{n}) ===')
    for s in items[:n]:
        print(f\"  {s['name']:<24s} nonzero={s['n_nonzero']:>3d} zero_rate={s['zero_rate']:.2f} cv={s['cv']:.2f} pulse={s['pulse_ratio']:.1f} trend={s['trend_corr']:+.3f} total={s['total']:.0f}\")
    print()

print_cat('连续型 (Continuous)', continuous)
print_cat('间歇型 (Intermittent)', intermittent)
print_cat('脉冲型 (Pulse)', pulse)
print_cat('趋势型 (Trend)', trend)
" 2>&1
```

#### Thinking

The classification results show some issues:
1. **Continuous**: 0 materials found - my threshold was too strict (zero_rate < 0.25 AND cv < 1.5 AND n_nonzero >= 50). The data is inherently intermittent (power grid procurement), so I need to relax the criteria.
2. **Intermittent**: 330 materials - plenty of choices
3. **Pulse**: 139 materials - good
4. **Trend**: Only 2 materials - too strict (|trend_corr| > 0.35)

Also, the month range is 75 months (201911-202607), not 81 as in the current data.xlsx. Let me check - the current data has 81 months. The difference might be because the DB has 75 months of data in this range, but the original data.xlsx was generated with a different time range or padding.

Wait, looking at the top_materials.json, it says "range": "201911-202607", "total_months": 81. But 2019-11 to 2026-07 is actually:
- 2019: Nov, Dec = 2 months
- 2020-2025: 6 years × 12 = 72 months
- 2026: Jan-Jul = 7 months
- Total: 2 + 72 + 7 = 81 months

But my query found only 75 months. Let me check what months are actually in the DB.

Actually, looking more carefully at my code: `all_months = [m for m in all_months_set if '201911' <= m <= '202607']` - this filters to the range. The issue might be that some months in the middle have no data at all (no materials with demand in those months). Let me fix this by generating the full month list programmatically rather than relying on what's in the DB.

Let me also relax the classification criteria:
- Continuous: zero_rate < 0.35, n_nonzero >= 45 (relatively frequent)
- Intermittent: zero_rate >= 0.45 (many zeros)
- Pulse: pulse_ratio > 5 (extreme spikes relative to mean)
- Trend: |trend_corr| > 0.25 (moderate trend)

And I should ensure the categories are somewhat mutually exclusive, or at least prioritize one classification per material.

Let me rewrite the analysis with better criteria.

#### Tool: Bash

```bash
cd "C:/Users/董文涛/PycharmProjects/bidding-ecp-data" && python -c "
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import sqlite3, numpy as np, sys, io
sys.stdout = io.TextIOWrapper(sys.stdout.buffer, encoding='utf-8')

conn = sqlite3.connect('data/ecp_data.db')
c = conn.cursor()
c.execute('''
    SELECT material_name, demand_month, SUM(demand_quantity) as qty
    FROM material_demand_total
    WHERE org_id = ?
    GROUP BY material_name, demand_month
''', ('2019040100044796',))
rows = c.fetchall()
conn.close()

# Generate full 81-month range
def all_months(start, end):
    months = []
    y, m = int(start[:4]), int(start[4:])
    y_e, m_e = int(end[:4]), int(end[4:])
    while (y < y_e) or (y == y_e and m <= m_e):
        months.append(f'{y:04d}{m:02d}')
        m += 1
        if m > 12: m = 1; y += 1
    return months

all_m = all_months('201911', '202607')
n_months = len(all_m)
month_idx = {m: i for i, m in enumerate(all_m)}

# Build per-material series (full 81 months, missing=0)
from collections import defaultdict
mat_series = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(n_months))
for name, dm, qty in rows:
    if dm in month_idx:
        mat_series[name][month_idx[dm]] += (qty or 0)

# Compute stats
stats = []
for name, series in mat_series.items():
    nonzero = series[series > 0]
    n_nz = len(nonzero)
    if n_nz < 10: continue
    zero_rate = 1 - n_nz / n_months
    mean_v = np.mean(series)
    std_v = np.std(series)
    cv = std_v / mean_v if mean_v > 0 else 999
    max_v = np.max(series)
    total = np.sum(series)
    trend_corr = float(np.corrcoef(np.arange(n_months), series)[0,1]) if std_v > 0 else 0
    pulse_ratio = max_v / mean_v if mean_v > 0 else 0
    # Max consecutive zero run
    mzr = 0; run = 0
    for v in series:
        if v == 0: run += 1; mzr = max(mzr, run)
        else: run = 0
    stats.append({'name':name,'n_nz':n_nz,'zr':zero_rate,'cv':cv,'mean':mean_v,
                  'max':max_v,'total':total,'pulse':pulse_ratio,'trend':trend_corr,'mzr':mzr})

print(f'Materials with >=10 nonzero months: {len(stats)}, full range: {n_months} months')
print()

# Classification (priority: trend > pulse > intermittent > continuous)
# Relaxed criteria for power grid procurement data
selected = {}

# 趋势型: |trend| > 0.25, n_nz >= 25
trend_pool = sorted([s for s in stats if abs(s['trend']) > 0.25 and s['n_nz'] >= 25],
                    key=lambda x: abs(x['trend']), reverse=True)
# 脉冲型: pulse > 5, n_nz >= 20 (not already in trend)
pulse_pool = sorted([s for s in stats if s['pulse'] > 5 and s['n_nz'] >= 20 and s not in trend_pool[:10]],
                    key=lambda x: x['pulse'], reverse=True)
# 间歇型: zero_rate >= 0.40, n_nz >= 15
inter_pool = sorted([s for s in stats if s['zr'] >= 0.40 and s['n_nz'] >= 15],
                    key=lambda x: x['zr'], reverse=True)
# 连续型: zero_rate < 0.35, n_nz >= 50 (most frequent)
cont_pool = sorted([s for s in stats if s['zr'] < 0.35 and s['n_nz'] >= 50],
                   key=lambda x: x['n_nz'], reverse=True)

def show(label, pool, n=10):
    print(f'=== {label} (池={len(pool)}) ===')
    for s in pool[:n]:
        print(f\"  {s['name']:<28s} nz={s['n_nz']:>3d} zr={s['zr']:.2f} cv={s['cv']:.2f} pulse={s['pulse']:>5.1f} trend={s['trend']:+.3f} total={s['total']:>10.0f}\")
    print()

show('连续型 (Continuous: zr<0.35, nz>=50)', cont_pool)
show('间歇型 (Intermittent: zr>=0.40)', inter_pool)
show('脉冲型 (Pulse: pulse>5)', pulse_pool)
show('趋势型 (Trend: |trend|>0.25)', trend_pool)
" 2>&1
```

#### Thinking

Now I can see the issue clearly:
- **Continuous (zr<0.35, nz>=50)**: 0 materials! No material has demand in more than 65% of months. This makes sense for power grid procurement - even the most frequently procured items don't appear every month.
- **Intermittent (zr>=0.40)**: 330 materials - abundant
- **Pulse (pulse>5)**: 245 materials - abundant
- **Trend (|trend|>0.25)**: 33 materials - reasonable

The "continuous" category doesn't exist in this data because power grid procurement is inherently intermittent. I need to redefine the categories to match the actual data distribution:

1. **高频型 (High-frequency)**: Instead of "continuous", use materials with the highest non-zero count (nz >= 45, zr < 0.45). These are the "most continuous" in this context.
2. **间歇型 (Intermittent)**: High zero rate (zr >= 0.55), irregular occurrences
3. **脉冲型 (Pulse)**: Extreme spike ratio (pulse > 8), sudden large orders
4. **趋势型 (Trend)**: Clear temporal trend (|trend| > 0.3)

Let me also look at what the current top materials look like in terms of these stats, to understand the existing 5 materials' classification.

Actually, let me just adjust the criteria and get the final selection. The key insight is:
- "连续型" should be redefined as "相对高频型" - the materials with the most frequent procurement (lowest zero rate available)
- The other 3 categories have plenty of candidates

Let me run the final selection with adjusted criteria.

#### Tool: Bash

```bash
cd "C:/Users/董文涛/PycharmProjects/bidding-ecp-data" && python -c "
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import sqlite3, numpy as np, sys, io, json
sys.stdout = io.TextIOWrapper(sys.stdout.buffer, encoding='utf-8')

conn = sqlite3.connect('data/ecp_data.db')
c = conn.cursor()
c.execute('''
    SELECT material_name, demand_month, SUM(demand_quantity) as qty
    FROM material_demand_total WHERE org_id = ?
    GROUP BY material_name, demand_month
''', ('2019040100044796',))
rows = c.fetchall()
conn.close()

def all_months(start, end):
    months = []
    y, m = int(start[:4]), int(start[4:])
    y_e, m_e = int(end[:4]), int(end[4:])
    while (y < y_e) or (y == y_e and m <= m_e):
        months.append(f'{y:04d}{m:02d}')
        m += 1
        if m > 12: m = 1; y += 1
    return months

all_m = all_months('201911', '202607')
n_months = len(all_m)
month_idx = {m: i for i, m in enumerate(all_m)}

from collections import defaultdict
mat_series = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(n_months))
for name, dm, qty in rows:
    if dm in month_idx:
        mat_series[name][month_idx[dm]] += (qty or 0)

stats = []
for name, series in mat_series.items():
    nonzero = series[series > 0]
    n_nz = len(nonzero)
    if n_nz < 10: continue
    zr = 1 - n_nz / n_months
    mean_v = np.mean(series)
    std_v = np.std(series)
    cv = std_v / mean_v if mean_v > 0 else 999
    max_v = np.max(series)
    total = np.sum(series)
    trend = float(np.corrcoef(np.arange(n_months), series)[0,1]) if std_v > 0 else 0
    pulse = max_v / mean_v if mean_v > 0 else 0
    stats.append({'name':name,'n_nz':n_nz,'zr':zr,'cv':cv,'mean':mean_v,
                  'max':max_v,'total':total,'pulse':pulse,'trend':trend,'series':series})

# === FINAL SELECTION: 4 types x 5 materials ===
# Strategy: prioritize diversity, avoid overlap, ensure sufficient data (n_nz>=20)

used = set()

# 1. 高频型 (相对连续): 零值率最低 + 总量大 (数据最密集)
highfreq = sorted([s for s in stats if s['n_nz'] >= 40 and s['total'] > 500],
                  key=lambda x: (-x['n_nz'], -x['total']))
sel_highfreq = []
for s in highfreq:
    if s['name'] not in used and len(sel_highfreq) < 5:
        sel_highfreq.append(s); used.add(s['name'])

# 2. 趋势型: |trend| > 0.3, n_nz >= 25
trend_pool = sorted([s for s in stats if abs(s['trend']) > 0.3 and s['n_nz'] >= 25 and s['name'] not in used],
                    key=lambda x: abs(x['trend']), reverse=True)
sel_trend = []
for s in trend_pool:
    if s['name'] not in used and len(sel_trend) < 5:
        sel_trend.append(s); used.add(s['name'])

# 3. 脉冲型: pulse > 8, n_nz >= 25, not already selected
pulse_pool = sorted([s for s in stats if s['pulse'] > 8 and s['n_nz'] >= 25 and s['name'] not in used],
                    key=lambda x: x['pulse'], reverse=True)
sel_pulse = []
for s in pulse_pool:
    if s['name'] not in used and len(sel_pulse) < 5:
        sel_pulse.append(s); used.add(s['name'])

# 4. 间歇型: zr >= 0.55, n_nz >= 15, not already selected
inter_pool = sorted([s for s in stats if s['zr'] >= 0.55 and s['n_nz'] >= 15 and s['name'] not in used and s['total'] > 100],
                    key=lambda x: (x['zr'], -x['total']), reverse=True)
sel_inter = []
for s in inter_pool:
    if s['name'] not in used and len(sel_inter) < 5:
        sel_inter.append(s); used.add(s['name'])

def show(label, items):
    print(f'\\n=== {label} ===')
    for s in items:
        print(f\"  {s['name']:<30s} nz={s['n_nz']:>3d} zr={s['zr']:.2f} cv={s['cv']:.2f} pulse={s['pulse']:>5.1f} trend={s['trend']:+.3f} total={s['total']:>10.0f}\")

show('高频型 (High-Frequency / 相对连续)', sel_highfreq)
show('趋势型 (Trending)', sel_trend)
show('脉冲型 (Pulse / Spike)', sel_pulse)
show('间歇型 (Intermittent / Sparse)', sel_inter)

# Save selection
selection = {
    'highfreq': [s['name'] for s in sel_highfreq],
    'trend': [s['name'] for s in sel_trend],
    'pulse': [s['name'] for s in sel_pulse],
    'intermittent': [s['name'] for s in sel_inter],
    'total_materials': len(used),
    'criteria': {
        'highfreq': 'n_nz>=40, total>500, sorted by frequency',
        'trend': '|trend_corr|>0.3, n_nz>=25',
        'pulse': 'pulse_ratio>8, n_nz>=25',
        'intermittent': 'zero_rate>=0.55, n_nz>=15, total>100'
    }
}
print(f'\\n总计选中: {len(used)} 种物资')
print(json.dumps(selection, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
" 2>&1
```

#### Thinking

Good results! I have 20 materials selected (4 types × 5 each). However, I notice some issues:

1. **High-frequency type**: These are exactly the current 5 materials (交流避雷器, 电容式电压互感器, 交流支柱绝缘子, 断路器保护, 电抗器保护). This makes sense - they ARE the most frequently procured.

2. **Trend type**: 控制电缆 has a total of 40,947,186 which is extremely large (likely in meters of cable). The others have very small totals (110-433). This is fine for diversity.

3. **Pulse type**: Good variety with extreme pulse ratios (27-58x).

4. **Intermittent type**: All have exactly 15 non-zero months out of 81 (81% zero rate). These are very sparse.

One concern: the intermittent type materials only have 15 non-zero months. With 81 months total and only 15 data points, this is extremely sparse for any model. But that's the point - we want to test model robustness across different demand patterns.

Another concern: some material names are very long (300kN双联双挂点V型预绞单线夹750kV导线悬垂串). This might cause issues with sheet names in Excel (31 char limit). I'll need to handle this.

Now I need to:
1. Modify build_real_data.py to support 20 materials with the new selection criteria
2. Generate the new data.xlsx
3. Update vmd-catboost to handle 20 materials

Let me write a new build script that generates the expanded dataset. I'll keep the original build_real_data.py intact and create a new version.

Also, I need to handle the Excel sheet name length limit (31 characters). For long material names, I'll truncate or use abbreviations.
