选定20种物资（4类型×5）。现在生成扩充版 data.xlsx。需要注意 Excel sheet名31字符限制，长名称需截断：



#### Tool: Bash

```bash
cd "C:/Users/董文涛/PycharmProjects/bidding-ecp-data" && python -c "
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import sqlite3, numpy as np, sys, io, os, json
sys.stdout = io.TextIOWrapper(sys.stdout.buffer, encoding='utf-8')
import openpyxl

DB_PATH = 'data/ecp_data.db'
OUTPUT = r'C:\Users\董文涛\PycharmProjects\vmd-catboost\inputs\data.xlsx'
SGCC_ORG_ID = '2019040100044796'
START, END = '201911', '202607'

# 4-type x 5 selection (from analysis above)
SELECTION = {
    'highfreq': ['交流避雷器','电容式电压互感器','交流支柱绝缘子','断路器保护','电抗器保护'],
    'trend': ['控制电缆','防误闭锁系统','智能变电站防误闭锁系统','级联型静止无功发生器（SVG）','10kV三相油浸分体式并联电抗器'],
    'pulse': ['PTN电接口模块','电容器组串联电抗器','地线用盘形悬式瓷绝缘子','电容器保护','PTN光接口模块'],
    'intermittent': ['330kV油浸电磁CT','160kN单联双线夹地线悬垂串','70kN双联地线耐张串','300kN双联双挂点V型预绞单线夹750kV导线悬垂串','OPGW'],
}

# Flatten and create short names for Excel sheets (<=31 chars)
all_mats = []
short_names = {}
for cat, mats in SELECTION.items():
    for m in mats:
        all_mats.append(m)
        if len(m) <= 31:
            short_names[m] = m
        else:
            # Truncate intelligently
            short_names[m] = m[:28] + '...'

print(f'Total materials: {len(all_mats)}')
for m in all_mats:
    sn = short_names[m]
    flag = ' (truncated)' if sn != m else ''
    print(f'  {sn}{flag}')

# Generate months
def all_months(start, end):
    months = []
    y, m = int(start[:4]), int(start[4:])
    y_e, m_e = int(end[:4]), int(end[4:])
    while (y < y_e) or (y == y_e and m <= m_e):
        months.append(f'{y:04d}{m:02d}')
        m += 1
        if m > 12: m = 1; y += 1
    return months

months = all_months(START, END)
print(f'\\nMonths: {months[0]} - {months[-1]} ({len(months)} total)')

# Load data from DB
conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
c = conn.cursor()

# Load demand data
placeholders = ','.join(['?']*len(all_mats))
c.execute(f'''
    SELECT material_name, demand_month, SUM(demand_quantity) as qty, MAX(notice_count) as ncnt
    FROM material_demand_total
    WHERE org_id=? AND material_name IN ({placeholders})
    GROUP BY material_name, demand_month
''', [SGCC_ORG_ID] + all_mats)
rows = c.fetchall()

# Load batch features
c.execute('''SELECT notice_id, title, notice_publish_time
    FROM bid_notices WHERE category='material' AND doctype='doci-bid' ''')
bids = c.fetchall()
conn.close()

from collections import defaultdict
monthly_bids = defaultdict(lambda: {'transformer':0,'uhv':0,'digital':0,'total':0})
for nid, title, pt in bids:
    ym = pt[:4] + pt[5:7]
    monthly_bids[ym]['total'] += 1
    if any(k in title for k in ['输变电','变电设备']): monthly_bids[ym]['transformer'] += 1
    if '特高压' in title: monthly_bids[ym]['uhv'] += 1
    if '数字化' in title: monthly_bids[ym]['digital'] += 1

# Organize demand by material
mat_data = {m: {} for m in all_mats}
for name, dm, qty, ncnt in rows:
    if name in mat_data:
        mat_data[name][dm] = (qty or 0, ncnt or 0)

# Build Excel
wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
wb.remove(wb.active)

HEADERS = ['日期','需求量','项目数量','transformer_bids','monthly_bid_count','uhv_bids','has_batch','digital_bids']

for mat in all_mats:
    sheet_name = short_names[mat]
    ws = wb.create_sheet(sheet_name)
    for ci, h in enumerate(HEADERS, 1):
        ws.cell(row=1, column=ci, value=h)
    
    zero_months = 0
    for ri, dm in enumerate(months, 2):
        qty, ncnt = mat_data[mat].get(dm, (0, 0))
        if qty == 0: zero_months += 1
        y, mo = int(dm[:4]), int(dm[4:])
        bf = monthly_bids.get(dm, {})
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=1, value=f'{y}-{mo:02d}-01')
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=2, value=qty)
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=3, value=ncnt)
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=4, value=bf.get('transformer', 0))
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=5, value=bf.get('total', 0))
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=6, value=bf.get('uhv', 0))
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=7, value=1 if bf.get('total',0)>0 else 0)
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=8, value=bf.get('digital', 0))
    
    nz = len(months) - zero_months
    print(f'  [{sheet_name}] {len(months)}月, 非零={nz}, 零值={zero_months}')

wb.save(OUTPUT)
print(f'\\n输出: {OUTPUT}')
print(f'Sheets: {len(all_mats)}')

# Save selection metadata
meta = {
    'range': f'{START}-{END}',
    'total_months': len(months),
    'selection_criteria': {
        'highfreq': 'n_nz>=40, total>500 (相对高频/连续型)',
        'trend': '|trend_corr|>0.3, n_nz>=25 (趋势型)',
        'pulse': 'pulse_ratio>8, n_nz>=25 (脉冲型)',
        'intermittent': 'zero_rate>=0.55, n_nz>=15, total>100 (间歇型)',
    },
    'materials_by_type': SELECTION,
    'short_name_map': {k:v for k,v in short_names.items() if k!=v},
}
meta_path = r'C:\Users\董文涛\PycharmProjects\vmd-catboost\inputs\material_selection.json'
with open(meta_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    json.dump(meta, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
print(f'选择元数据: {meta_path}')
" 2>&1
```

#### Thinking

Excellent! The baseline on 20 materials is complete. Key findings:

- R² mean: 0.4886 (much lower than the 5-material baseline of 0.68, as expected with more diverse/harder materials)
- R² range: 0.0236 (电容器组串联电抗器) to 0.8931 (智能变电站防误闭锁系统)
- Only 1/20 materials meets the 0.85 threshold (智能变电站防误闭锁系统 at 0.8931)
- MAPE mean: 161.28%

Notable observations:
1. **智能变电站防误闭锁系统** (trend type) already achieves R²=0.8931 - above the 0.85 threshold!
2. **控制电缆** (trend type) achieves R²=0.776 - close to threshold
3. The high-frequency type materials perform similarly to before (0.61-0.75)
4. The intermittent type materials are very challenging (0.04-0.56)
5. Some NaN issues with OPGW (Ridge-2S and ElasticNet-2S failed)

This confirms the user's insight: expanding to more diverse materials reveals much more about model limitations. The current models are completely inadequate for pulse and intermittent types.

import sqlite3, sys, io
sys.stdout = io.TextIOWrapper(sys.stdout.buffer, encoding='utf-8')
conn = sqlite3.connect('data/ecp_data.db')
c = conn.cursor()
c.execute('SELECT * FROM external_factors ORDER BY month')
rows = c.fetchall()
c.execute('PRAGMA table_info(external_factors)')
cols = [col[1] for col in c.fetchall()]
conn.close()

print(f'Columns: {cols}')
print(f'Total rows: {len(rows)}')
print(f'Month range: {rows[0][0]} - {rows[-1][0]}')
print()
print(f'{\"month\":<8s} {\"copper\":>8s} {\"aluminum\":>8s} {\"ind_elec\":>10s} {\"ind_yoy\":>8s} {\"grid_inv\":>10s} {\"wind\":>8s} {\"solar\":>8s}')

" 2>&1

```bash

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import sqlite3, numpy as np, sys, io, os, json
sys.stdout = io.TextIOWrapper(sys.stdout.buffer, encoding='utf-8')
import openpyxl

DB_PATH = 'data/ecp_data.db'
OUTPUT = r'C:\Users\董文涛\PycharmProjects\vmd-catboost\inputs\data.xlsx'
SGCC_ORG_ID = '2019040100044796'
START, END = '201911', '202607'

SELECTION = {
    'highfreq': ['交流避雷器','电容式电压互感器','交流支柱绝缘子','断路器保护','电抗器保护'],
    'trend': ['控制电缆','防误闭锁系统','智能变电站防误闭锁系统','级联型静止无功发生器（SVG）','10kV三相油浸分体式并联电抗器'],
    'pulse': ['PTN电接口模块','电容器组串联电抗器','地线用盘形悬式瓷绝缘子','电容器保护','PTN光接口模块'],
    'intermittent': ['330kV油浸电磁CT','160kN单联双线夹地线悬垂串','70kN双联地线耐张串','300kN双联双挂点V型预绞单线夹750kV导线悬垂串','OPGW'],
}
all_mats = []
short_names = {}
for cat, mats in SELECTION.items():
    for m in mats:
        all_mats.append(m)
        short_names[m] = m if len(m) <= 31 else m[:28]+'...'

def all_months(start, end):
    months = []
    y, m = int(start[:4]), int(start[4:])
    y_e, m_e = int(end[:4]), int(end[4:])
    while (y < y_e) or (y == y_e and m <= m_e):
        months.append(f'{y:04d}{m:02d}')
        m += 1
        if m > 12: m = 1; y += 1
    return months

months = all_months(START, END)

conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
c = conn.cursor()

# Load demand data
placeholders = ','.join(['?']*len(all_mats))
c.execute(f'''
    SELECT material_name, demand_month, SUM(demand_quantity) as qty, MAX(notice_count) as ncnt
    FROM material_demand_total
    WHERE org_id=? AND material_name IN ({placeholders})
    GROUP BY material_name, demand_month
''', [SGCC_ORG_ID] + all_mats)
rows = c.fetchall()

# Load batch features
c.execute('''SELECT notice_id, title, notice_publish_time
    FROM bid_notices WHERE category='material' AND doctype='doci-bid' ''')
bids = c.fetchall()

# Load external factors
c.execute('SELECT month, aluminum_price, industrial_elec, industrial_elec_yoy FROM external_factors')
ext_rows = c.fetchall()
conn.close()

# Build external factors lookup
ext_factors = {}
for month, al_price, ind_elec, ind_yoy in ext_rows:
    ext_factors[month] = {
        'aluminum_price': al_price if al_price and al_price > 0 else None,
        'industrial_elec': ind_elec if ind_elec and ind_elec > 0 else None,
        'industrial_elec_yoy': ind_yoy if ind_yoy is not None else None,
    }

# Backfill: for months before external data starts, use first available value
first_ext_month = min(ext_factors.keys())
first_vals = ext_factors[first_ext_month]
for m in months:
    if m not in ext_factors:
        ext_factors[m] = first_vals.copy()

# Build batch features
from collections import defaultdict
monthly_bids = defaultdict(lambda: {'transformer':0,'uhv':0,'digital':0,'total':0})
for nid, title, pt in bids:
    ym = pt[:4] + pt[5:7]
    monthly_bids[ym]['total'] += 1
    if any(k in title for k in ['输变电','变电设备']): monthly_bids[ym]['transformer'] += 1
    if '特高压' in title: monthly_bids[ym]['uhv'] += 1
    if '数字化' in title: monthly_bids[ym]['digital'] += 1

# Organize demand
mat_data = {m: {} for m in all_mats}
for name, dm, qty, ncnt in rows:
    if name in mat_data:
        mat_data[name][dm] = (qty or 0, ncnt or 0)

# Build Excel with expanded columns
wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
wb.remove(wb.active)

HEADERS = ['日期','需求量','项目数量','transformer_bids','monthly_bid_count','uhv_bids',
           'has_batch','digital_bids','aluminum_price','industrial_elec','industrial_elec_yoy']

for mat in all_mats:
    sheet_name = short_names[mat]
    ws = wb.create_sheet(sheet_name)
    for ci, h in enumerate(HEADERS, 1):
        ws.cell(row=1, column=ci, value=h)
    
    for ri, dm in enumerate(months, 2):
        qty, ncnt = mat_data[mat].get(dm, (0, 0))
        y, mo = int(dm[:4]), int(dm[4:])
        bf = monthly_bids.get(dm, {})
        ef = ext_factors.get(dm, {})
        
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=1, value=f'{y}-{mo:02d}-01')
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=2, value=qty)
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=3, value=ncnt)
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=4, value=bf.get('transformer', 0))
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=5, value=bf.get('total', 0))
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=6, value=bf.get('uhv', 0))
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=7, value=1 if bf.get('total',0)>0 else 0)
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=8, value=bf.get('digital', 0))
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=9, value=ef.get('aluminum_price', 0) or 0)
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=10, value=ef.get('industrial_elec', 0) or 0)
        ws.cell(row=ri, column=11, value=ef.get('industrial_elec_yoy', 0) or 0)

wb.save(OUTPUT)
print(f'输出: {OUTPUT}')
print(f'Sheets: {len(all_mats)}, Columns: {len(HEADERS)}')
print(f'新增列: aluminum_price, industrial_elec, industrial_elec_yoy')
print(f'外部因子覆盖: {min(ext_factors.keys())} - {max(ext_factors.keys())}')
print(f'前6月(201911-202004)使用首值回填')
" 2>&1
```

#### Thinking

Let me analyze the D-01 results. Comparing with the previous baseline (without external factors):

Previous baseline (without external factors): R² mean = 0.4886
Current (with external factors): R² mean = 0.4674

The R² mean actually DECREASED slightly (-0.021). This means the external factors (aluminum_price, industrial_elec, industrial_elec_yoy) are NOT providing a positive contribution with the current top-4 Spearman selection mechanism.

Looking at individual materials:
- Most materials show no change (the Spearman selection still picks the same top-4 factors)
- 控制电缆: 0.776 → 0.746 (slightly worse)
- 10kV并联电抗器: 0.644 → 0.651 (slightly better)
- 160kN地线悬垂串: 0.368 → 0.032 (much worse! The external factors displaced better internal factors)
- 电容器组串联电抗器: 0.024 → -0.035 (worse)

The issue is clear: the Spearman top-4 selection is picking external factors for some materials where they're actually harmful. The external factors have a smooth trend (aluminum price goes up over time, industrial electricity goes up) which correlates with any trending material but doesn't help with prediction because they're essentially proxying for "time" rather than providing causal information.

This is a classic spurious correlation problem. The external factors trend upward over time, and some materials also trend upward, so Spearman correlation is high, but using them as features just adds a time proxy that doesn't generalize to the test set.

Decision for D-01: The external factors in their current form (raw levels) are NOT helpful. They need to be transformed:
1. Use YoY changes instead of levels (already have industrial_elec_yoy)
2. Use detrended/differenced versions
3. Or use them only for specific material types where they make causal sense

Per the 7-step protocol:
- Hypothesis: External factors (aluminum price, industrial electricity) will improve R²
- Evidence: Literature supports macro factors as demand drivers
- Implementation: Added to FACTOR_NAMES, Spearman top-4 selection
- Measurement: R² mean 0.4886 → 0.4674 (ΔR² = -0.021)
- Decision: **Switch off (default disabled)** - the raw external factors hurt more than help due to spurious trend correlation. Keep code, record finding.

Next step: I should try a modified approach - use DIFFERENCED external factors (month-over-month change) instead of raw levels, which removes the spurious trend correlation. Or better yet, I should change the approach: instead of adding them to the Spearman top-4 pool (which competes with existing factors), I should add them as ALWAYS-ON additional features that don't displace the proven internal factors.

Actually, let me think about this more carefully. The real issue is:
1. The top-4 mechanism means adding 3 new factors to the pool of 7 means some materials will now pick external factors INSTEAD of internal ones
2. For materials where the external factor has a spurious trend correlation, this hurts

The fix: increase top-K from 4 to 6 (use top-6 factors instead of top-4), so external factors ADD information rather than REPLACE existing good factors. Or better: keep top-4 for internal factors and add external factors as separate always-included features.

Let me try the "top-6" approach first as it's the minimal change.

Actually, looking at the code more carefully, the `get_top_factors` function selects top-4 from ALL factors in FACTOR_NAMES. If I change it to top-6, it will use 6 factors instead of 4, which means more features for the model. This might help for some materials but could also add noise for others.

A better approach: separate the factor selection into "internal" (always top-4 from the original 4) and "external" (always include all 3 external factors as additional). This way external factors supplement rather than compete.

But this requires more code changes. Let me try the simpler approach first: just increase to top-6 and see if it helps.

Actually, the simplest and most principled approach per the experiment protocol: record D-01 as "switch_off" (negative result), then try D-01b with a modified approach (differenced factors or forced inclusion).

Let me record this result and move on to the next hypothesis. The key insight is that raw level external factors with trend don't help - we need stationary transformations.

Let me record this in the experiment log and move to the next D-track hypothesis. I'll try:
- D-01b: Use differenced external factors (Δaluminum, Δindustrial_elec) instead of levels
- Or D-02: Seasonal/calendar factor enhancement (which doesn't have the trend problem)

Let me be efficient and try D-02 (seasonal enhancement) next, since it's lower risk and the current code already has month/quarter encoding that could be improved.

Actually, let me first properly record the D-01 result, then proceed.

D-01结果：R²均值从0.4886降至0.4674（ΔR²=-0.021），**外部因子原始值无正向收益**。

根因分析：铝价/工业用电量呈单调上升趋势，与趋势型物资产生伪相关，Spearman选中后实际是"时间代理"而非因果信息，在测试集上不泛化。

**决策：开关化保留（默认关）**。记录"为何无效"：原始level外部因子→伪趋势相关→替换了有效内部因子→负向。

下一步修正方向：用差分/同比变化率（已去除趋势）替代原始level，或将外部因子作为"附加特征"而非"竞争替换"。我先记录台账，然后尝试D-02（季节因子强化+间歇性编码），这个不依赖外部数据且无伪相关风险：

#### Thinking

Good, D-01 is recorded. Now let me proceed to D-02: seasonal/calendar factor enhancement + intermittent demand encoding. 

The current code already has:
- sin/cos month encoding (2 features)
- sin/cos quarter encoding (2 features)
- Event features (SHOS): gap_since_last, evt_cnt_6m, evt_cnt_12m, cumul_12m, month_freq

For D-02, I can add:
1. **Enhanced seasonal features**: 
   - Month-of-year as categorical (one-hot or target-encoded)
   - Quarter-end effect (already tried as USE_QUARTER_DUMMIES, was negative)
   - Chinese New Year effect (Jan/Feb suppression)
   - Year-end rush (Nov/Dec surge)
   
2. **Intermittent demand specific encoding**:
   - Average inter-demand interval (ADI)
   - Coefficient of variation of demand sizes (CV²)
   - These are the Syntetos-Boylan classification features for intermittent demand

3. **Lag features enhancement**:
   - More lag orders (lag2, lag3, lag6)
   - Exponential weighted moving average
   - Rolling std (volatility)

Let me implement D-02 as a modification to the preprocess_data function. I'll add these as new features controlled by a switch.

Actually, let me think about what's most likely to help:
- The current features are: 4 market factors + lag1 + lag12 + roll3 + is_zero_lag1 + is_zero_lag12 + sin/cos month + sin/cos quarter + 5 event features = 18 features
- The models are tree-based (CatBoost) which can capture non-linear interactions
- The issue is that for sparse materials (15 non-zero months), there's very little signal

For D-02, the highest-impact additions would be:
1. **Rolling statistics** (std, max, min over 6/12 months) - captures volatility regime
2. **Lag2, lag3** - more autoregressive information
3. **Year-over-year change** (demand[t] - demand[t-12]) - captures trend without external data
4. **Exponential weighted mean** - smoother than simple rolling mean

Let me implement these as a new feature block controlled by a switch (USE_ENHANCED_LAG_FEATURES or similar).

Actually, I realize I should be more strategic. The biggest gap is between high-frequency materials (R²~0.6-0.75) and the target (0.85). For these materials, the models already have decent signal. The issue might be:
1. Not enough autoregressive information (only lag1, lag12, roll3)
2. The test set is only 12 months, and with 69 training months, there's limited data

Let me add richer lag/rolling features and see if they help the high-frequency materials first, since those are closest to the target.

Let me implement this now.

560	def baseline_naive_seasonal(y_train, y_test, period=12):
561	    """季节性朴素预测: yhat_t = y_{t-period} (抄去年同期)"""
562	    preds = np.array([y_train[-period + (i % period)] for i in range(len(y_test))])
563	    y_test_orig = y_test
564	    y_pred_orig = preds
565	    return y_pred_orig, y_test_orig
566	
567	def baseline_persistence(y_train, y_test):
568	    """持久性预测: yhat_{t+1} = y_t (抄上月)"""
569	    preds = np.full(len(y_test), y_train[-1])
570	    y_test_orig = y_test
571	    y_pred_orig = preds
572	    return y_pred_orig, y_test_orig
573	
574	def baseline_sarima(y_train, y_test):
575	    """SARIMA(1,0,1)(1,0,1,12) 基线预测"""
576	    try:
577	        from statsmodels.tsa.statespace.sarimax import SARIMAX
578	        y_train_orig = y_train
579	        model = SARIMAX(y_train_orig, order=(1,0,1), seasonal_order=(1,0,1,12),
580	                        enforce_stationarity=False, enforce_invertibility=False)
581	        fit = model.fit(disp=False)
582	        y_pred_orig = fit.forecast(steps=len(y_test))
583	        y_test_orig = y_test
584	        return np.maximum(y_pred_orig, 0), y_test_orig
585	    except Exception:
586	        # Fallback to Naive Seasonal
587	        return baseline_naive_seasonal(y_train, y_test)
588	
589	
590	def baseline_naive_mean(y_train, y_test):
591	    """历史均值预测: 所有测试月预测为训练集均值"""
592	    preds = np.full(len(y_test), np.mean(y_train))
593	    return preds, y_test
594

597

   - roll6_mean, roll12_mean (longer rolling means)
   - roll3_std (rolling volatility)
   - ewm_alpha03 (exponential weighted mean, α=0.3)
   - yoy_diff (year-over-year difference: demand[t-1] - demand[t-13])

These are all purely autoregressive features using only past demand values. No external data dependency, no trend contamination.

Now I'll add the D-02 enhanced lag features block. I'll insert it after the `q_train` computation (line 446) and before the event features block (line 448). The features will be controlled by `USE_ENHANCED_LAG_FEATURES`.

Features to add:
- lag2: demand[t-2]
- lag3: demand[t-3]
- lag6: demand[t-6]
- roll6_mean: mean of demand[t-6:t]
- roll12_mean: mean of demand[t-12:t]
- roll3_std: std of demand[t-3:t] (volatility)
- ewm_alpha03: exponential weighted mean with α=0.3
- yoy_diff: demand[t-1] - demand[t-13] (year-over-year change in lagged value)

All purely autoregressive, no external data, no future leakage.

    for i in range(12, train_len):
        is_zero_lag12_tr[i] = (demand_train[i-12] == 0)
    m_train = (np.arange(train_len)+1) % 12; m_train[m_train==0]=12
    q_train = ((np.arange(train_len)+1) // 3) % 4; q_train[q_train==0]=4

    # D-02: 增强滞后/滚动统计特征 (纯自回归, 无外部依赖, 无未来泄露)
    enh_lag_feats_tr = []
    if USE_ENHANCED_LAG_FEATURES:
        seq = demand_train
        n = train_len
        # 多步滞后
        lag2 = np.zeros(n); lag2[2:] = seq[:-2]
        lag3 = np.zeros(n); lag3[3:] = seq[:-3]
        lag6 = np.zeros(n); lag6[6:] = seq[:-6]
        # 长窗口滚动均值
        roll6_mean = np.array([np.mean(seq[max(0,i-6):i]) if i > 0 else 0 for i in range(n)])
        roll12_mean = np.array([np.mean(seq[max(0,i-12):i]) if i > 0 else 0 for i in range(n)])
        # 滚动标准差(波动性)
        roll3_std = np.array([np.std(seq[max(0,i-3):i]) if i > 1 else 0 for i in range(n)])
        # 指数加权均值 (α=0.3, 近期权重更高)
        ewm = np.zeros(n)
        alpha = 0.3
        for i in range(1, n):
            ewm[i] = alpha * seq[i-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm[i-1]
        # 同比差分: lag1 - lag13 (捕捉年度趋势变化)
        yoy_diff = np.zeros(n)
        for i in range(13, n):
            yoy_diff[i] = seq[i-1] - seq[i-13]
        enh_lag_feats_tr = [lag2, lag3, lag6, roll6_mean, roll12_mean, roll3_std, ewm, yoy_diff]

    # 实验1: 事件保持特征 (SHOS-based)
    evt_feats_tr = []
    if USE_EVENT_FEATURES:
        d = demand_train
        n = train_len

#### Thinking

I need to add the test set enhanced lag features computation after q_test (line 537) and before the event features block (line 539). Then add `enh_lag_feats_te` to X_test_raw.

For the test set, I use `demand_raw` which contains the full series (train + test actuals). This is standard practice - when computing lag features for test set row i, I use actual past values from demand_raw. The key is that for test row at index `train_len + i`, I only use values at indices < `train_len + i` (i.e., strictly past values).

Wait - there's a subtlety. For the test set, when computing lag1 for test row i, the code uses `demand_raw[idx-1]` where `idx = train_len + i`. For i=0, this is `demand_raw[train_len - 1]` (last training value). For i=1, this is `demand_raw[train_len]` which is the actual test value at position 0. This means the test set lag features use actual test values as "past" for subsequent test rows. This is the standard approach in time series evaluation (using actual past values, not predicted values).

So for the enhanced features, I'll compute them the same way - using demand_raw with strict past-only access.

    is_zero_lag1_te = (lag1_te == 0).astype(float)
    is_zero_lag12_te = (lag12_te == 0).astype(float)
    m_test = (np.arange(train_len+1, train_len+N_TEST+1)) % 12; m_test[m_test==0]=12
    q_test = ((np.arange(train_len+1, train_len+N_TEST+1)) // 3) % 4; q_test[q_test==0]=4

    # D-02: 测试集增强滞后/滚动特征 (使用demand_raw的历史真实值, 无未来泄露)
    enh_lag_feats_te = []
    if USE_ENHANCED_LAG_FEATURES:
        nt = N_TEST; tl = train_len
        lag2_te = np.zeros(nt); lag3_te = np.zeros(nt); lag6_te = np.zeros(nt)
        roll6_te = np.zeros(nt); roll12_te = np.zeros(nt); roll3std_te = np.zeros(nt)
        ewm_te = np.zeros(nt); yoy_te = np.zeros(nt)
        # EWM需要训练集末尾状态作为初始值
        alpha = 0.3
        ewm_state = 0.0
        for i in range(1, tl):
            ewm_state = alpha * demand_raw[i-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm_state
        for i in range(nt):
            idx = tl + i
            lag2_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-2] if idx >= 2 else 0
            lag3_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-3] if idx >= 3 else 0

            roll3std_te[i] = np.std(demand_raw[max(0,idx-3):idx]) if idx > 1 else 0
            ewm_state = alpha * demand_raw[idx-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm_state
            ewm_te[i] = ewm_state
            yoy_te[i] = (demand_raw[idx-1] - demand_raw[idx-13]) if idx >= 13 else 0
        enh_lag_feats_te = [lag2_te, lag3_te, lag6_te, roll6_te, roll12_te, roll3std_te, ewm_te, yoy_te]

#### Thinking

Good. Now let me also update the logger.info line to show when enhanced lag features are on. Let me also check if there's a potential issue with the test set EWM computation. 

Wait - there's a subtle bug in my test set EWM computation. The test set uses `demand_raw[idx-1]` which for test row i=0 is `demand_raw[train_len - 1]` (last training value). But for test row i=1, it's `demand_raw[train_len]` which is the actual test value at position 0. This is the same approach used by the existing lag1_te computation, so it's consistent.

However, there's an issue: the EWM state initialization loop `for i in range(1, tl)` computes the EWM state at the end of training. But this uses `demand_raw[i-1]` for i from 1 to tl-1, which means it processes demand_raw[0] through demand_raw[tl-2]. The last value processed is demand_raw[tl-2], not demand_raw[tl-1]. So the EWM state at the start of test is based on data up to index tl-2.

Actually wait, let me re-check the training set EWM:
```
ewm[0] = 0
for i in range(1, n):
    ewm[i] = alpha * seq[i-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm[i-1]
```
So ewm[n-1] = alpha * seq[n-2] + (1-alpha) * ewm[n-2]. The last training EWM value uses seq[n-2] = demand_train[train_len-2].

For the test set initialization:
```
ewm_state = 0.0
for i in range(1, tl):
    ewm_state = alpha * demand_raw[i-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm_state
```
After this loop, ewm_state = alpha * demand_raw[tl-2] + (1-alpha) * prev_state. This matches the training EWM at position tl-1.

Then for test row i=0:
```
ewm_state = alpha * demand_raw[idx-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm_state
```
where idx = tl + 0 = tl, so demand_raw[tl-1] is the last training value. This gives the EWM at test position 0, which uses the last training value. This is correct - it's the EWM "prediction" for position tl based on all past data.

Actually wait, I need to think more carefully. In the training set:
- ewm[i] represents the EWM "feature" for predicting position i
- ewm[i] = alpha * seq[i-1] + (1-alpha) * ewm[i-1]
- So ewm[i] is a smoothed version of past values up to i-1

For the test set, I want ewm_te[0] to be the EWM feature for predicting test position 0 (which is global position tl):
- ewm_te[0] should = alpha * demand_raw[tl-1] + (1-alpha) * ewm_state_at_tl-1

My initialization loop computes ewm_state through demand_raw[tl-2], giving the state at position tl-1. Then in the test loop for i=0, idx=tl:
- ewm_state = alpha * demand_raw[tl-1] + (1-alpha) * ewm_state

This gives the correct EWM feature for test position 0. Good.

But wait - there's a problem. For test row i=1, idx=tl+1:
- ewm_state = alpha * demand_raw[tl] + (1-alpha) * ewm_state

demand_raw[tl] is the actual test value at position 0. This means we're using the actual test value to compute the EWM feature for the next test row. This is the same approach as lag1_te (which uses demand_raw[idx-1] = the actual previous value). This is standard in time series evaluation - we assume we observe the actual value before predicting the next one.

OK, this is fine and consistent with the existing code.

Actually wait - the current run has BOTH changes:
1. External factors OFF (D-01 cleanup)
2. Enhanced lag features ON (D-02)

So the comparison should be:
- Old baseline (external factors ON, no enhanced lag): R²=0.4886
- New (external factors OFF + enhanced lag ON): R²=0.6715

Actually, looking more carefully at the history:
- The v2 baseline (R²=0.4886) was run WITH the 7 FACTOR_NAMES (including external factors)
- D-01 showed that external factors caused ΔR²=-0.021 compared to the original 5-material baseline
- But the v2 baseline was on 20 materials, not 5

The key comparison is: v2 baseline (R²=0.4886, 20 materials, external factors in Spearman pool) vs D-02 result (R²=0.6715, 19 materials, external factors removed + enhanced lag added).

The improvement is +0.183 in R² mean. This is a very significant improvement. The pass ratio went from 5% to 37%.

However, I should note that this improvement comes from TWO changes simultaneously:
1. Removing external factors from the Spearman pool (so top-4 selection only picks from 4 internal factors)
2. Adding 8 enhanced lag features

To properly attribute, I'd need to run with only one change at a time. But given the user's instruction to not ask for confirmation and make optimal decisions, and given that D-01 was already proven negative, the combined result is what matters.

#### Thinking

Now let me fix the OPGW NaN issue. The problem is that OPGW has sparse data, and after ffill/bfill, some factor columns still have NaN. Let me look at the preprocess_data function to understand where NaN could come from.

The issue is in `sub = df[cols].copy().ffill().bfill().fillna(0)`. If a column is entirely NaN (e.g., a factor that doesn't exist for OPGW), ffill/bfill won't help, but fillna(0) should catch it. 

Wait, the error says "Input X contains NaN" for Ridge-2S and ElasticNet-2S. This happens AFTER the MinMaxScaler. Let me think about what could cause NaN after MinMaxScaler...

MinMaxScaler can produce NaN if a column has all identical values (std=0 → division by zero). Or if the input already has NaN.

Actually, looking more carefully: `sub = df[cols].copy().ffill().bfill().fillna(0)` - this should handle NaN. But what if the top-4 factor selection picks a column that doesn't exist in the DataFrame? Let me check...

In `get_top_factors`, it checks `if f in df.columns`. So it only selects factors that exist. But what if the factor column exists but is all NaN for OPGW?

Actually, the more likely issue is with the enhanced lag features. If demand_raw has NaN values (which shouldn't happen after fillna(0)), or if the rolling computations produce NaN...

Wait - let me look at the roll3_std computation: `np.std(seq[max(0,i-3):i])` - when i=0, this is `np.std(seq[0:0])` which is `np.std([])` = NaN! And when i=1, `np.std(seq[0:1])` = 0 (std of single element). 

Actually, `np.std([])` returns NaN with a warning. Let me check my code:
```python
roll3_std = np.array([np.std(seq[max(0,i-3):i]) if i > 1 else 0 for i in range(n)])
```

I have `if i > 1 else 0`, so for i=0 and i=1, it returns 0. For i=2, `seq[max(0,-1):2]` = `seq[0:2]`, std of 2 elements is fine. So this shouldn't produce NaN.

Let me check the test set version:
```python
roll3std_te[i] = np.std(demand_raw[max(0,idx-3):idx]) if idx > 1 else 0
```
For idx = train_len + 0 = 69, this is `np.std(demand_raw[66:69])` which is fine.

Hmm, maybe the issue is pre-existing (before D-02). The OPGW NaN error was mentioned in the summary as a known issue. Let me check if it's related to the factor columns being all-zero or all-NaN for OPGW.

Actually, looking at the data: OPGW is a material in the data.xlsx. The factors are project_count, transformer_bids, monthly_bid_count, uhv_bids. If OPGW's sheet has these columns but they're all 0 or NaN, then after ffill/bfill/fillna(0), they'd be all 0. Then MinMaxScaler would produce NaN for those columns (because max-min = 0, division by zero).

Wait, MinMaxScaler handles constant columns by setting them to 0 (it has a check for zero range). Let me verify... Actually, sklearn's MinMaxScaler does handle this: if max == min, it sets the output to 0. So that shouldn't be the issue.

Let me think again. The error is specifically for Ridge-2S and ElasticNet-2S. These use sklearn's RidgeCV and ElasticNetCV which don't accept NaN. The NaN must be coming from somewhere in the feature matrix.

Could it be from the `data_test[:,1:]` part? If the test set factor columns have NaN... But we did ffill/bfill/fillna(0) on the full df before splitting.

Actually wait - let me re-read the code. The `sub = df[cols].copy().ffill().bfill().fillna(0)` operates on the full DataFrame. But what if the top-4 factors selected for OPGW include a column that doesn't exist in the sheet? 

Looking at get_top_factors: it uses `df.iloc[:len(df)-N_TEST]` (training portion only) and checks `if f in df.columns`. The df passed to preprocess_data is the full sheet DataFrame. If the sheet has columns like '日期', '需求量', '项目数量', etc., the factor names need to match.

Actually, I think the issue might be that OPGW's data has very few non-zero demand months, and the Spearman correlation computation might fail or produce NaN correlations, leading to an empty top4 list or factors that don't exist.

Let me just add a NaN guard in preprocess_data after the feature matrix is assembled - replace any remaining NaN with 0 before scaling. This is a safe defensive fix.

Here are the findings from `C:\Users\董文涛\PycharmProjects\vmd-catboost\main.py`:

---

## 1. Column Name Mapping (Lines 282–292)

```python
# Excel 列名中英文映射
COLUMN_CN = {
    'date': '日期',
    'demand': '需求量',
    'project_count': '项目数量',
    'transformer_bids': 'transformer_bids',
    'monthly_bid_count': 'monthly_bid_count',
    'uhv_bids': 'uhv_bids',
    'has_batch': 'has_batch',
    'digital_bids': 'digital_bids',
}
COLUMN_EN = {v: k for k, v in COLUMN_CN.items()}
```

`COLUMN_EN` is the **reverse** dict used for renaming Excel columns to internal names:

| Excel column (Chinese/original) | Internal name |
|---|---|
| `日期` | `date` |
| `需求量` | `demand` |
| `项目数量` | `project_count` |
| `transformer_bids` | `transformer_bids` (no-op) |
| `monthly_bid_count` | `monthly_bid_count` (no-op) |
| `uhv_bids` | `uhv_bids` (no-op) |
| `has_batch` | `has_batch` (no-op) |
| `digital_bids` | `digital_bids` (no-op) |

**Important:** The columns `aluminum_price`, `industrial_elec`, and `industrial_elec_yoy` are **NOT** in `COLUMN_CN`/`COLUMN_EN`. They are expected to already have English names in the Excel file (no renaming needed). They are declared as external factors at lines 116–117:

```python
_EXTERNAL_FACTORS = ['aluminum_price', 'industrial_elec', 'industrial_elec_yoy']
```

but are gated behind `USE_EXTERNAL_FACTORS = False` (line 113), so they are excluded from `FACTOR_NAMES` by default.

---

## 2. Data Loading — `load_or_generate_data()` (Lines 295–322)

```python
def load_or_generate_data():
    """加载数据：读取 data.xlsx 全部 sheet，动态匹配物资"""
    if os.path.exists(DATA_FILE):
        logger.info(f"读取已有数据文件: {DATA_FILE}")
        data_dict = {}
        all_sheets = pd.read_excel(DATA_FILE, sheet_name=None)
        for sheet_name, df in all_sheets.items():
            df.rename(columns=COLUMN_EN, inplace=True)
            df['date'] = pd.to_datetime(df['date'])
            if 'demand' not in df.columns: continue
            data_dict[sheet_name] = df
            logger.info(f"  Sheet[{sheet_name}]: {len(df)} 条, ...")
        if data_dict:
            return data_dict
```

Key mechanics:
- `pd.read_excel(DATA_FILE, sheet_name=None)` reads **all sheets** into a dict `{sheet_name: DataFrame}`.
- Each sheet's DataFrame is renamed in-place via `df.rename(columns=COLUMN_EN, inplace=True)` — this converts `日期→date`, `需求量→demand`, `项目数量→project_count`.
- Sheets without a `demand` column after renaming are skipped.
- The file path is `inputs/data.xlsx` (line 237: `DATA_FILE = os.path.join(DATA_DIR, 'data.xlsx')`).

---

## 3. DataFrame Columns When Passed to `preprocess_data()` (Lines 1357–1363 in `main()`)

```python
df = data_dict[material]
X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, feature_scaler = \
    preprocess_data(df, material)
```

At this point, `df` contains **all columns from the Excel sheet after renaming**:

`['date', 'demand', 'project_count', 'transformer_bids', 'monthly_bid_count', 'uhv_bids', 'has_batch', 'digital_bids']`

Plus, if present in the Excel: `aluminum_price`, `industrial_elec`, `industrial_elec_yoy` (already English-named, untouched by rename).

---

## 4. What `preprocess_data()` Actually Uses (Lines 419–423)

```python
def preprocess_data(df, material):
    top4 = get_top_factors(material, df.iloc[:len(df)-N_TEST])
    cols = ['demand'] + top4
    sub = df[cols].copy().ffill().bfill().fillna(0)
```

It selects only `demand` + the top-4 factors (chosen dynamically via Spearman correlation from `FACTOR_NAMES`). With `USE_EXTERNAL_FACTORS = False`, the candidate pool is:

```python
_INTERNAL_FACTORS = ['project_count', 'transformer_bids', 'monthly_bid_count', 'uhv_bids']
```

So `top4` will be some permutation of those 4 internal factors, and the working subset is `['demand', <4 factors>]`. The remaining columns (`date`, `has_batch`, `digital_bids`, and any external factors) are carried in `df` but not used in feature construction unless `USE_EXTERNAL_FACTORS` is enabled.
```

31	# ---- 抑制 matplotlib Agg 后端 Windows 字体权限错误 ----
32	# 某些系统字体文件（如 C:\Windows\Fonts\ 下）有权限限制，
33	# matplotlib 尝试读取时会产生 PermissionError，表现为控制台大量
34	# "Exception ignored in: 'read_from_file_callback'" 输出。
35	# 该错误不会影响图表正常生成，但污染控制台输出。

37	
38	# 清除旧的字体缓存以强制重建
39	_font_cache_dir = matplotlib.get_cachedir()
40	for _fname in os.listdir(_font_cache_dir):
41	    if _fname.startswith('fontlist'):
42	        _cache_path = os.path.join(_font_cache_dir, _fname)
43	        try:
44	            os.remove(_cache_path)
45	        except OSError:
46	            pass
47	
48	# 仅加载可访问的 TrueType 字体，跳过有权限问题的字体文件
49	_font_dirs = set(fm.findSystemFonts())
50	_accessible_fonts = []
51	for _fp in _font_dirs:
52	    try:
53	        with open(_fp, 'rb') as _f:
54	            _f.read(4)
55	        _accessible_fonts.append(_fp)
56	    except (PermissionError, OSError):
57	        pass
58	
59	# 使用过滤后的字体列表重建字体管理器
60	fm.fontManager = fm.FontManager()
61	for _fp in _accessible_fonts:
62	    try:
63	        fm.fontManager.addfont(_fp)
64	    except Exception:
65	        pass
66	
67	# 设置中文字体 —— 有 SimHei 用 SimHei，否则退回 DejaVu Sans
68	_zh_fonts = [f for f in _accessible_fonts if any(
69	    name in f.lower() for name in ['simhei', 'simsun', 'msyh', 'yahei', 'wqy'])
70	]
71	if not _zh_fonts:
72	    _zh_fonts = [f for f in _accessible_fonts if 'dejavu' in f.lower()]
73	
74	plt.rcParams['font.sans-serif'] = ['SimHei', 'WenQuanYi Micro Hei', 'Noto Sans CJK SC', 'DejaVu Sans']
75	plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False
76	
77	# 抑制底层 C 扩展在字体回调中抛出的 "Exception ignored" 噪音
78	# 当 matplotlib Agg 后端渲染文字时，尝试读取某些受限制的系统字体文件
79	# 会触发 PermissionError。fontmanager 重建后基本不会再发生，此处作为兜底。
80	@contextmanager
81	def suppress_font_stderr():
82	    if sys.platform != 'win32':
83	        yield
84	        return
85	    _orig_stderr = sys.stderr
86	    class _Filter(io.StringIO):
87	        def write(self, s):
88	            if isinstance(s, str) and ('Permission' in s or 'read_from_file_callback' in s):
89	                return len(s)  # 静默吞掉该条消息
90	            _orig_stderr.write(s)
91	            return len(s) if isinstance(s, str) else 0
92	    sys.stderr = _Filter()

215	def _match_hp(material, overrides):
216	    """在 overrides 字典中查找匹配 material 的键, 返回合并参数(或空dict)"""
217	    for key, params in overrides.items():
218	        if key in material:
219	            return params
220	    return {}
221	
222	
223	def get_hp(model_key, material):
224	    """获取指定模型+物资的超参数: HP_DEFAULTS[model_key] + HP_OVERRIDES[model_key][material_match]
225	
226	    用法: params = get_hp('catboost_2s', material)
227	          reg = CatBoostRegressor(**params, loss_function='RMSE', ...)
228	    """
229	    defaults = HP_DEFAULTS.get(model_key, {})
230	    overrides = HP_OVERRIDES.get(model_key, {})
231	    material_overrides = _match_hp(material, overrides)
232	    return {**defaults, **material_overrides}  # 覆盖合并 (material takes priority)

284	    'demand': '需求量',

295	def load_or_generate_data():
296	    """加载数据：读取 data.xlsx 全部 sheet，动态匹配物资"""
297	    if os.path.exists(DATA_FILE):
298	        logger.info(f"读取已有数据文件: {DATA_FILE}")
299	        data_dict = {}
300	        all_sheets = pd.read_excel(DATA_FILE, sheet_name=None)
301	        for sheet_name, df in all_sheets.items():
302	            df.rename(columns=COLUMN_EN, inplace=True)
303	            df['date'] = pd.to_datetime(df['date'])
304	            if 'demand' not in df.columns: continue
305	            data_dict[sheet_name] = df
306	            logger.info(f"  Sheet[{sheet_name}]: {len(df)} 条, demand范围=[{df['demand'].min():.2f}, {df['demand'].max():.2f}]")
307	        if data_dict:
308	            return data_dict
309	        logger.warning("  未加载到有效数据，删除文件重试...")
310	        os.remove(DATA_FILE)
311	
312	    if DATA_LOCKED:
313	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"数据文件不存在且DATA_LOCKED=True: {DATA_FILE}")
314	    logger.info("数据文件不存在，重新生成...")
315	    # Trigger rebuild via build_real_data.py
316	    build_script = r'C:/Users/董文涛/PycharmProjects/bidding-ecp-data/build_real_data.py'
317	    if os.path.exists(build_script):
318	        import subprocess
319	        subprocess.run([sys.executable, build_script], check=False)
320	    if os.path.exists(DATA_FILE):
321	        return load_or_generate_data()
322	    raise FileNotFoundError(f"无法生成数据文件: {DATA_FILE}")

325	# ===================== 1.6 量级特征 (lag-1, 备选) =====================
326	_mag_cache = {}
327	
328	def build_magnitude_features(df, material):
329	    """从bid_items表提取采购事件量级特征(lag-1, 无数据泄漏)。
330	
331	    特征(6维): n_packages, n_sub_bids, n_orgs, n_items, avg_item_qty, max_item_qty
332	    关键设计: lag-1 — 当前月使用的是上个月的实际值, 避免同源泄漏。
333	    工业逻辑: '上次采购规模大→这次可能是间歇期'(lag-1 rho为负)。
334	    """
335	    if not USE_MAG_FEATURES:
336	        return np.zeros((len(df), 0))
337	    if material in _mag_cache:
338	        return _mag_cache[material]
339	
340	    import sqlite3
341	    # Try both relative and absolute paths for portability
342	    db_path = r'D:\Users\dell\PycharmProjects\bidding-ecp-data\data\ecp_data.db'
343	    if not os.path.exists(db_path):
344	        alt = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)),
345	                           '..', '..', 'bidding-ecp-data', 'data', 'ecp_data.db')
346	        db_path = os.path.normpath(alt)
347	    if not os.path.exists(db_path):
348	        return np.zeros((len(df), 0))
349	
350	    mat_short = material
351	    if ',' in mat_short: mat_short = mat_short.split(',')[0]
352	    pattern = f'%{mat_short}%'
353	    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path); c = conn.cursor()
354	    c.execute('''SELECT demand_month, demand_quantity, package_no, sub_bid_name, project_org_name
355	        FROM bid_items WHERE material_name LIKE ? AND demand_quantity > 0 AND demand_month >= ? AND demand_month <= ?''',
356	        (pattern, '201911', '202607'))
357	    rows = c.fetchall(); conn.close()
358	
359	    from collections import defaultdict
360	    monthly = defaultdict(lambda: {'qty':0, 'pkgs':set(), 'subs':set(), 'orgs':set(), 'items':0, 'max_q':0})
361	    for dm, q, pkg, sub, org in rows:
362	        qf = float(q) if q else 0
363	        monthly[dm]['qty'] += qf; monthly[dm]['pkgs'].add(pkg or '')
364	        monthly[dm]['subs'].add(sub or ''); monthly[dm]['orgs'].add(org or '')
365	        monthly[dm]['items'] += 1; monthly[dm]['max_q'] = max(monthly[dm]['max_q'], qf)
366	
367	    # Align with df dates (81 months from 2019-11)
368	    n_df = len(df)
369	    dates = df['date'].values
370	    arr = np.zeros((n_df, 6))
371	    for i in range(n_df):
372	        d = pd.Timestamp(dates[i])
373	        dm_key = f'{d.year}{d.month:02d}'
374	        entry = monthly.get(dm_key, {'qty':0, 'pkgs':set(), 'subs':set(), 'orgs':set(), 'items':0, 'max_q':0})
375	        arr[i,0] = len(entry['pkgs']); arr[i,1] = len(entry['subs'])
376	        arr[i,2] = len(entry['orgs']); arr[i,3] = entry['items']
377	        arr[i,4] = entry['qty'] / max(entry['items'], 1)
378	        arr[i,5] = entry['max_q']
379	    # LAG-1: row[t] uses row[t-1] value (only past info, no future leak)
380	    arr_lagged = np.vstack([np.zeros((1, 6)), arr[:-1]])
381	    _mag_cache[material] = arr_lagged
382	    return arr_lagged
383	
384	
385	# ===================== 2. Top-4 影响因子（基于Spearman动态计算） =====================
386	_top_factors_cache = {}
387	
388	def get_top_factors(material, df=None):
389	    """基于Spearman相关系数动态选择top-4影响因子（首次计算后缓存）"""
390	    if material in _top_factors_cache:
391	        return _top_factors_cache[material]
392	    if df is None or 'demand' not in df.columns:
393	        # fallback: 按默认顺序取前4个
394	        fallback = FACTOR_NAMES[:4]
395	        _top_factors_cache[material] = fallback
396	        return fallback
397	    from scipy.stats import spearmanr
398	    scores = {}
399	    for f in FACTOR_NAMES:
400	        if f in df.columns:
401	            valid = df[[f, 'demand']].dropna()
402	            if len(valid) > 5:
403	                corr, _ = spearmanr(valid[f], valid['demand'])
404	                scores[f] = abs(corr)
405	    ranked = sorted(scores, key=scores.get, reverse=True)
406	    top4 = ranked[:4]
407	    if len(top4) < 4:
408	        for f in FACTOR_NAMES:
409	            if f not in top4 and f in df.columns:
410	                top4.append(f)
411	                if len(top4) == 4:
412	                    break
413	    _top_factors_cache[material] = top4
414	    logger.info(f"  [Spearman因子选择] {MATERIAL_LABELS.get(material, material)}: top4={top4}")
415	    return top4
416	
417	
418	# ===================== 3. 数据预处理 =====================
419	def preprocess_data(df, material):
420	    """时序安全特征工程: 树模型不需要y归一化, 仅对特征做MinMaxScaler."""
421	    top4 = get_top_factors(material, df.iloc[:len(df)-N_TEST])
422	    cols = ['demand'] + top4
423	    sub = df[cols].copy().ffill().bfill().fillna(0)
424	    data = sub.values.astype(np.float64)
425	    demand_raw = data[:, 0].copy()
426	
427	    # Step 1: 分割
428	    train_len = len(data) - N_TEST
429	    data_train, data_test = data[:train_len], data[train_len:]
430	    demand_train, demand_test = demand_raw[:train_len], demand_raw[train_len:]
431	
432	    # Step 2: 训练集lag/rolling (无未来泄露)
433	    def make_lag_rolling(seq):
434	        n = len(seq)
435	        lag1 = np.zeros(n); lag1[1:] = seq[:-1]
436	        lag12 = np.zeros(n); lag12[12:] = seq[:-12]
437	        roll3 = np.array([np.mean(seq[max(0,i-3):i]) for i in range(n)])
438	        return lag1, lag12, roll3
439	
440	    lag1_tr, lag12_tr, roll3_tr = make_lag_rolling(demand_train)
441	    is_zero_lag1_tr = (demand_train == 0).astype(float)
442	    is_zero_lag12_tr = np.zeros(train_len, dtype=float)
443	    for i in range(12, train_len):
444	        is_zero_lag12_tr[i] = (demand_train[i-12] == 0)
445	    m_train = (np.arange(train_len)+1) % 12; m_train[m_train==0]=12
446	    q_train = ((np.arange(train_len)+1) // 3) % 4; q_train[q_train==0]=4
447	
448	    # D-02: 增强滞后/滚动统计特征 (纯自回归, 无外部依赖, 无未来泄露)
449	    enh_lag_feats_tr = []
450	    if USE_ENHANCED_LAG_FEATURES:
451	        seq = demand_train
452	        n = train_len
453	        # 多步滞后
454	        lag2 = np.zeros(n); lag2[2:] = seq[:-2]
455	        lag3 = np.zeros(n); lag3[3:] = seq[:-3]
456	        lag6 = np.zeros(n); lag6[6:] = seq[:-6]
457	        # 长窗口滚动均值
458	        roll6_mean = np.array([np.mean(seq[max(0,i-6):i]) if i > 0 else 0 for i in range(n)])
459	        roll12_mean = np.array([np.mean(seq[max(0,i-12):i]) if i > 0 else 0 for i in range(n)])
460	        # 滚动标准差(波动性)
461	        roll3_std = np.array([np.std(seq[max(0,i-3):i]) if i > 1 else 0 for i in range(n)])
462	        # 指数加权均值 (α=0.3, 近期权重更高)
463	        ewm = np.zeros(n)
464	        alpha = 0.3
465	        for i in range(1, n):
466	            ewm[i] = alpha * seq[i-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm[i-1]
467	        # 同比差分: lag1 - lag13 (捕捉年度趋势变化)
468	        yoy_diff = np.zeros(n)
469	        for i in range(13, n):
470	            yoy_diff[i] = seq[i-1] - seq[i-13]
471	        enh_lag_feats_tr = [lag2, lag3, lag6, roll6_mean, roll12_mean, roll3_std, ewm, yoy_diff]
472	
473	    # 实验1: 事件保持特征 (SHOS-based)
474	    evt_feats_tr = []
475	    if USE_EVENT_FEATURES:
476	        d = demand_train
477	        n = train_len
478	        # Stop类: 距上次事件的间隔
479	        gap_since_last = np.zeros(n)
480	        last_event = -999
481	        for i in range(n):
482	            if d[i] > 0: last_event = i
483	            gap_since_last[i] = i - last_event if last_event >= 0 else n
484	        # Hoover类: 事件密度
485	        evt_cnt_6m = np.zeros(n); evt_cnt_12m = np.zeros(n)
486	        cumul_12m = np.zeros(n)
487	        for i in range(n):
488	            evt_cnt_6m[i] = np.sum(d[max(0,i-5):i+1] > 0)
489	            evt_cnt_12m[i] = np.sum(d[max(0,i-11):i+1] > 0)
490	            cumul_12m[i] = np.sum(d[max(0,i-11):i+1])
491	        # Occurrence类: 月份事件频率(平滑)
492	        month_freq = np.zeros(n)
493	        cal_month = np.array([(4+i)%12+1 for i in range(n)])
494	        for i in range(12, n):
495	            past_same_month = [j for j in range(i) if cal_month[j]==cal_month[i]]
496	            if past_same_month:
497	                month_freq[i] = np.mean(d[past_same_month] > 0)
498	        # 注: last_evt_mag/evt_trend/evt_cv 在测试集为常数(仅复制训练集最后值), 已移除
499	        evt_feats_tr = [gap_since_last, evt_cnt_6m, evt_cnt_12m, cumul_12m, month_freq]
500	
501	    # 方案A: 季度末哑变量
502	    qtr_end_cols = []
503	    if USE_QUARTER_DUMMIES:
504	        cal_month_tr = np.array([(4 + i) % 12 + 1 for i in range(train_len)])
505	        is_q1_end = (cal_month_tr == 3).astype(float)
506	        is_q2_end = (cal_month_tr == 6).astype(float)
507	        is_q3_q4_end = ((cal_month_tr == 9) | (cal_month_tr == 12)).astype(float)
508	        qtr_end_cols = [is_q1_end, is_q2_end, is_q3_q4_end]
509	
510	    X_train_raw = np.column_stack([
511	        data_train[:,1:], lag1_tr, lag12_tr, roll3_tr,
512	        is_zero_lag1_tr, is_zero_lag12_tr,
513	        np.sin(2*np.pi*m_train/12), np.cos(2*np.pi*m_train/12),
514	        np.sin(2*np.pi*q_train/4), np.cos(2*np.pi*q_train/4),
515	    ] + evt_feats_tr + qtr_end_cols + enh_lag_feats_tr)
516	
517	    # 实验6: 量级特征(lag-1) → 追加到特征矩阵末尾
518	    mag_all = build_magnitude_features(df, material)
519	    if USE_MAG_FEATURES and mag_all.shape[1] > 0:
520	        X_train_raw = np.column_stack([X_train_raw, mag_all[:train_len]])
521	
522	    # Step 3: 防御性NaN清洗 + 特征Scaler仅对训练集fit
523	    X_train_raw = np.nan_to_num(X_train_raw, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
524	    feature_scaler = MinMaxScaler()
525	    X_train = feature_scaler.fit_transform(X_train_raw)
526	    y_train = demand_train.copy()
527	
528	    # Step 4: 测试集特征
529	    lag1_te = np.zeros(N_TEST); lag12_te = np.zeros(N_TEST); roll3_te = np.zeros(N_TEST)
530	    for i in range(N_TEST):
531	        idx = train_len + i
532	        lag1_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-1] if idx>0 else 0
533	        lag12_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-12] if idx>=12 else 0
534	        roll3_te[i] = np.mean(demand_raw[max(0,idx-3):idx])
535	    is_zero_lag1_te = (lag1_te == 0).astype(float)
536	    is_zero_lag12_te = (lag12_te == 0).astype(float)
537	    m_test = (np.arange(train_len+1, train_len+N_TEST+1)) % 12; m_test[m_test==0]=12
538	    q_test = ((np.arange(train_len+1, train_len+N_TEST+1)) // 3) % 4; q_test[q_test==0]=4
539	
540	    # D-02: 测试集增强滞后/滚动特征 (使用demand_raw的历史真实值, 无未来泄露)
541	    enh_lag_feats_te = []
542	    if USE_ENHANCED_LAG_FEATURES:
543	        nt = N_TEST; tl = train_len
544	        lag2_te = np.zeros(nt); lag3_te = np.zeros(nt); lag6_te = np.zeros(nt)
545	        roll6_te = np.zeros(nt); roll12_te = np.zeros(nt); roll3std_te = np.zeros(nt)
546	        ewm_te = np.zeros(nt); yoy_te = np.zeros(nt)
547	        # EWM需要训练集末尾状态作为初始值
548	        alpha = 0.3
549	        ewm_state = 0.0
550	        for i in range(1, tl):
551	            ewm_state = alpha * demand_raw[i-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm_state
552	        for i in range(nt):
553	            idx = tl + i
554	            lag2_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-2] if idx >= 2 else 0
555	            lag3_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-3] if idx >= 3 else 0
556	            lag6_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-6] if idx >= 6 else 0
557	            roll6_te[i] = np.mean(demand_raw[max(0,idx-6):idx]) if idx > 0 else 0
558	            roll12_te[i] = np.mean(demand_raw[max(0,idx-12):idx]) if idx > 0 else 0
559	            roll3std_te[i] = np.std(demand_raw[max(0,idx-3):idx]) if idx > 1 else 0
560	            ewm_state = alpha * demand_raw[idx-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm_state
561	            ewm_te[i] = ewm_state
562	            yoy_te[i] = (demand_raw[idx-1] - demand_raw[idx-13]) if idx >= 13 else 0
563	        enh_lag_feats_te = [lag2_te, lag3_te, lag6_te, roll6_te, roll12_te, roll3std_te, ewm_te, yoy_te]
564	
565	    # 实验1: 测试集事件特征
566	    evt_feats_te = []
567	    if USE_EVENT_FEATURES:
568	        d_all = demand_raw; tl = train_len; nt = N_TEST
569	        gap_sl_te = np.zeros(nt); last_ev = -999
570	        for i in range(tl):
571	            if d_all[i] > 0: last_ev = i
572	        for i in range(nt):
573	            idx = tl + i
574	            if d_all[idx] > 0: last_ev = idx
575	            gap_sl_te[i] = idx - last_ev if last_ev >= 0 else tl
576	        ec6_te = np.zeros(nt); ec12_te = np.zeros(nt); cu12_te = np.zeros(nt)
577	        for i in range(nt):
578	            seq = d_all[max(0,tl+i-5):tl+i+1]
579	            seq12 = d_all[max(0,tl+i-11):tl+i+1]
580	            ec6_te[i] = np.sum(np.array(seq) > 0)
581	            ec12_te[i] = np.sum(np.array(seq12) > 0)
582	            cu12_te[i] = np.sum(seq12)
583	        mf_te = np.zeros(nt); cal_m_all = np.array([(4+i)%12+1 for i in range(tl+nt)])
584	        for i in range(nt):
585	            past_same = [j for j in range(tl+i) if cal_m_all[j]==cal_m_all[tl+i]]
586	            if past_same: mf_te[i] = np.mean(d_all[past_same] > 0)
587	        evt_feats_te = [gap_sl_te, ec6_te, ec12_te, cu12_te, mf_te]
588	
589	    qtr_end_cols_te = []
590	    if USE_QUARTER_DUMMIES:
591	        cal_month_te = np.array([(4 + (train_len + i)) % 12 + 1 for i in range(N_TEST)])
592	        is_q1_end_te = (cal_month_te == 3).astype(float)
593	        is_q2_end_te = (cal_month_te == 6).astype(float)
594	        is_q3_q4_end_te = ((cal_month_te == 9) | (cal_month_te == 12)).astype(float)
595	        qtr_end_cols_te = [is_q1_end_te, is_q2_end_te, is_q3_q4_end_te]
596	
597	    X_test_raw = np.column_stack([
598	        data_test[:,1:], lag1_te, lag12_te, roll3_te,
599	        is_zero_lag1_te, is_zero_lag12_te,
600	        np.sin(2*np.pi*m_test/12), np.cos(2*np.pi*m_test/12),
601	        np.sin(2*np.pi*q_test/4), np.cos(2*np.pi*q_test/4),
602	    ] + evt_feats_te + qtr_end_cols_te + enh_lag_feats_te)
603	    if USE_MAG_FEATURES and mag_all.shape[1] > 0:
604	        X_test_raw = np.column_stack([X_test_raw, mag_all[train_len:train_len+N_TEST]])
605	    X_test_raw = np.nan_to_num(X_test_raw, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
606	    X_test = feature_scaler.transform(X_test_raw)
607	    y_test = demand_test.copy()
608	
609	    logger.info(f"  [特征工程] top4={top4}, n_feat={X_train.shape[1]}维, 训练={train_len}月"
610	                + (" [事件特征ON]" if USE_EVENT_FEATURES else "")
611	                + (" [量级特征ON]" if USE_MAG_FEATURES else "")
612	                + (" [增强滞后ON]" if USE_ENHANCED_LAG_FEATURES else "")
613	                + (" [log1p]" if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else ""))
614	    return X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, feature_scaler
615	
616	def baseline_naive_seasonal(y_train, y_test, period=12):
617	    """季节性朴素预测: yhat_t = y_{t-period} (抄去年同期)"""
618	    preds = np.array([y_train[-period + (i % period)] for i in range(len(y_test))])
619	    y_test_orig = y_test
620	    y_pred_orig = preds
621	    return y_pred_orig, y_test_orig
622	
623	def baseline_persistence(y_train, y_test):
624	    """持久性预测: yhat_{t+1} = y_t (抄上月)"""
625	    preds = np.full(len(y_test), y_train[-1])
626	    y_test_orig = y_test
627	    y_pred_orig = preds
628	    return y_pred_orig, y_test_orig
629	
630	def baseline_sarima(y_train, y_test):
631	    """SARIMA(1,0,1)(1,0,1,12) 基线预测"""
632	    try:
633	        from statsmodels.tsa.statespace.sarimax import SARIMAX
634	        y_train_orig = y_train
635	        model = SARIMAX(y_train_orig, order=(1,0,1), seasonal_order=(1,0,1,12),
636	                        enforce_stationarity=False, enforce_invertibility=False)
637	        fit = model.fit(disp=False)
638	        y_pred_orig = fit.forecast(steps=len(y_test))
639	        y_test_orig = y_test
640	        return np.maximum(y_pred_orig, 0), y_test_orig
641	    except Exception:
642	        # Fallback to Naive Seasonal
643	        return baseline_naive_seasonal(y_train, y_test)
644	
645	
646	def baseline_naive_mean(y_train, y_test):
647	    """历史均值预测: 所有测试月预测为训练集均值"""
648	    preds = np.full(len(y_test), np.mean(y_train))
649	    return preds, y_test
650	
651	def evaluate_model(y_true, y_pred, y_train=None):
652	    """计算 MSE/RMSE/MAE/R²/sMAPE/MASE/WRMSSE
653	
654	    WRMSSE (M5竞赛标准): 分母为训练集季节性naive误差，对零值不敏感。
655	    """
656	    mse = mean_squared_error(y_true, y_pred)
657	    rmse = np.sqrt(mse)
658	    mae = mean_absolute_error(y_true, y_pred)
659	    r2 = r2_score(y_true, y_pred)
660	    smape = np.mean(2*np.abs(y_pred-y_true)/(np.abs(y_pred)+np.abs(y_true)+1e-10))*100
661	    mase_denom = np.mean(np.abs(y_true[1:]-y_true[:-1])) if len(y_true)>1 else 1
662	    mase = mae / max(mase_denom, 1e-10) if mase_denom > 0 else 999
663	    # WRMSSE: 分母是训练集季节性naive的RMSE (M5标准)
664	    wmsse = None
665	    if y_train is not None and len(y_train) >= 12:
666	        seas_naive_pred = np.array([y_train[-12 + (i % 12)] for i in range(len(y_true))])
667	        seas_rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((seas_naive_pred - y_true)**2))
668	        seas_naive_train_err = np.sqrt(np.mean((y_train[12:] - y_train[:-12])**2))
669	        wmsse = round(seas_rmse / max(seas_naive_train_err, 1e-10), 4)
670	    return {'MSE': round(mse,4), 'RMSE': round(rmse,4), 'MAE': round(mae,4),
671	            'R2': round(r2,4), 'sMAPE': round(smape,2), 'MASE': round(mase,4),
672	            'WRMSSE': wmsse,
673	            'zero_acc': round(np.mean((y_true==0)==(y_pred==0)),4)}
674	
675	
676	def crps_score(y_true, pred_quantiles):
677	    """sCRPS: 缩放连续排位概率分数。评估概率预测分布质量。
678	
679	    Args:
680	        y_true: shape (n,) 真实值
681	        pred_quantiles: shape (n, n_quantiles) 每个时间步的各分位数预测
682	    Returns:
683	        sCRPS 值 (数值积分近似)
684	    """
685	    n = len(y_true)
686	    yt = np.array(y_true).reshape(-1)
687	    pq = np.array(pred_quantiles)
688	    if pq.ndim != 2 or pq.shape[0] != n:
689	        return None
690	    q_levels = np.linspace(0, 1, pq.shape[1])  # [0, 1/(k-1), 2/(k-1), ..., 1]
691	    crps_vals = np.zeros(n)
692	    for i in range(n):
693	        fi = pq[i]
694	        indicator = np.array(fi <= yt[i], dtype=float)
695	        integrand = (q_levels - indicator)**2
696	        # 梯形积分: np.trapz removed in numpy 2.0
697	        crps_vals[i] = np.sum((integrand[:-1] + integrand[1:]) / 2 * np.diff(q_levels))
698	    crps = np.mean(crps_vals)
699	    # 缩放因子: 真实值绝对均值
700	    scale = np.mean(np.abs(yt)) if np.mean(np.abs(yt)) > 0 else 1
701	    return round(crps / scale, 4)
702	
703	
704	# ===================== 10. 可视化 =====================
705	def plot_prediction_comparison(all_results, material):
706	    """预测对比曲线：所有模型预测 vs 真实值"""
707	    fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 1, figsize=(14, 7))
708	    pal = ['#FF0000','#2196F3','#4CAF50','#FF9800','#8E24AA','#00BCD4','#795548','#E91E63']
709	    mks = ['o','^','D','v','s','p','*','h']
710	    unit_map = {'交流避雷器': '台/只', '电容式电压互感器': '台', '交流支柱绝缘子': '只/支',
711	                '断路器保护': '套', '电抗器保护': '套',
712	                '线路保护': '套', '10kV变压器': '台', '变压器保护': '套',
713	                '母线保护': '套', '500kVGIS组合电器': '套'}
714	
715	    results = all_results[material]
716	    test_len = len(results['CatBoost']['y_test'])
717	    end_date = pd.Timestamp.today().replace(day=1) - pd.DateOffset(months=1)
718	    x = pd.date_range(end=end_date, periods=test_len, freq='MS')
719	
720	    # Truth line
721	    ax.plot(x, results['CatBoost']['y_test'][:len(x)], color='black', marker='o',
722	            linestyle='solid', label='真实值', markersize=5, linewidth=2.5, zorder=10)
723	
724	    # All model predictions
725	    for mi, (model_name, preds) in enumerate(results.items()):
726	        if 'y_pred' not in preds: continue
727	        pred = preds['y_pred']
728	        pred_x = x[:len(pred)]
729	        r2_val = preds.get('metrics', {}).get('R2', 0)
730	        lbl = f'{model_name} (R²={r2_val:.3f})'
731	        ax.plot(pred_x, pred, color=pal[mi % len(pal)], marker=mks[mi % len(mks)],
732	                linestyle='--', label=lbl, markersize=4, alpha=0.85)
733	
734	    # Unit
735	    unit = ''
736	    for kw, u in unit_map.items():
737	        if kw in material: unit = u; break
738	    ax.set_xlabel('日期')
739	    ax.set_ylabel(f'需求量({unit})' if unit else '需求量')
740	    ax.set_title(f'{material} — 模型预测对比', fontsize=13, fontweight='bold')
741	    ax.legend(fontsize=7, loc='upper left', bbox_to_anchor=(1.02, 1), framealpha=0.9)
742	    ax.tick_params(axis='x', rotation=30)
743	    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
744	    ax.spines['top'].set_visible(False); ax.spines['right'].set_visible(False)
745	
746	    plt.tight_layout()
747	    path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f'预测对比_{material}.png')
748	    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')
749	    plt.close()
750	    logger.info(f"  [图表] 预测对比图({material}) → {path}")
751	
752	
753	def plot_feature_importance(importance_dict, material):
754	    """特征重要性条形图（CatBoost 特征重要性）"""
755	    top4 = get_top_factors(material)
756	    imp = importance_dict.get('catboost_imp')
757	    if imp is None or len(imp) == 0:
758	        return
759	
760	    n_features = len(imp)
761	    feat_names = list(top4)
762	    if len(feat_names) > n_features:
763	        feat_names = feat_names[:n_features]
764	    elif len(feat_names) < n_features:
765	        feat_names = feat_names + [f'F{i+1}' for i in range(len(feat_names), n_features)]
766	
767	    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, max(5, n_features * 0.4)))
768	    colors = plt.cm.Blues(np.linspace(0.4, 0.9, n_features))
769	    ax.barh(range(n_features), imp, color=colors, edgecolor='navy', alpha=0.85)
770	    ax.set_yticks(range(n_features))
771	    ax.set_yticklabels(feat_names)
772	    ax.set_xlabel('Importance')
773	    ax.set_title(f'CatBoost 特征重要性 — {MATERIAL_LABELS[material]}', fontweight='bold')
774	    ax.invert_yaxis()
775	    ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3, axis='x')
776	
777	    plt.tight_layout()
778	    path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f'特征重要性_{material}.png')
779	    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')
780	    plt.close()
781	    logger.info(f"  [图表] 特征重要性({material}) → {path}")
782	
783	
784	def plot_metrics_comparison(all_metrics):
785	    """模型指标对比：分组柱状图（各物资各模型的四项指标）"""
786	    # 仅使用实际有预测结果的物资（跳过被SKIP_REACTOR_PROTECT等开关排除的）
787	    active_materials = [m for m in MATERIALS if m in all_metrics]
788	    if not active_materials:
789	        return
790	    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(16, 12))
791	    metric_names = ['MSE', 'RMSE', 'MAE', 'R2']
792	    # Dynamically collect all model names from results
793	    model_names = []
794	    for material in active_materials:
795	        for m in all_metrics[material]:
796	            if m not in model_names:
797	                model_names.append(m)
798	    colors = plt.cm.tab20(np.linspace(0, 1, max(len(model_names), 1)))
799	
800	    for ax_idx, metric in enumerate(metric_names):
801	        ax = axes[ax_idx // 2, ax_idx % 2]
802	        x = np.arange(len(active_materials))
803	        width = 0.8 / max(len(model_names), 1)
804	
805	        for i, model_name in enumerate(model_names):
806	            values = []
807	            for material in active_materials:
808	                vals = all_metrics[material].get(model_name, {})
809	                values.append(vals.get(metric, 0))
810	            bars = ax.bar(x + i * width, values, width, label=model_name,
811	                          color=colors[i], alpha=0.85, edgecolor='white')
812	
813	            # 数值标注
814	            for bar, val in zip(bars, values):
815	                ax.text(bar.get_x() + bar.get_width() / 2, bar.get_height() + 0.01,
816	                        f'{val:.4f}', ha='center', va='bottom', fontsize=7, rotation=90)
817	
818	        ax.set_title(metric, fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')
819	        ax.set_xticks(x + width * (len(model_names) - 1) / 2)
820	        ax.set_xticklabels([MATERIAL_LABELS[m] for m in active_materials])
821	        ax.legend(fontsize=8)
822	        ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3, axis='y')
823	
824	    plt.suptitle('模型评估指标对比', fontsize=16, fontweight='bold', y=1.01)
825	    plt.tight_layout()
826	    path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, '指标对比图.png')
827	    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')
828	    plt.close()
829	    logger.info(f"  [图表] 指标对比图 → {path}")

832	def plot_demand_curves(data_dict):

842	        unit = ''; [unit := u for kw,u in {'交流避雷器':'台','电容式电压互感器':'台','交流支柱绝缘子':'只','断路器保护':'套','电抗器保护':'套','线路保护':'套','10kV变压器':'台'}.items() if kw in material]
843	        ax.set_ylabel(f'{MATERIAL_LABELS[material]}\n需求量({unit})' if unit else f'{MATERIAL_LABELS[material]}\n需求量', fontsize=10)
844	        ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3, linestyle='--')
845	        ax.spines['top'].set_visible(False); ax.spines['right'].set_visible(False)
846	        # 标记 train/test 分割线

848	        if split_date is not None:
849	            ax.axvline(x=split_date, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=1.2)
850	    n_months = len(data_dict[MATERIALS[0]])
851	    date_range = f"{data_dict[MATERIALS[0]]['date'].iloc[0].strftime('%Y.%m')}–{data_dict[MATERIALS[0]]['date'].iloc[-1].strftime('%Y.%m')}"
852	    axes[0].set_title(f'配电网物资需求量 — {n_months}个月完整序列 ({date_range})', fontsize=13, fontweight='bold')
853	    (axes[-1] if n_mat > 1 else axes).set_xlabel('日期')
854	    plt.tight_layout()

856	    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')

861	# ===================== log1p逆变换辅助 =====================
862	def _inverse_log1p(y_pred):
863	    """如果USE_LOG1P_TARGET启用, 将预测值从log空间逆变换回原始空间"""
864	    return np.expm1(y_pred) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_pred
865	
866	
867	# ===================== 4. 两阶段预测框架 =====================
868	def _two_stage_fit_predict(X_tr, y_tr, X_te, stage2_regressor, material):
869	    """所有模型共享的两阶段预测: Stage1分类×Stage2回归 = P×Q"""
870	    y_bin = (y_tr > 0).astype(int)
871	    n_pos, n_neg = y_bin.sum(), len(y_bin) - y_bin.sum()
872	    if n_pos < 5 or n_neg < 5 or (y_tr > 0).sum() < 10:
873	        return None, None  # 回退信号
874	
875	    hp = get_hp('stage1_cls', material)
876	    cls = CatBoostClassifier(**hp,
877	        loss_function='Logloss', early_stopping_rounds=30, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
878	    nv = max(6, len(y_tr)//4)
879	    cls.fit(X_tr[:-nv], y_bin[:-nv], eval_set=(X_tr[-nv:], y_bin[-nv:]))
880	    prob = np.clip(cls.predict_proba(X_te)[:,1], 0, 1)
881	
882	    nz = y_tr > 0
883	    nv2 = min(6, nz.sum()//4)
884	    is_cb = hasattr(stage2_regressor, 'get_params')
885	    try:
886	        stage2_regressor.fit(X_tr[nz][:-nv2], y_tr[nz][:-nv2],
887	            eval_set=(X_tr[nz][-nv2:], y_tr[nz][-nv2:]))
888	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
889	        stage2_regressor.fit(X_tr[nz], y_tr[nz])
890	    return prob * np.maximum(stage2_regressor.predict(X_te), 0), cls
891	
892	
893	def run_catboost(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
894	    """CatBoost(朴素): 直接回归, 不使用两阶段框架(消融基线)"""
895	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
896	    hp = get_hp('catboost_direct', material)
897	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
898	        loss_function='RMSE', early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
899	    nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
900	    reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
901	    yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
902	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET: yp = np.expm1(yp)
903	    return yp, y_test, reg.get_feature_importance(), reg
904	
905	def run_catboost_tweedie(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
906	    """CatBoost-Tweedie(单阶段):  复合Poisson-Gamma损失函数直接回归。
907	
908	    关键差异: 不同于两阶段Tweedie(Stage1分类+Stage2的Tweedie loss), 这是单阶段——
909	    用全部69个月(含零值)训练, Tweedie损失天然建模"零=未发标+正=采购量"的生成机制。
910	    与RMSE直接回归的对比: Tweedie在零点有离散概率质量, 不会强迫模型预测非零。
911	
912	    预期: 波动数据(CV>1)上 Tweedie > RMSE(直接) >> 两阶段
913	    """
914	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
915	    hp = get_hp('catboost_tweedie', material)
916	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
917	        loss_function='Tweedie:variance_power=1.5',
918	        early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
919	    nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
920	    reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
921	    yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
922	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET: yp = np.expm1(yp)
923	    return yp, y_test, reg.get_feature_importance(), reg
924	
925	
926	# ===================== 实验4: 分位数回归 (可取消) =====================
927	def run_catboost_quantile(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
928	    """分位数回归: 训练 alpha=0.1/0.5/0.9 三个分位数模型。
929	
930	    中位数(0.5)作为点预测, (0.1, 0.9)作为80%预测区间。
931	    优势: 不做硬判决"有/无需求", 而是输出"不确定区间"。
932	    当下限>0 → 高置信度有需求; 上限≈0 → 高置信度没有。
933	    """
934	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
935	    hp = get_hp('quantile', material)
936	    preds = {}
937	    for alpha in [0.1, 0.5, 0.9]:
938	        reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
939	            loss_function=f'Quantile:alpha={alpha}',
940	            early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
941	        nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
942	        reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
943	        p = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
944	        if USE_LOG1P_TARGET: p = np.expm1(p)
945	        preds[alpha] = p
946	    yp = preds[0.5]  # 中位数点预测
947	    logger.info(f'  [分位数] 80%区间宽度均值={np.mean(preds[0.9]-preds[0.1]):.2f}')
948	    return yp, y_test, None, (preds[0.1], preds[0.9])
949	
950	
951	def run_catboost_2s(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
952	    """TwoStage-CatBoost: 两阶段=分类×CatBoost回归"""
953	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
954	    hp = get_hp('catboost_2s', material)
955	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
956	        loss_function='RMSE', early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
957	    yp, _ = _two_stage_fit_predict(X_train_factors, y_tr_log, X_test_factors, reg, material)
958	    if yp is None:
959	        logger.warning(f'  [CatBoost-2S] 回退到直接回归')
960	        nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
961	        reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
962	        yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
963	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET and yp is not None: yp = np.expm1(yp)
964	    return yp, y_test, reg.get_feature_importance(), reg
965
