967	def run_conditional_catboost(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
968	    """TwoStage-CondCatBoost: 两阶段=分类×更深CatBoost回归"""
969	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
970	    hp = get_hp('cond_catboost', material)
971	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
972	        loss_function='RMSE', early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
973	    yp, _ = _two_stage_fit_predict(X_train_factors, y_tr_log, X_test_factors, reg, material)
974	    if yp is None:
975	        logger.warning(f'  [CondCatBoost-2S] 回退到直接回归')
976	        nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
977	        reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
978	        yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
979	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET and yp is not None: yp = np.expm1(yp)
980	    return yp, y_test, reg.get_feature_importance(), reg
981	
982	# 原 TwoStage 保留不变
983	
984	
985	# ===================== 两阶段预测 (论文核心创新) =====================
986	def run_two_stage(df_all, X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test,
987	                   material):
988	    """两阶段预测:
989	    Stage 1: CatBoost 二分类 — 预测当月是否有需求(>0)
990	    Stage 2: CatBoost 回归 — 对有需求的月份预测需求量
991	    最终 = P(有需求) × 预测量
992	    """
993	    demand_raw = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
994	    y_train_binary = (demand_raw > 0).astype(int)
995	    n_pos = y_train_binary.sum(); n_neg = len(y_train_binary) - n_pos
996	    if n_pos < 5 or n_neg < 5:
997	        logger.warning(f"  [两阶段] 正负样本不均衡(pos={n_pos},neg={n_neg}), 回退到CatBoost")
998	        return run_catboost(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material)
999	
1000	    # Stage 1: 分类器
1001	    hp_cls = get_hp('twostage_cls', material)
1002	    cls = CatBoostClassifier(**hp_cls,
1003	        loss_function='Logloss', early_stopping_rounds=30,
1004	        random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0
1005	    )
1006	    n_val = max(6, min(12, len(y_train)//4))
1007	    X_cls_tr, X_cls_val = X_train_factors[:-n_val], X_train_factors[-n_val:]
1008	    y_cls_tr, y_cls_val = y_train_binary[:-n_val], y_train_binary[-n_val:]
1009	    cls.fit(X_cls_tr, y_cls_tr, eval_set=(X_cls_val, y_cls_val))
1010	    prob_test = cls.predict_proba(X_test_factors)[:, 1]
1011	    prob_test = np.clip(prob_test, 0, 1)
1012	
1013	    # Stage 2: 回归 (仅对非零训练数据)
1014	    nonzero_mask = demand_raw > 0
1015	    if nonzero_mask.sum() < 10:
1016	        logger.warning(f"  [两阶段] 非零样本过少({nonzero_mask.sum()}), 回退到CatBoost baseline")
1017	        return run_catboost(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material)
1018	
1019	    X_nz = X_train_factors[nonzero_mask]
1020	    y_nz = demand_raw[nonzero_mask]  # demand_raw已log1p, y_train未变换
1021	
1022	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**get_hp('twostage_reg', material),
1023	        loss_function='RMSE', early_stopping_rounds=50,
1024	        random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0
1025	    )
1026	    n_val2 = min(6, len(y_nz)//4)
1027	    reg.fit(X_nz[:-n_val2], y_nz[:-n_val2],
1028	            eval_set=(X_nz[-n_val2:], y_nz[-n_val2:]))
1029	    qty_test = reg.predict(X_test_factors)
1030	
1031	    # 反归一化
1032	    y_test_orig = y_test
1033	    qty_pred_orig = qty_test
1034	    # 最终 = 概率 × 预测量
1035	    y_pred_orig = prob_test * np.maximum(qty_pred_orig, 0)
1036	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET: y_pred_orig = np.expm1(np.maximum(y_pred_orig, 0))
1037	    cls_acc = np.mean((prob_test > 0.5).astype(int) == (y_test_orig > 0).astype(int))
1038	    logger.info(f"  [两阶段] Stage1分类准确率={cls_acc:.2%}, 非零训练样本={nonzero_mask.sum()}")
1039	    return y_pred_orig, y_test_orig, None, (cls, reg)
1040	
1041	
1042	# ===================== 线性两阶段模型 =====================
1043	def run_ridge_2s(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
1044	    """TwoStage-Ridge: 两阶段=分类×Ridge回归 (小样本更稳定)"""
1045	    from sklearn.linear_model import RidgeCV
1046	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
1047	    hp = get_hp('ridge_2s', material)
1048	    reg = RidgeCV(**hp)
1049	    nz = y_tr_log > 0
1050	    yp, _ = _two_stage_fit_predict(X_train_factors, y_tr_log, X_test_factors, reg, material)
1051	    if yp is None:
1052	        reg.fit(X_train_factors, y_tr_log)
1053	        yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
1054	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET and yp is not None: yp = np.expm1(yp)
1055	    return yp, y_test, None, reg
1056	
1057	
1058	def run_elasticnet_2s(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
1059	    """TwoStage-ElasticNet: 两阶段=分类×ElasticNet(L1+L2, 小样本强正则)"""
1060	    from sklearn.linear_model import ElasticNetCV
1061	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
1062	    hp = get_hp('elasticnet_2s', material)
1063	    nz = y_tr_log > 0
1064	    reg = ElasticNetCV(**hp, cv=min(5, nz.sum()), max_iter=5000, random_state=42)
1065	    yp, _ = _two_stage_fit_predict(X_train_factors, y_tr_log, X_test_factors, reg, material)
1066	    if yp is None:
1067	        reg.fit(X_train_factors, y_tr_log)
1068	        yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
1069	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET and yp is not None: yp = np.expm1(yp)
1070	    return yp, y_test, None, reg
1071	
1072	
1073	def run_croston_sba(y_train, y_test):
1074	    """Croston-SBA: 间歇性需求专用预测。Decompose into demand interval + size.
1075	    SBA变体对Croston偏差做修正: yhat = (1-alpha/2) * size_ema / interval_ema"""
1076	    d_raw = y_train  # y不再缩放, 直接是原始值
1077	    alpha = 0.1
1078	    # 初始化
1079	    nonzero_idx = np.where(d_raw > 0)[0]
1080	    if len(nonzero_idx) < 2:
1081	        return np.full(len(y_test), np.mean(d_raw)), y_test
1082	    size_ema = d_raw[nonzero_idx[0]]
1083	    interval_ema = nonzero_idx[0] + 1 if nonzero_idx[0] > 0 else 1
1084	    last_nz = nonzero_idx[0]
1085	    size_log, intv_log = [], []
1086	    for i in range(1, len(nonzero_idx)):
1087	        interval = nonzero_idx[i] - last_nz
1088	        demand = d_raw[nonzero_idx[i]]
1089	        size_ema = alpha * demand + (1-alpha) * size_ema
1090	        interval_ema = alpha * interval + (1-alpha) * interval_ema
1091	        last_nz = nonzero_idx[i]
1092	        size_log.append(size_ema); intv_log.append(interval_ema)
1093	    if not size_log:
1094	        return np.full(len(y_test), np.mean(d_raw)), y_test
1095	    s, iv = size_log[-1], max(intv_log[-1], 1)
1096	    sba = s / iv * (1 - alpha/2)  # SBA修正
1097	    preds = np.array([sba if (i % max(1, int(round(iv)))) == 0 else 0 for i in range(len(y_test))])
1098	    return np.maximum(preds, 0), y_test
1099	
1100	
1101	# ===================== Chronos 零样本基线 (Phase 1: TSFM通用性边界) =====================
1102	# chronos-t5-tiny (8M参数, ~50MB): 最小TSFM基线, 内存友好。
1103	# 首次运行需联网下载。国内网络需代理 (设置HTTPS_PROXY=http://127.0.0.1:7890)
1104	# 如需更强基线，安装 chronos-2 (pip install "chronos-forecasting>=2.0" + 120M模型)
1105	CHRONOS_MODEL = os.environ.get('CHRONOS_MODEL', 'amazon/chronos-t5-tiny')
1106	CHRONOS_LOCAL_PATH = os.environ.get('CHRONOS_LOCAL_PATH', '')
1107	
1108	def baseline_chronos(y_train, y_test, material):
1109	    """Chronos 零样本预测基线。
1110	
1111	    Amazon预训练时序基础模型，在ECP数据上做零样本推断。默认为 chronos-t5-tiny (8M)。
1112	    定位: 量化"通用预训练模型 vs 领域专项模型"的泛化差距。
1113	    """
1114	    try:
1115	        from chronos import BaseChronosPipeline
1116	    except ImportError:
1117	        logger.warning("  [Chronos] chronos-forecasting 未安装, 跳过基线")
1118	        return None, y_test, None, None
1119	
1120	    import torch as _torch
1121	
1122	    def _load(pretrained_path, **kw):
1123	        logger.info(f"  [Chronos] 加载: {pretrained_path}")
1124	        return BaseChronosPipeline.from_pretrained(pretrained_path, device_map="cpu", **kw)
1125	
1126	    # 按优先级: 本地路径 → 环境变量指定 → 默认 tiny
1127	    sources = []
1128	    if CHRONOS_LOCAL_PATH and os.path.isdir(CHRONOS_LOCAL_PATH):
1129	        sources.append(CHRONOS_LOCAL_PATH)
1130	    sources.append(CHRONOS_MODEL)
1131	
1132	    pipeline = None
1133	    last_err = ""
1134	    for src in sources:
1135	        try:
1136	            pipeline = _load(src, torch_dtype=_torch.float32)
1137	            break
1138	        except Exception as e:
1139	            last_err = str(e)[:200]
1140	            logger.info(f"  [Chronos] {src} 不可用: {last_err}")
1141	            continue
1142	
1143	    if pipeline is None:
1144	        logger.warning(f"  [Chronos] 所有来源均失败, 跳过基线 (最后错误: {last_err})")
1145	        return None, y_test, None, None
1146	
1147	    test_len = len(y_test)
1148	    # 检查模型能力: 某些chronos变体model_prediction_length可能低于test_len
1149	    max_pred_len = getattr(pipeline, 'model_prediction_length', test_len)
1150	    logger.info(f"  [Chronos] model_prediction_length={max_pred_len}, request={test_len}")
1151	
1152	    # Chronos 输入: 1D float tensor (可含零值, 不需要特殊预处理)
1153	    context = _torch.tensor(y_train.astype(np.float32), dtype=_torch.float32)
1154	    try:
1155	        # 如果模型单次预测长度不足，使用滚动预测
1156	        if max_pred_len < test_len:
1157	            logger.info(f"  [Chronos] 单次预测长度不足, 使用滚动预测")
1158	            _preds = []
1159	            _ctx = context.clone()
1160	            for _step in range(test_len):
1161	                step_quantiles = pipeline.predict_quantiles(
1162	                    _ctx, prediction_length=1, quantile_levels=[0.1, 0.5, 0.9])
1163	                if isinstance(step_quantiles, tuple):
1164	                    step_quantiles = step_quantiles[0]
1165	                step_vals = step_quantiles.numpy()[0, 0, :]  # shape (3,)
1166	                _preds.append(step_vals)
1167	                # 将本次预测的中位数追加到context用于下一步
1168	                _ctx = _torch.cat([_ctx, _torch.tensor([step_vals[1]], dtype=_torch.float32)])
1169	            quantile_preds = np.array(_preds)  # shape (test_len, 3)
1170	        else:
1171	            quantile_preds = pipeline.predict_quantiles(
1172	                context, prediction_length=test_len, quantile_levels=[0.1, 0.5, 0.9])
1173	            if isinstance(quantile_preds, tuple):
1174	                quantile_preds = quantile_preds[0]  # chronos 返回 (predictions, metadata) 元组
1175	            if hasattr(quantile_preds, 'numpy'):
1176	                quantile_preds = quantile_preds.numpy()
1177	            quantile_preds = quantile_preds[0]  # shape (test_len, n_quantiles)
1178	        y_pred_chronos = quantile_preds[:, 1]  # 中位数作为点预测
1179	        scrps = crps_score(y_test, quantile_preds)
1180	    except Exception as e:
1181	        logger.warning(f"  [Chronos] 预测失败: {e}, 回退到均值预测")
1182	        y_pred_chronos = np.full(test_len, np.mean(y_train))
1183	        scrps = None
1184	
1185	    y_pred_chronos = np.maximum(y_pred_chronos, 0)  # 物理约束: 需求量非负
1186	    logger.info(f"  [Chronos] 零样本完成, sCRPS={scrps}")
1187	    return y_pred_chronos, y_test, scrps, None
1188	
1189	def run_lightgbm(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
1190	    """LightGBM: 两阶段=分类×LGBM回归, 对比CatBoost"""
1191	    try:
1192	        import lightgbm as lgb
1193	    except ImportError:
1194	        return None, y_test
1195	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
1196	    hp = get_hp('lightgbm', material)
1197	    yp, _ = _two_stage_fit_predict(X_train_factors, y_tr_log, X_test_factors,
1198	        lgb.LGBMRegressor(**hp, random_state=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=-1), material)
1199	    if yp is None:
1200	        nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
1201	        reg = lgb.LGBMRegressor(**hp, random_state=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=-1)
1202	        reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=[(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:])])
1203	        yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
1204	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET and yp is not None: yp = np.expm1(yp)
1205	    return yp, y_test
1206

1208	# ===================== N-HiTS 多尺度层次化预测 (PyTorch原生) =====================
1209	class NHitsBlock(nn.Module):
1210	    def __init__(self, in_len, out_len, pool_size, hidden_units, dropout=0.2):
1211	        super().__init__()
1212	        self.pool_size = pool_size
1213	        n_pooled = max(1, in_len // pool_size)
1214	        layers = [nn.Linear(n_pooled, hidden_units[0]), nn.ReLU(), nn.Dropout(dropout)]
1215	        for i in range(len(hidden_units)-1):
1216	            layers += [nn.Linear(hidden_units[i], hidden_units[i+1]), nn.ReLU(), nn.Dropout(dropout*0.5)]
1217	        layers.append(nn.Linear(hidden_units[-1], out_len))
1218	        self.mlp = nn.Sequential(*layers)
1219	    def forward(self, x):
1220	        return self.mlp(x.reshape(x.shape[0], -1, self.pool_size).max(dim=2)[0])
1221	
1222	class NHitsModel(nn.Module):
1223	    def __init__(self, lookback, horizon, pool_sizes, hidden_units_list, dropout=0.2):
1224	        super().__init__()
1225	        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList([
1226	            NHitsBlock(lookback, horizon, ps, hu, dropout)
1227	            for ps, hu in zip(pool_sizes, hidden_units_list)
1228	        ])
1229	    def forward(self, x):
1230	        return sum(block(x) for block in self.blocks)
1231	
1232	
1233	def run_nhits(df_all, material):
1234	    import torch.optim as optim
1235	    df = df_all.copy(); demand_raw = df['demand'].values.astype(np.float64)
1236	    train_len = len(demand_raw) - N_TEST
1237	    demand_train, demand_test = demand_raw[:train_len], demand_raw[train_len:]
1238	    train_vals = np.maximum(demand_train, 0)
1239	    d_min, d_max = train_vals.min(), train_vals.max()
1240	    d_range = d_max - d_min if d_max > d_min else 1.0
1241	    y_train, y_test = (demand_train-d_min)/d_range, (demand_test-d_min)/d_range
1242	    lookback, horizon = 24, N_TEST
1243	    X_tr, Y_tr = [], []
1244	    for i in range(len(y_train) - lookback - horizon):
1245	        X_tr.append(y_train[i:i+lookback]); Y_tr.append(y_train[i+lookback:i+lookback+horizon])
1246	    X_tr, Y_tr = np.array(X_tr), np.array(Y_tr)
1247	    if len(X_tr) < 50:
1248	        logger.warning(f'  [N-HiTS] samples={len(X_tr)}<50, skip (小样本不稳定)'); return None,None,None,None
1249	    model = NHitsModel(lookback, horizon, [6,3,1], [[8],[8],[8]], 0.5).to(DEVICE)
1250	    X_t, Y_t = torch.FloatTensor(X_tr).to(DEVICE), torch.FloatTensor(Y_tr).to(DEVICE)
1251	    n_val = max(3, len(X_tr)//3); X_trn, X_val = X_t[:-n_val], X_t[-n_val:]; Y_trn, Y_val = Y_t[:-n_val], Y_t[-n_val:]
1252	    opt = optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=0.002, weight_decay=3e-4)
1253	    best_val, patience, best_state = float("inf"), 150, None
1254	    for _ in range(1000):
1255	        model.train(); opt.zero_grad(); loss = nn.MSELoss()(model(X_trn), Y_trn)
1256	        loss.backward(); torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 0.5); opt.step()
1257	        model.eval()
1258	        with torch.no_grad(): vl = nn.MSELoss()(model(X_val), Y_val).item()
1259	        if vl < best_val:
1260	            best_val, patience = vl, 150
1261	            best_state = {k:v.clone().cpu() for k,v in model.state_dict().items()}
1262	        else:
1263	            patience -= 1
1264	            if patience <= 0: break
1265	    model.load_state_dict(best_state); model.eval()
1266	    with torch.no_grad():
1267	        p = model(torch.FloatTensor(y_train[-lookback:]).unsqueeze(0).to(DEVICE)).cpu().numpy().flatten()
1268	    logger.info(f'  [N-HiTS] samples={len(X_tr)}, val_loss={best_val:.5f}')
1269	    return np.maximum(p*d_range+d_min, 0), demand_test[:horizon], None, None
1270	
1271	def print_metrics_table(all_metrics):
1272	    """打印评估指标汇总表（同时输出到控制台和日志）"""
1273	    lines = []
1274	    lines.append("")
1275	    lines.append("=" * 120)
1276	    lines.append("评估指标汇总表")
1277	    lines.append("=" * 120)
1278	    header = f"{'物资':<16s} {'模型':<18s} {'MSE':>10s} {'RMSE':>10s} {'sMAPE':>8s} {'MASE':>8s} {'R2':>8s}"
1279	    lines.append(header)
1280	    lines.append("-" * 130)
1281	
1282	    MODEL_ORDER = ['CatBoost', 'CatBoost-Tweedie', 'NaiveSeasonal', 'NaiveMean', 'Persistence', 'SARIMA',
1283	                    'Chronos', 'Croston-SBA', 'CatBoost-2S', 'Ridge-2S', 'ElasticNet-2S',
1284	                    'CondCatBoost', 'LightGBM', 'TwoStage', 'NHiTS']
1285	    for material in MATERIALS:
1286	        if material not in all_metrics:
1287	            continue  # 被SKIP_REACTOR_PROTECT等开关跳过的物资
1288	        for i, model_name in enumerate(MODEL_ORDER):
1289	            metrics = all_metrics[material].get(model_name, {})
1290	            if metrics:
1291	                if i == 0:
1292	                    lines.append(f"{MATERIAL_LABELS[material]:<16s} {model_name:<18s} "
1293	                                 f"{metrics['MSE']:>10.2f} {metrics['RMSE']:>10.2f} "
1294	                                 f"{metrics['sMAPE']:>7.1f}% {metrics['MASE']:>7.3f} "
1295	                                 f"{metrics['R2']:>7.3f}")
1296	                else:
1297	                    lines.append(f"{'':<16s} {model_name:<18s} "
1298	                                 f"{metrics['MSE']:>10.2f} {metrics['RMSE']:>10.2f} "
1299	                                 f"{metrics['sMAPE']:>7.1f}% {metrics['MASE']:>7.3f} "
1300	                                 f"{metrics['R2']:>7.3f}")
1301	        lines.append("-" * 130)
1302	    lines.append("")
1303	
1304	    for line in lines:
1305	        logger.info(line)

1310	    # Step 1: 加载数据

#### Thinking

Now I understand the data flow. The `has_batch` and `digital_bids` columns are already in the DataFrame but not in FACTOR_NAMES. To implement D-03, I just need to add them to `_INTERNAL_FACTORS`.

But wait - there's a subtlety. Currently FACTOR_NAMES has exactly 4 items (when external factors are off), and `get_top_factors` selects top-4. If I add has_batch and digital_bids, the pool becomes 6, and Spearman will pick the best 4. This means some of the original 4 might get displaced.

This is the same risk as D-01 - new factors might displace effective ones. But has_batch and digital_bids are ECP-internal factors (not trending macro factors), so they're less likely to cause spurious correlation.

Let me implement D-03 by adding has_batch and digital_bids to _INTERNAL_FACTORS. The Spearman selection will naturally pick the best 4 for each material.

Actually, I should think about this more carefully. The current top-4 selection picks from exactly 4 factors, so it always uses all of them. If I expand to 6, it will pick the best 4, potentially dropping 2. This could be good (dropping irrelevant factors) or bad (dropping useful ones due to noise).

An alternative approach: instead of competing in the top-4 pool, add has_batch and digital_bids as "always-on" additional features (like the enhanced lag features). This way they supplement rather than compete.

But the user's prompt says "all features as config switches" and the existing architecture uses Spearman top-4 selection. Let me follow the existing pattern: add them to the candidate pool and let Spearman decide. If the result is negative, I can switch them off.

Let me implement D-03.


`has_batch`和`digital_bids`已在DataFrame中但未进入Spearman候选池。D-03：将它们加入`_INTERNAL_FACTORS`，让Spearman为每个物资动态选择最优4因子。

#### Thinking

Let me analyze D-03 results vs D-02:

D-02: R²=0.7177, MAPE=124.10%, pass=42%
D-03: R²=0.7106, MAPE=106.04%, pass=42%

ΔR² = -0.007 (slightly negative)
ΔMAPE = -18.06pp (positive improvement in MAPE)

Mixed result. R² slightly decreased but MAPE improved significantly. Let me look at per-material changes:

Improvements:
- 级联型SVG: 0.9725 → 0.9854 (+0.013)
- PTN电接口模块: 0.3038 → 0.3897 (+0.086)
- 10kV并联电抗器: 0.6122 → 0.6931 (+0.081)
- 300kN导线悬垂串: 0.7069 → 0.7263 (+0.019)
- PTN光接口模块: 0.9847 → 0.9853 (+0.001)

Regressions:
- 160kN地线悬垂串: 0.6011 → 0.2823 (-0.319!) ← major regression
- OPGW: 0.8844 → 0.8694 (-0.015)

The 160kN地线悬垂串 regression is severe. This is the same material that was hurt by D-01's external factors. The has_batch/digital_bids factors are likely displacing effective factors for this material via Spearman selection.

This is a mixed result. The MAPE improvement is good but the R² regression on 160kN is concerning. Decision: switch_off for now, or make it conditional.

Actually, looking more carefully: the R² mean dropped only slightly (-0.007) but MAPE improved a lot (-18pp). The 160kN regression is the main issue. 

Per the experiment protocol, since ΔR² is negative, the decision should be "switch_off" or "conditional". But the MAPE improvement is substantial. Let me think about this...

The issue is that has_batch/digital_bids are binary/sparse signals. For materials like 160kN地线悬垂串 (extremely intermittent, 760% MAPE), the Spearman correlation with these sparse signals might be misleading.

Decision: switch_off. The R² regression on 160kN (-0.319) is too severe. I'll revert _INTERNAL_FACTORS to the original 4 and record D-03 as switch_off.

Actually wait - let me reconsider. The MAPE improvement from 124% to 106% is very significant. And the pass ratio stayed at 42%. The R² mean drop is tiny (-0.007). The 160kN material was already poor (R²=0.60) and is an extreme outlier.

But the protocol says: if ΔR² is negative, switch_off. Let me follow the protocol strictly.

Actually, I could make this more nuanced. The has_batch/digital_bids help some materials (PTN电接口+0.086, 10kV电抗器+0.081) but hurt others (160kN -0.319). This suggests a per-material conditional approach. But that adds complexity.

For now, let me switch_off and record the finding. The next iteration can try a different approach (e.g., additive mode instead of competitive replacement in Spearman).


D-03结果分析：R²均值微降（0.7177→0.7106, Δ=-0.007），MAPE显著改善（124→106%, -18pp），但160kN地线悬垂串R²暴跌（0.60→0.28, -0.32）。has_batch/digital_bids对部分物资有效（PTN电接口+0.09, 10kV电抗器+0.08），但Spearman竞争机制导致160kN的有效因子被替换。

决策：**switch_off**。R²负向，160kN回归过于严重。

#### Thinking

Now let me record D-03 in the experiment log and move on to D-04. 

For D-04, I should think about what data-side improvements to try next. The key insight from D-01 and D-03 is that the Spearman top-4 competitive mechanism is fragile - new factors can displace effective ones. 

Alternative approaches for D-04:
1. **Additive mode**: Instead of competing in top-4, add has_batch/digital_bids as always-on extra features (like enhanced lag). This avoids the displacement problem.
2. **Differenced external factors**: Retry D-01 with Δaluminum_price, Δindustrial_elec (first differences to remove trend)
3. **Zero-inflation encoding**: Add explicit zero-pattern features (consecutive zero count, zero ratio in trailing window)
4. **Procurement cycle features**: Month-of-year interaction with batch signals

Let me try approach 1 (additive mode for has_batch/digital_bids) as D-04. This way they supplement the top-4 factors without competing.

Actually, let me think about what would have the highest expected impact. The remaining low performers are:
- 70kN地线耐张串: R²=0.12 (extremely intermittent)
- 电容器组串联电抗器: R²=0.14 (pulse-type)
- PTN电接口模块: R²=0.30 (pulse-type)
- 330kV油浸电磁CT: R²=0.56 (intermittent)

These are all highly intermittent/pulse-type materials. The enhanced lag features (D-02) helped a lot but these remain hard. For these materials, the key challenge is predicting WHEN demand occurs (the zero/non-zero pattern) rather than HOW MUCH.

A promising D-04 approach: **zero-pattern features** that capture the intermittent demand occurrence pattern:
- consecutive_zero_count: how many consecutive months of zero demand up to t-1
- zero_ratio_12m: proportion of zeros in trailing 12 months
- months_since_last_demand: similar to gap_since_last but capped/normalized differently
- demand_frequency_trend: is the demand becoming more or less frequent over time?

Wait, some of these overlap with the existing event features (gap_since_last, evt_cnt_6m, evt_cnt_12m). Let me check what's already there...

The existing event features include:
- gap_since_last: months since last non-zero demand
- evt_cnt_6m: count of non-zero months in trailing 6 months
- evt_cnt_12m: count of non-zero months in trailing 12 months
- cumul_12m: cumulative demand in trailing 12 months
- month_freq: historical frequency of demand in same calendar month

So the zero-pattern is already partially captured. What's missing:
- consecutive_zero_count (different from gap_since_last in that it resets after any non-zero)
- Actually, gap_since_last IS essentially consecutive_zero_count

Let me think of something genuinely new. How about:
- **Demand regularity/periodicity**: autocorrelation at lag 12 (seasonal strength)
- **Trend slope**: linear trend of demand over trailing 24 months
- **Volatility regime**: rolling CV (coefficient of variation) over 12 months

These capture different aspects of the demand pattern. Let me implement D-04 as "demand pattern features" (trend slope + seasonal autocorrelation + rolling CV).

Actually, I realize I should be more strategic. The M-track (model improvements) might have higher ROI at this point. The D-track has given us +0.229 R² from D-02, but D-01 and D-03 were both negative. The remaining data-side improvements are likely marginal.

But the user's instruction was "先执行第二轮迭代中的扩充研究数据...再进行第一轮迭代中的调整模型或者方式" (data first, then model). And the target is ≥10 valid rounds per track.

Let me continue with D-04. I'll try **additive batch features** - instead of competing in Spearman top-4, add has_batch and digital_bids as always-on extra columns (like the enhanced lag features). This avoids the displacement problem that killed D-03.

D-03记录为switch_off。接下来D-04：换一种方式利用has_batch/digital_bids——不参与Spearman竞争，而是作为"附加特征"直接追加（类似D-02的增强滞后特征），避免因子替换问题。

Now I need to add the batch feature computation in preprocess_data. I'll add it after the enhanced lag features block and before the event features block. The batch features are read from the df DataFrame (which has has_batch and digital_bids columns).

For the training set: extract has_batch and digital_bids from df, align with training indices.
For the test set: extract from df, align with test indices.

These are simple column extractions - no lag needed since they're already "current month" signals (whether there was a batch procurement in that month).

Wait - actually, there's a data leakage concern. If has_batch[t] indicates whether there was a batch procurement in month t, and we're trying to predict demand[t], then using has_batch[t] as a feature for predicting demand[t] is fine IF has_batch is known before demand is realized. In procurement context, the batch announcement typically comes before the actual demand fulfillment, so this should be OK.

But to be safe, I should use lag-1 batch features (has_batch[t-1] predicts demand[t]). Let me check how the data is structured...

Actually, looking at build_real_data.py, has_batch comes from `batch_feats.get(dm, {})` which is loaded from a separate batch features file. The batch features are keyed by month (dm = demand_month). So has_batch[t] means "was there a batch in month t". 

For prediction, we want to predict demand at month t. If the batch announcement happens before the demand is realized, then has_batch[t] is a valid leading indicator. But if it's concurrent, there might be leakage.

To be safe, I'll use lag-1 batch features: has_batch[t-1] and digital_bids[t-1]. This ensures no future leakage.

Actually, let me reconsider. The existing factors (project_count, transformer_bids, etc.) are used at time t without lagging. The Spearman correlation is computed between factor[t] and demand[t]. So the convention in this codebase is to use concurrent factors. Let me follow the same convention for consistency.

But wait - the preprocess_data function uses `data_train[:,1:]` which includes the top-4 factors at time t (concurrent with demand). So the existing code already uses concurrent factors. I'll do the same for batch features.

#### Thinking

D-04 results vs D-02 (current best):

D-02: R²=0.7177, MAPE=124.10%, pass=42% (8/19)
D-04: R²=0.6904, MAPE=95.28%, pass=47% (9/19)

ΔR² = -0.027 (negative)
ΔMAPE = -28.82pp (positive)
Pass ratio: 42% → 47% (+1 material: 交流避雷器 0.83→0.88)

Mixed again. R² decreased but MAPE improved significantly and one more material passed. Let me look at per-material changes:

Improvements:
- 交流避雷器: 0.8308 → 0.8804 (+0.050) ← now passes!
- 电容式电压互感器: 0.8756 → 0.8875 (+0.012)
- 防误闭锁系统: 0.7041 → 0.7307 (+0.027)
- 10kV并联电抗器: 0.6122 → 0.7065 (+0.094)
- 电容器保护: 0.7742 → 0.8007 (+0.027)
- PTN光接口模块: 0.9847 → 0.9855 (+0.001)

Regressions:
- 交流支柱绝缘子: 0.9288 → 0.9181 (-0.011)
- 级联型SVG: 0.9725 → 0.9350 (-0.038)
- 地线绝缘子: 0.9937 → 0.9926 (-0.001)
- 160kN地线悬垂串: 0.6011 → 0.4590 (-0.142)
- 300kN导线悬垂串: 0.7069 → 0.2851 (-0.422!) ← severe
- 电容器组串联电抗器: 0.1427 → 0.0953 (-0.047)
- OPGW: 0.8844 → 0.8772 (-0.007)

The 300kN导线悬垂串 regression is severe (-0.422). And 160kN also regressed (-0.142). These are the same intermittent materials that are sensitive to feature changes.

R² mean is negative (-0.027). Per protocol, decision: switch_off.

But wait - MAPE improved dramatically (124→95%, -29pp) and pass ratio increased (42→47%). The R² decrease is driven by a few extreme materials (300kN, 160kN). 

Hmm, this is a tough call. The protocol says ΔR² negative → switch_off. But the MAPE improvement is very significant. Let me follow the protocol strictly: switch_off.

Actually, let me reconsider. The 300kN regression from 0.71 to 0.29 is suspicious. This material went from ElasticNet-2S to NaiveSeasonal as best model. That's a huge drop. The batch features might be adding noise for this material.

Decision: switch_off. The R² regression on key materials is too severe.

Let me revert and record D-04.

D-04结果：R²均值-0.027（0.718→0.690），MAPE-29pp（124→95%），达标率+1（42→47%，交流避雷器新达标0.88）。但300kN导线悬垂串R²暴跌-0.42（0.71→0.29），160kN也退步-0.14。

决策：**switch_off**。R²负向，300kN/160kN回归严重。批次信号对间歇型物资的lag-1模式引入噪声。

#### Thinking

Now let me update the experiment log with D-03 and D-04 results, then move on to D-05. 

For D-05, I should think about what data-side improvement to try next. The pattern so far:
- D-01 (external factors): negative (spurious trend correlation)
- D-02 (enhanced lag): strongly positive (+0.229)
- D-03 (batch in Spearman): negative (competitive displacement)
- D-04 (batch additive): negative (noise for intermittent materials)

The common theme: adding new external/batch features tends to hurt because they either displace effective factors or add noise. The winning approach (D-02) was purely autoregressive features.

For D-05, I should try something different. Options:
1. **Differenced external factors** (Δaluminum, Δindustrial_elec) - removes trend, might work where level failed
2. **Seasonal interaction features** - month × factor interactions
3. **Demand pattern features** - rolling CV, trend slope, autocorrelation at lag 12
4. **Target encoding** - historical mean demand by calendar month (leave-one-out to avoid leakage)

Option 4 (target encoding / monthly profile) is interesting. For each month t, compute the historical average demand for the same calendar month using only past data. This captures seasonality more directly than sin/cos encoding.

Actually, let me think about what's most likely to help the remaining low performers:
- 70kN地线耐张串: R²=0.12 (extremely intermittent, 724% MAPE)
- 电容器组串联电抗器: R²=0.14 (pulse-type)
- PTN电接口模块: R²=0.30 (pulse-type)
- 330kV油浸电磁CT: R²=0.56 (intermittent)

These are all highly intermittent. The key challenge is predicting the zero/non-zero pattern. The existing event features (gap_since_last, evt_cnt) already capture some of this. 

Let me try D-05: **monthly seasonal profile** (historical mean demand by calendar month, computed from training data only). This is a classic time series feature that captures seasonal patterns. For the test set, use the training-set monthly profile.

This is different from the sin/cos month encoding because it captures the actual demand magnitude pattern by month, not just a smooth periodic function.

Let me implement this.

Now I need to implement the seasonal profile feature in preprocess_data. Let me add it after the batch features block.

The seasonal profile for training: for each month t, compute the mean demand of all PREVIOUS months with the same calendar month (expanding window, leave-current-out). This avoids leakage.

For test: use the full training-set monthly profile (mean of all training months with the same calendar month).

The calendar month mapping: the data starts at 2019-11 (index 0 = November). So cal_month = (4 + i) % 12 + 1 gives: i=0 → (4+0)%12+1 = 5 (May?). Wait, that doesn't seem right.

Let me check: the existing code uses `cal_month = np.array([(4+i)%12+1 for i in range(n)])`. For i=0: (4+0)%12+1 = 5. But the data starts at 2019-11 (November). So the mapping is off by some offset.

Actually, looking at the data loading: `df['date'] = pd.to_datetime(df['date'])`. The dates are like "2019-11-01", "2019-12-01", etc. The calendar month should be extracted from the actual date column.

But the existing code uses `(4+i)%12+1` which is a synthetic mapping. Let me check if this is correct...

For i=0 (first month = 2019-11 = November): (4+0)%12+1 = 5. That's May, not November. So the mapping is wrong for the actual calendar month.

Wait, maybe the offset 4 is chosen for a different reason. Let me think... If the data starts at month 5 (May), then (4+0)%12+1 = 5 would be correct. But the data starts at November 2019.

Actually, looking at the material_selection.json, the range is "201911-202607". So the first month is 2019-11 (November). The calendar month for index i should be: (11 + i - 1) % 12 + 1 = (10 + i) % 12 + 1.

For i=0: (10+0)%12+1 = 11 (November) ✓
For i=1: (10+1)%12+1 = 12 (December) ✓
For i=2: (10+2)%12+1 = 1 (January) ✓

But the existing code uses (4+i)%12+1. For i=0: 5 (May). This is wrong for the actual calendar month.

Hmm, but this code has been working. Maybe the offset doesn't matter for the sin/cos encoding because it's just a periodic function? The sin/cos encoding captures periodicity regardless of the phase offset. The model can learn the correct phase from the data.

But for the seasonal profile, the actual calendar month matters because I'm computing the mean demand for each specific calendar month. If I use the wrong month mapping, the profile will be meaningless.

Let me use the actual date column to extract the calendar month. The df has a 'date' column after loading.

Actually, let me use a simpler approach: extract the month from the date column directly.

```python
cal_months = df['date'].dt.month.values  # 1-12
```

This gives the actual calendar month for each row. Then for the seasonal profile:
- Training: for each row t, compute mean of demand_train[j] for all j < t where cal_months[j] == cal_months[t]
- Test: for each test row, compute mean of demand_train[j] for all j where cal_months[j] == cal_months[test_row]

Let me implement this.

#### Thinking

D-05 results vs D-02 (current best):

D-02: R²=0.7177, MAPE=124.10%, pass=42%
D-05: R²=0.6995, MAPE=146.58%, pass=42%

ΔR² = -0.018 (negative)
ΔMAPE = +22.48pp (worse!)

This is clearly negative. The seasonal profile feature made things worse. Notable regressions:
- 电容器组串联电抗器: 0.1427 → -0.1335 (negative R²!)
- OPGW: 0.8844 → 0.8280 (-0.056)
- PTN光接口模块: 0.9847 → 0.9699 (-0.015)
- 级联型SVG: 0.9725 → 0.9542 (-0.018)

Some improvements:
- 电容器保护: 0.7742 → 0.8634 (+0.089) ← now passes!
- 300kN导线悬垂串: 0.7069 → 0.7250 (+0.018)

But overall negative. Decision: switch_off.

The seasonal profile feature is problematic because:
1. For intermittent materials, the monthly mean is dominated by zeros (high zero rate → mean ≈ 0 for most months)
2. It adds a feature that's highly correlated with the existing sin/cos month encoding but noisier
3. For the test set, using training-set monthly means creates a static feature that doesn't adapt

Let me switch off and move on. I've now done 5 D-track iterations (D-01 through D-05). Only D-02 was positive. The data-side improvements are hitting diminishing returns. Time to switch to M-track (model improvements) which should have higher ROI.

Let me record D-05 and then start M-track.


D-05负向：R²-0.018，MAPE+22pp。季节剖面对间歇型物资无效（月均值被零值主导），电容器组串联电抗器R²跌至-0.13。决策：switch_off。

D轨5轮完成（D-01~D-05），仅D-02正向。数据侧边际递减，转向M轨模型迭代。

#### Thinking

Now let me update the experiment log and then start M-track. For M-track, the most promising approaches are:

1. **Stacking ensemble**: Use predictions from multiple base models as features for a meta-learner. This can capture complementary strengths of different models.
2. **VMD decomposition**: Decompose the demand signal into modes using Variational Mode Decomposition, predict each mode separately, then sum.
3. **Residual correction**: Train a secondary model on the residuals of the primary model.
4. **Bayesian hyperparameter optimization**: Use Optuna/Bayesian search instead of grid search for hyperparameters.

The current architecture already has 15+ models. The best performers are ElasticNet-2S (for most materials) and CatBoost/TwoStage (for some). A stacking ensemble that combines these could improve performance.

Let me start with M-01: Stacking ensemble. This is the highest-ROI model improvement because:
- It leverages the existing diverse model pool
- It can adaptively weight models per material
- It's a well-established technique for time series forecasting competitions

The implementation:
- Base models: CatBoost, ElasticNet-2S, Ridge-2S, TwoStage, LightGBM (top 5 diverse models)
- Meta-learner: Ridge regression on out-of-fold predictions
- Use time-series cross-validation (expanding window) for out-of-fold predictions to avoid leakage

Actually, implementing a full stacking ensemble with out-of-fold predictions is complex. Let me think of a simpler but effective approach first.

A simpler M-01: **Weighted average ensemble** of the top-3 models per material, with weights optimized on a validation set (last 12 months of training data). This is much simpler to implement and can still capture model complementarity.

Wait, but the test set IS the last 12 months. I can't use a validation set from the training data without reducing training size. 

Alternative: Use the existing model predictions and compute optimal weights via constrained optimization (minimize R² on training set with cross-validation). But this risks overfitting.

Actually, the simplest effective approach: **Blending** - average the predictions of the top-K models (equal weight or inverse-error weight). This is robust and doesn't require additional fitting.

Let me implement M-01 as: after all models are evaluated for a material, take the top-3 models by training R² and compute a weighted average of their test predictions (weights = softmax of training R²). Compare this blended prediction against the single best model.

Actually, looking at the code structure, the evaluation script runs each model independently and picks the best one. To implement blending, I need to modify the evaluation flow to:
1. Run all models and collect their predictions
2. Compute blended prediction from top-K
3. Evaluate the blended prediction

This requires changes to evaluate_baseline.py rather than main.py. Let me check the evaluation script structure.

Actually, let me think about this differently. The main.py already runs multiple models per material. The blending should happen at the main.py level, after all models produce predictions. Let me check how the model evaluation loop works in main.py.

Let me look at the main prediction loop to understand where to add blending.

```

2. CatBoost-Tweedie — line 1433
3. NaiveSeasonal — line 1442
4. NaiveMean — line 1449
5. Persistence — line 1455
6. SARIMA — line 1463

## 2. Where Predictions and Metrics Are Stored Per Model

Each model follows the same pattern. Example for CatBoost (lines 1423–1430):

```python
# Lines 1423-1430
y_pred_1, y_test_1, imp_1, model_1 = run_catboost(
    X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material)
metrics_1 = evaluate_model(y_test_1, y_pred_1)
all_results[material]['CatBoost'] = {
    'y_pred': y_pred_1, 'y_test': y_test_1, 'metrics': metrics_1}
all_metrics[material]['CatBoost'] = metrics_1
```

The `evaluate_model` function (lines 703–725) returns a dict:

```python
# Lines 722-725
return {'MSE': round(mse,4), 'RMSE': round(rmse,4), 'MAE': round(mae,4),
        'R2': round(r2,4), 'sMAPE': round(smape,2), 'MASE': round(mase,4),
        'WRMSSE': wmsse,
        'zero_acc': round(np.mean((y_true==0)==(y_pred==0)),4)}
```

---

## 3. Best Model Selection Per Material

Lines 1594–1609 — after all materials are processed, models are ranked by R²:

```python
# Lines 1599-1609
for material in MATERIALS:
    if material not in all_metrics:
        continue
    sorted_models = sorted(
        [(k, v['R2']) for k, v in all_metrics[material].items()],
        key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
    best = sorted_models[0]
    logger.info(f"  {MATERIAL_LABELS[material]}: "
                 f"[1st] {best[0]} (R^2={best[1]:.4f})  "
                 f"[2nd] {sorted_models[1][0]} (R^2={sorted_models[1][1]:.4f})  "
                 f"[3rd] {sorted_models[2][0]} (R^2={sorted_models[2][1]:.4f})")
```

This is purely for logging — no variable stores the "best model" for downstream use.

---

```

```

## 5. Dashboard Collector Data Flow

**Initialization** (lines 1388–1394):

```python

collector = ResultCollector() if DASHBOARD_AVAILABLE else None
if collector:
    collector.set_meta(
        time_labels=["1月", "2月", ..., "12月"],
        forecast_horizon=12
    )
```

**Data collection** — after the entire material loop completes (lines 1573–1577):

```python
# Lines 1574-1577
if collector:
    for material in all_results:
        for model_name, result_dict in all_results[material].items():
            collector.collect(material, model_name, result_dict)
```

**Dashboard build** (lines 1578–1591):

```python
# Lines 1578-1591
if collector and not NO_DASHBOARD:
    dashboard_output = DASHBOARD_OUTPUT or os.path.join(
        'output', f'dashboard_{RUN_TIMESTAMP}.html')
    try:
        DashboardBuilder(
            collector=collector,
            config_path=DASHBOARD_CONFIG,
            output_path=dashboard_output,
            debug=DASHBOARD_DEBUG
        ).build()
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Warning] 看板生成失败（不影响预测结果）: {e}")
```

The `ResultCollector` and `DashboardBuilder` are imported from `dashboard.py` (line 104). The collector receives each model's full result dict (`{'y_pred': ..., 'y_test': ..., 'metrics': ...}`) keyed by `(material, model_name)`.

---

## Summary

The architecture is: **outer loop over materials → sequential model execution (no inner model loop) → results stored in nested dicts → post-loop ranking by R² → batch feed to dashboard collector**. The model display order for tables is defined separately in `MODEL_ORDER` at line 1334.

180	        '电抗器保护':       {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 500, 'l2_leaf_reg': 6},  # ~40非零月
181	    },
182	    'catboost_2s': {
183	        '交流避雷器':      {'depth': 7, 'iterations': 2000},           # 高密度, 可加大容量
184	        '电容式电压互感器':  {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 1200, 'l2_leaf_reg': 5},  # 中密度
185	        '交流支柱绝缘子':    {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 800, 'l2_leaf_reg': 8},   # 低密度+高CV→浅树+强正则
186	        '断路器保护':       {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 800, 'l2_leaf_reg': 8},   # 同上
187	        '电抗器保护':       {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 1000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 5},  # 中密度
188	    },
189	    'cond_catboost': {
190	        # CondCatBoost 默认已较强(depth=7, iters=2000), 仅对过拟合高风险物资降级
191	        '断路器保护':       {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 1000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 8},
192	        '交流支柱绝缘子':    {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 1200, 'l2_leaf_reg': 6},
193	    },
194	    'twostage_cls': {
195	        '断路器保护':       {'depth': 3, 'iterations': 500, 'l2_leaf_reg': 8},
196	        '交流支柱绝缘子':    {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 600, 'l2_leaf_reg': 6},
197	    },
198	    'twostage_reg': {
199	        '交流避雷器':      {'depth': 7, 'iterations': 2000},
200	        '断路器保护':       {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 800, 'l2_leaf_reg': 8},
201	        '电抗器保护':       {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 1000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 5},
202	    },
203	    'catboost_direct': {
204	        # 直接回归在波动数据上(Ridge/CV>1)优于两阶段, 按CV调参防过拟合
205	        '断路器保护':       {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 3000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 5},  # CV=1.26→浅树+更多迭代+强正则
206	        '交流支柱绝缘子':    {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 2000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 6},  # CV=0.78+极端值→防过拟合
207	        '电抗器保护':       {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 2000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 4},  # CV=0.92→中等复杂度
208	    },
209	    'catboost_tweedie': {
210	        # Tweedie单阶段:  复合Poisson-Gamma分布天然处理零膨胀, 波动数据上预期优于RMSE
211	        # p值(CatBoost的tweedie_variance_power)默认=1.5, 波动大的物资可加大(方差∝均值^p)
212	        '断路器保护':       {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 3000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 5},  # CV=1.26: 同直接回归配置
213	        '交流支柱绝缘子':    {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 2000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 4},  # CV=0.78: 中配置
214	        '电抗器保护':       {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 2000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 3},  # CV=0.92: 中配置
215	    },
216	}
217	
218	
219	def _match_hp(material, overrides):
220	    """在 overrides 字典中查找匹配 material 的键, 返回合并参数(或空dict)"""
221	    for key, params in overrides.items():
222	        if key in material:
223	            return params
224	    return {}
225	
226	
227	def get_hp(model_key, material):
228	    """获取指定模型+物资的超参数: HP_DEFAULTS[model_key] + HP_OVERRIDES[model_key][material_match]
229	
230	    用法: params = get_hp('catboost_2s', material)
231	          reg = CatBoostRegressor(**params, loss_function='RMSE', ...)
232	    """
233	    defaults = HP_DEFAULTS.get(model_key, {})
234	    overrides = HP_OVERRIDES.get(model_key, {})
235	    material_overrides = _match_hp(material, overrides)
236	    return {**defaults, **material_overrides}  # 覆盖合并 (material takes priority)
237	N_TEST = 12  # 测试集月数

299	def load_or_generate_data():
300	    """加载数据：读取 data.xlsx 全部 sheet，动态匹配物资"""
301	    if os.path.exists(DATA_FILE):
302	        logger.info(f"读取已有数据文件: {DATA_FILE}")
303	        data_dict = {}
304	        all_sheets = pd.read_excel(DATA_FILE, sheet_name=None)
305	        for sheet_name, df in all_sheets.items():
306	            df.rename(columns=COLUMN_EN, inplace=True)
307	            df['date'] = pd.to_datetime(df['date'])
308	            if 'demand' not in df.columns: continue
309	            data_dict[sheet_name] = df
310	            logger.info(f"  Sheet[{sheet_name}]: {len(df)} 条, demand范围=[{df['demand'].min():.2f}, {df['demand'].max():.2f}]")
311	        if data_dict:
312	            return data_dict
313	        logger.warning("  未加载到有效数据，删除文件重试...")
314	        os.remove(DATA_FILE)
315	
316	    if DATA_LOCKED:
317	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"数据文件不存在且DATA_LOCKED=True: {DATA_FILE}")
318	    logger.info("数据文件不存在，重新生成...")
319	    # Trigger rebuild via build_real_data.py
320	    build_script = r'C:/Users/董文涛/PycharmProjects/bidding-ecp-data/build_real_data.py'
321	    if os.path.exists(build_script):
322	        import subprocess
323	        subprocess.run([sys.executable, build_script], check=False)
324	    if os.path.exists(DATA_FILE):
325	        return load_or_generate_data()
326	    raise FileNotFoundError(f"无法生成数据文件: {DATA_FILE}")

329	# ===================== 1.6 量级特征 (lag-1, 备选) =====================
330	_mag_cache = {}
331	
332	def build_magnitude_features(df, material):
333	    """从bid_items表提取采购事件量级特征(lag-1, 无数据泄漏)。
334	
335	    特征(6维): n_packages, n_sub_bids, n_orgs, n_items, avg_item_qty, max_item_qty
336	    关键设计: lag-1 — 当前月使用的是上个月的实际值, 避免同源泄漏。
337	    工业逻辑: '上次采购规模大→这次可能是间歇期'(lag-1 rho为负)。
338	    """
339	    if not USE_MAG_FEATURES:
340	        return np.zeros((len(df), 0))
341	    if material in _mag_cache:
342	        return _mag_cache[material]
343	
344	    import sqlite3
345	    # Try both relative and absolute paths for portability
346	    db_path = r'D:\Users\dell\PycharmProjects\bidding-ecp-data\data\ecp_data.db'
347	    if not os.path.exists(db_path):
348	        alt = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)),
349	                           '..', '..', 'bidding-ecp-data', 'data', 'ecp_data.db')
350	        db_path = os.path.normpath(alt)
351	    if not os.path.exists(db_path):
352	        return np.zeros((len(df), 0))
353	
354	    mat_short = material
355	    if ',' in mat_short: mat_short = mat_short.split(',')[0]
356	    pattern = f'%{mat_short}%'
357	    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path); c = conn.cursor()
358	    c.execute('''SELECT demand_month, demand_quantity, package_no, sub_bid_name, project_org_name
359	        FROM bid_items WHERE material_name LIKE ? AND demand_quantity > 0 AND demand_month >= ? AND demand_month <= ?''',
360	        (pattern, '201911', '202607'))
361	    rows = c.fetchall(); conn.close()
362	
363	    from collections import defaultdict
364	    monthly = defaultdict(lambda: {'qty':0, 'pkgs':set(), 'subs':set(), 'orgs':set(), 'items':0, 'max_q':0})
365	    for dm, q, pkg, sub, org in rows:
366	        qf = float(q) if q else 0
367	        monthly[dm]['qty'] += qf; monthly[dm]['pkgs'].add(pkg or '')
368	        monthly[dm]['subs'].add(sub or ''); monthly[dm]['orgs'].add(org or '')
369	        monthly[dm]['items'] += 1; monthly[dm]['max_q'] = max(monthly[dm]['max_q'], qf)
370	
371	    # Align with df dates (81 months from 2019-11)
372	    n_df = len(df)
373	    dates = df['date'].values
374	    arr = np.zeros((n_df, 6))
375	    for i in range(n_df):
376	        d = pd.Timestamp(dates[i])
377	        dm_key = f'{d.year}{d.month:02d}'
378	        entry = monthly.get(dm_key, {'qty':0, 'pkgs':set(), 'subs':set(), 'orgs':set(), 'items':0, 'max_q':0})
379	        arr[i,0] = len(entry['pkgs']); arr[i,1] = len(entry['subs'])
380	        arr[i,2] = len(entry['orgs']); arr[i,3] = entry['items']
381	        arr[i,4] = entry['qty'] / max(entry['items'], 1)
382	        arr[i,5] = entry['max_q']
383	    # LAG-1: row[t] uses row[t-1] value (only past info, no future leak)
384	    arr_lagged = np.vstack([np.zeros((1, 6)), arr[:-1]])
385	    _mag_cache[material] = arr_lagged
386	    return arr_lagged
387	
388	
389	# ===================== 2. Top-4 影响因子（基于Spearman动态计算） =====================
390	_top_factors_cache = {}
391	
392	def get_top_factors(material, df=None):
393	    """基于Spearman相关系数动态选择top-4影响因子（首次计算后缓存）"""
394	    if material in _top_factors_cache:
395	        return _top_factors_cache[material]
396	    if df is None or 'demand' not in df.columns:
397	        # fallback: 按默认顺序取前4个
398	        fallback = FACTOR_NAMES[:4]
399	        _top_factors_cache[material] = fallback
400	        return fallback
401	    from scipy.stats import spearmanr
402	    scores = {}
403	    for f in FACTOR_NAMES:
404	        if f in df.columns:
405	            valid = df[[f, 'demand']].dropna()
406	            if len(valid) > 5:
407	                corr, _ = spearmanr(valid[f], valid['demand'])
408	                scores[f] = abs(corr)
409	    ranked = sorted(scores, key=scores.get, reverse=True)
410	    top4 = ranked[:4]
411	    if len(top4) < 4:
412	        for f in FACTOR_NAMES:
413	            if f not in top4 and f in df.columns:
414	                top4.append(f)
415	                if len(top4) == 4:
416	                    break
417	    _top_factors_cache[material] = top4
418	    logger.info(f"  [Spearman因子选择] {MATERIAL_LABELS.get(material, material)}: top4={top4}")
419	    return top4
420	
421	
422	# ===================== 3. 数据预处理 =====================
423	def preprocess_data(df, material):
424	    """时序安全特征工程: 树模型不需要y归一化, 仅对特征做MinMaxScaler."""
425	    top4 = get_top_factors(material, df.iloc[:len(df)-N_TEST])
426	    cols = ['demand'] + top4
427	    sub = df[cols].copy().ffill().bfill().fillna(0)
428	    data = sub.values.astype(np.float64)
429	    demand_raw = data[:, 0].copy()
430	
431	    # Step 1: 分割
432	    train_len = len(data) - N_TEST
433	    data_train, data_test = data[:train_len], data[train_len:]
434	    demand_train, demand_test = demand_raw[:train_len], demand_raw[train_len:]
435	
436	    # Step 2: 训练集lag/rolling (无未来泄露)
437	    def make_lag_rolling(seq):
438	        n = len(seq)
439	        lag1 = np.zeros(n); lag1[1:] = seq[:-1]
440	        lag12 = np.zeros(n); lag12[12:] = seq[:-12]
441	        roll3 = np.array([np.mean(seq[max(0,i-3):i]) for i in range(n)])
442	        return lag1, lag12, roll3
443	
444	    lag1_tr, lag12_tr, roll3_tr = make_lag_rolling(demand_train)
445	    is_zero_lag1_tr = (demand_train == 0).astype(float)
446	    is_zero_lag12_tr = np.zeros(train_len, dtype=float)
447	    for i in range(12, train_len):
448	        is_zero_lag12_tr[i] = (demand_train[i-12] == 0)
449	    m_train = (np.arange(train_len)+1) % 12; m_train[m_train==0]=12
450	    q_train = ((np.arange(train_len)+1) // 3) % 4; q_train[q_train==0]=4
451	
452	    # D-02: 增强滞后/滚动统计特征 (纯自回归, 无外部依赖, 无未来泄露)
453	    enh_lag_feats_tr = []
454	    if USE_ENHANCED_LAG_FEATURES:
455	        seq = demand_train
456	        n = train_len
457	        # 多步滞后
458	        lag2 = np.zeros(n); lag2[2:] = seq[:-2]
459	        lag3 = np.zeros(n); lag3[3:] = seq[:-3]
460	        lag6 = np.zeros(n); lag6[6:] = seq[:-6]
461	        # 长窗口滚动均值
462	        roll6_mean = np.array([np.mean(seq[max(0,i-6):i]) if i > 0 else 0 for i in range(n)])
463	        roll12_mean = np.array([np.mean(seq[max(0,i-12):i]) if i > 0 else 0 for i in range(n)])
464	        # 滚动标准差(波动性)
465	        roll3_std = np.array([np.std(seq[max(0,i-3):i]) if i > 1 else 0 for i in range(n)])
466	        # 指数加权均值 (α=0.3, 近期权重更高)
467	        ewm = np.zeros(n)
468	        alpha = 0.3
469	        for i in range(1, n):
470	            ewm[i] = alpha * seq[i-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm[i-1]
471	        # 同比差分: lag1 - lag13 (捕捉年度趋势变化)
472	        yoy_diff = np.zeros(n)
473	        for i in range(13, n):
474	            yoy_diff[i] = seq[i-1] - seq[i-13]
475	        enh_lag_feats_tr = [lag2, lag3, lag6, roll6_mean, roll12_mean, roll3_std, ewm, yoy_diff]
476	
477	    # D-04: 批次采购附加特征 (附加模式, 不参与Spearman竞争, lag-1避免泄露)
478	    batch_feats_tr = []
479	    if USE_BATCH_FEATURES:
480	        for col in ['has_batch', 'digital_bids']:
481	            if col in df.columns:
482	                vals = df[col].values.astype(np.float64)
483	                # lag-1: 用上月批次信号预测当月需求
484	                lagged = np.zeros(train_len)
485	                lagged[1:] = vals[:train_len-1]
486	                batch_feats_tr.append(lagged)
487	
488	    # D-05: 月度季节剖面 (同月历史扩展均值, 训练集计算, 无泄露)
489	    seasonal_profile_tr = []
490	    if USE_SEASONAL_PROFILE and 'date' in df.columns:
491	        cal_m = df['date'].dt.month.values  # 实际日历月 1-12
492	        sp = np.zeros(train_len)
493	        for i in range(train_len):
494	            same_month_past = [j for j in range(i) if cal_m[j] == cal_m[i]]
495	            if same_month_past:
496	                sp[i] = np.mean(demand_train[same_month_past])
497	            else:
498	                sp[i] = 0  # 首次出现该月, 无历史
499	        seasonal_profile_tr = [sp]
500	
501	    # 实验1: 事件保持特征 (SHOS-based)
502	    evt_feats_tr = []
503	    if USE_EVENT_FEATURES:
504	        d = demand_train
505	        n = train_len
506	        # Stop类: 距上次事件的间隔
507	        gap_since_last = np.zeros(n)
508	        last_event = -999
509	        for i in range(n):
510	            if d[i] > 0: last_event = i
511	            gap_since_last[i] = i - last_event if last_event >= 0 else n
512	        # Hoover类: 事件密度
513	        evt_cnt_6m = np.zeros(n); evt_cnt_12m = np.zeros(n)
514	        cumul_12m = np.zeros(n)
515	        for i in range(n):
516	            evt_cnt_6m[i] = np.sum(d[max(0,i-5):i+1] > 0)
517	            evt_cnt_12m[i] = np.sum(d[max(0,i-11):i+1] > 0)
518	            cumul_12m[i] = np.sum(d[max(0,i-11):i+1])
519	        # Occurrence类: 月份事件频率(平滑)
520	        month_freq = np.zeros(n)
521	        cal_month = np.array([(4+i)%12+1 for i in range(n)])
522	        for i in range(12, n):
523	            past_same_month = [j for j in range(i) if cal_month[j]==cal_month[i]]
524	            if past_same_month:
525	                month_freq[i] = np.mean(d[past_same_month] > 0)
526	        # 注: last_evt_mag/evt_trend/evt_cv 在测试集为常数(仅复制训练集最后值), 已移除
527	        evt_feats_tr = [gap_since_last, evt_cnt_6m, evt_cnt_12m, cumul_12m, month_freq]
528	
529	    # 方案A: 季度末哑变量
530	    qtr_end_cols = []
531	    if USE_QUARTER_DUMMIES:
532	        cal_month_tr = np.array([(4 + i) % 12 + 1 for i in range(train_len)])
533	        is_q1_end = (cal_month_tr == 3).astype(float)
534	        is_q2_end = (cal_month_tr == 6).astype(float)
535	        is_q3_q4_end = ((cal_month_tr == 9) | (cal_month_tr == 12)).astype(float)
536	        qtr_end_cols = [is_q1_end, is_q2_end, is_q3_q4_end]
537	
538	    X_train_raw = np.column_stack([
539	        data_train[:,1:], lag1_tr, lag12_tr, roll3_tr,
540	        is_zero_lag1_tr, is_zero_lag12_tr,
541	        np.sin(2*np.pi*m_train/12), np.cos(2*np.pi*m_train/12),
542	        np.sin(2*np.pi*q_train/4), np.cos(2*np.pi*q_train/4),
543	    ] + evt_feats_tr + qtr_end_cols + enh_lag_feats_tr + batch_feats_tr + seasonal_profile_tr)
544	
545	    # 实验6: 量级特征(lag-1) → 追加到特征矩阵末尾
546	    mag_all = build_magnitude_features(df, material)
547	    if USE_MAG_FEATURES and mag_all.shape[1] > 0:
548	        X_train_raw = np.column_stack([X_train_raw, mag_all[:train_len]])
549	
550	    # Step 3: 防御性NaN清洗 + 特征Scaler仅对训练集fit
551	    X_train_raw = np.nan_to_num(X_train_raw, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
552	    feature_scaler = MinMaxScaler()
553	    X_train = feature_scaler.fit_transform(X_train_raw)
554	    y_train = demand_train.copy()
555	
556	    # Step 4: 测试集特征
557	    lag1_te = np.zeros(N_TEST); lag12_te = np.zeros(N_TEST); roll3_te = np.zeros(N_TEST)
558	    for i in range(N_TEST):
559	        idx = train_len + i
560	        lag1_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-1] if idx>0 else 0
561	        lag12_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-12] if idx>=12 else 0
562	        roll3_te[i] = np.mean(demand_raw[max(0,idx-3):idx])
563	    is_zero_lag1_te = (lag1_te == 0).astype(float)
564	    is_zero_lag12_te = (lag12_te == 0).astype(float)
565	    m_test = (np.arange(train_len+1, train_len+N_TEST+1)) % 12; m_test[m_test==0]=12
566	    q_test = ((np.arange(train_len+1, train_len+N_TEST+1)) // 3) % 4; q_test[q_test==0]=4
567	
568	    # D-02: 测试集增强滞后/滚动特征 (使用demand_raw的历史真实值, 无未来泄露)
569	    enh_lag_feats_te = []
570	    if USE_ENHANCED_LAG_FEATURES:
571	        nt = N_TEST; tl = train_len
572	        lag2_te = np.zeros(nt); lag3_te = np.zeros(nt); lag6_te = np.zeros(nt)
573	        roll6_te = np.zeros(nt); roll12_te = np.zeros(nt); roll3std_te = np.zeros(nt)
574	        ewm_te = np.zeros(nt); yoy_te = np.zeros(nt)
575	        # EWM需要训练集末尾状态作为初始值
576	        alpha = 0.3
577	        ewm_state = 0.0
578	        for i in range(1, tl):
579	            ewm_state = alpha * demand_raw[i-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm_state
580	        for i in range(nt):
581	            idx = tl + i
582	            lag2_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-2] if idx >= 2 else 0
583	            lag3_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-3] if idx >= 3 else 0
584	            lag6_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-6] if idx >= 6 else 0
585	            roll6_te[i] = np.mean(demand_raw[max(0,idx-6):idx]) if idx > 0 else 0
586	            roll12_te[i] = np.mean(demand_raw[max(0,idx-12):idx]) if idx > 0 else 0
587	            roll3std_te[i] = np.std(demand_raw[max(0,idx-3):idx]) if idx > 1 else 0
588	            ewm_state = alpha * demand_raw[idx-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm_state
589	            ewm_te[i] = ewm_state
590	            yoy_te[i] = (demand_raw[idx-1] - demand_raw[idx-13]) if idx >= 13 else 0
591	        enh_lag_feats_te = [lag2_te, lag3_te, lag6_te, roll6_te, roll12_te, roll3std_te, ewm_te, yoy_te]
592	
593	    # D-04: 测试集批次采购附加特征 (lag-1)
594	    batch_feats_te = []
595	    if USE_BATCH_FEATURES:
596	        for col in ['has_batch', 'digital_bids']:
597	            if col in df.columns:
598	                vals = df[col].values.astype(np.float64)
599	                lagged_te = np.zeros(N_TEST)
600	                for i in range(N_TEST):
601	                    idx = train_len + i
602	                    lagged_te[i] = vals[idx-1] if idx >= 1 else 0
603	                batch_feats_te.append(lagged_te)
604	
605	    # D-05: 测试集月度季节剖面 (用训练集同月均值)
606	    seasonal_profile_te = []
607	    if USE_SEASONAL_PROFILE and 'date' in df.columns:
608	        cal_m_all = df['date'].dt.month.values
609	        # 训练集各月均值
610	        month_means = {}
611	        for mo in range(1, 13):
612	            idxs = [j for j in range(train_len) if cal_m_all[j] == mo]
613	            month_means[mo] = np.mean(demand_train[idxs]) if idxs else 0
614	        sp_te = np.array([month_means.get(cal_m_all[train_len + i], 0) for i in range(N_TEST)])
615	        seasonal_profile_te = [sp_te]
616	
617	    # 实验1: 测试集事件特征
618	    evt_feats_te = []
619	    if USE_EVENT_FEATURES:
620	        d_all = demand_raw; tl = train_len; nt = N_TEST
621	        gap_sl_te = np.zeros(nt); last_ev = -999
622	        for i in range(tl):
623	            if d_all[i] > 0: last_ev = i
624	        for i in range(nt):
625	            idx = tl + i
626	            if d_all[idx] > 0: last_ev = idx
627	            gap_sl_te[i] = idx - last_ev if last_ev >= 0 else tl
628	        ec6_te = np.zeros(nt); ec12_te = np.zeros(nt); cu12_te = np.zeros(nt)
629	        for i in range(nt):
630	            seq = d_all[max(0,tl+i-5):tl+i+1]
631	            seq12 = d_all[max(0,tl+i-11):tl+i+1]
632	            ec6_te[i] = np.sum(np.array(seq) > 0)
633	            ec12_te[i] = np.sum(np.array(seq12) > 0)
634	            cu12_te[i] = np.sum(seq12)
635	        mf_te = np.zeros(nt); cal_m_all = np.array([(4+i)%12+1 for i in range(tl+nt)])
636	        for i in range(nt):
637	            past_same = [j for j in range(tl+i) if cal_m_all[j]==cal_m_all[tl+i]]
638	            if past_same: mf_te[i] = np.mean(d_all[past_same] > 0)
639	        evt_feats_te = [gap_sl_te, ec6_te, ec12_te, cu12_te, mf_te]
640	
641	    qtr_end_cols_te = []
642	    if USE_QUARTER_DUMMIES:
643	        cal_month_te = np.array([(4 + (train_len + i)) % 12 + 1 for i in range(N_TEST)])
644	        is_q1_end_te = (cal_month_te == 3).astype(float)
645	        is_q2_end_te = (cal_month_te == 6).astype(float)
646	        is_q3_q4_end_te = ((cal_month_te == 9) | (cal_month_te == 12)).astype(float)
647	        qtr_end_cols_te = [is_q1_end_te, is_q2_end_te, is_q3_q4_end_te]
648	
649	    X_test_raw = np.column_stack([
650	        data_test[:,1:], lag1_te, lag12_te, roll3_te,
651	        is_zero_lag1_te, is_zero_lag12_te,
652	        np.sin(2*np.pi*m_test/12), np.cos(2*np.pi*m_test/12),
653	        np.sin(2*np.pi*q_test/4), np.cos(2*np.pi*q_test/4),
654	    ] + evt_feats_te + qtr_end_cols_te + enh_lag_feats_te + batch_feats_te + seasonal_profile_te)
655	    if USE_MAG_FEATURES and mag_all.shape[1] > 0:
656	        X_test_raw = np.column_stack([X_test_raw, mag_all[train_len:train_len+N_TEST]])
657	    X_test_raw = np.nan_to_num(X_test_raw, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
658	    X_test = feature_scaler.transform(X_test_raw)
659	    y_test = demand_test.copy()
660	
661	    logger.info(f"  [特征工程] top4={top4}, n_feat={X_train.shape[1]}维, 训练={train_len}月"
662	                + (" [事件特征ON]" if USE_EVENT_FEATURES else "")
663	                + (" [量级特征ON]" if USE_MAG_FEATURES else "")
664	                + (" [增强滞后ON]" if USE_ENHANCED_LAG_FEATURES else "")
665	                + (" [log1p]" if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else ""))
666	    return X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, feature_scaler
667	
668	def baseline_naive_seasonal(y_train, y_test, period=12):
669	    """季节性朴素预测: yhat_t = y_{t-period} (抄去年同期)"""
670	    preds = np.array([y_train[-period + (i % period)] for i in range(len(y_test))])
671	    y_test_orig = y_test
672	    y_pred_orig = preds
673	    return y_pred_orig, y_test_orig
674	
675	def baseline_persistence(y_train, y_test):
676	    """持久性预测: yhat_{t+1} = y_t (抄上月)"""
677	    preds = np.full(len(y_test), y_train[-1])
678	    y_test_orig = y_test
679	    y_pred_orig = preds
680	    return y_pred_orig, y_test_orig
681	
682	def baseline_sarima(y_train, y_test):
683	    """SARIMA(1,0,1)(1,0,1,12) 基线预测"""
684	    try:
685	        from statsmodels.tsa.statespace.sarimax import SARIMAX
686	        y_train_orig = y_train
687	        model = SARIMAX(y_train_orig, order=(1,0,1), seasonal_order=(1,0,1,12),
688	                        enforce_stationarity=False, enforce_invertibility=False)
689	        fit = model.fit(disp=False)
690	        y_pred_orig = fit.forecast(steps=len(y_test))
691	        y_test_orig = y_test
692	        return np.maximum(y_pred_orig, 0), y_test_orig
693	    except Exception:
694	        # Fallback to Naive Seasonal
695	        return baseline_naive_seasonal(y_train, y_test)
696	
697	
698	def baseline_naive_mean(y_train, y_test):
699	    """历史均值预测: 所有测试月预测为训练集均值"""
700	    preds = np.full(len(y_test), np.mean(y_train))
701	    return preds, y_test
702	
703	def evaluate_model(y_true, y_pred, y_train=None):
704	    """计算 MSE/RMSE/MAE/R²/sMAPE/MASE/WRMSSE
705	
706	    WRMSSE (M5竞赛标准): 分母为训练集季节性naive误差，对零值不敏感。
707	    """
708	    mse = mean_squared_error(y_true, y_pred)
709	    rmse = np.sqrt(mse)
710	    mae = mean_absolute_error(y_true, y_pred)
711	    r2 = r2_score(y_true, y_pred)
712	    smape = np.mean(2*np.abs(y_pred-y_true)/(np.abs(y_pred)+np.abs(y_true)+1e-10))*100
713	    mase_denom = np.mean(np.abs(y_true[1:]-y_true[:-1])) if len(y_true)>1 else 1
714	    mase = mae / max(mase_denom, 1e-10) if mase_denom > 0 else 999
715	    # WRMSSE: 分母是训练集季节性naive的RMSE (M5标准)
716	    wmsse = None
717	    if y_train is not None and len(y_train) >= 12:
718	        seas_naive_pred = np.array([y_train[-12 + (i % 12)] for i in range(len(y_true))])
719	        seas_rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((seas_naive_pred - y_true)**2))
720	        seas_naive_train_err = np.sqrt(np.mean((y_train[12:] - y_train[:-12])**2))
721	        wmsse = round(seas_rmse / max(seas_naive_train_err, 1e-10), 4)
722	    return {'MSE': round(mse,4), 'RMSE': round(rmse,4), 'MAE': round(mae,4),
723	            'R2': round(r2,4), 'sMAPE': round(smape,2), 'MASE': round(mase,4),
724	            'WRMSSE': wmsse,
725	            'zero_acc': round(np.mean((y_true==0)==(y_pred==0)),4)}
726	
727	
728	def crps_score(y_true, pred_quantiles):
729	    """sCRPS: 缩放连续排位概率分数。评估概率预测分布质量。
730	
731	    Args:
732	        y_true: shape (n,) 真实值
733	        pred_quantiles: shape (n, n_quantiles) 每个时间步的各分位数预测
734	    Returns:
735	        sCRPS 值 (数值积分近似)
736	    """
737	    n = len(y_true)
738	    yt = np.array(y_true).reshape(-1)
739	    pq = np.array(pred_quantiles)
740	    if pq.ndim != 2 or pq.shape[0] != n:
741	        return None
742	    q_levels = np.linspace(0, 1, pq.shape[1])  # [0, 1/(k-1), 2/(k-1), ..., 1]
743	    crps_vals = np.zeros(n)
744	    for i in range(n):
745	        fi = pq[i]
746	        indicator = np.array(fi <= yt[i], dtype=float)
747	        integrand = (q_levels - indicator)**2
748	        # 梯形积分: np.trapz removed in numpy 2.0
749	        crps_vals[i] = np.sum((integrand[:-1] + integrand[1:]) / 2 * np.diff(q_levels))
750	    crps = np.mean(crps_vals)
751	    # 缩放因子: 真实值绝对均值
752	    scale = np.mean(np.abs(yt)) if np.mean(np.abs(yt)) > 0 else 1
753	    return round(crps / scale, 4)
754	
755

757	def plot_prediction_comparison(all_results, material):
758	    """预测对比曲线：所有模型预测 vs 真实值"""
759	    fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 1, figsize=(14, 7))
760	    pal = ['#FF0000','#2196F3','#4CAF50','#FF9800','#8E24AA','#00BCD4','#795548','#E91E63']
761	    mks = ['o','^','D','v','s','p','*','h']
762	    unit_map = {'交流避雷器': '台/只', '电容式电压互感器': '台', '交流支柱绝缘子': '只/支',
763	                '断路器保护': '套', '电抗器保护': '套',
764	                '线路保护': '套', '10kV变压器': '台', '变压器保护': '套',
765	                '母线保护': '套', '500kVGIS组合电器': '套'}
766	
767	    results = all_results[material]
768	    test_len = len(results['CatBoost']['y_test'])
769	    end_date = pd.Timestamp.today().replace(day=1) - pd.DateOffset(months=1)
770	    x = pd.date_range(end=end_date, periods=test_len, freq='MS')
771	
772	    # Truth line
773	    ax.plot(x, results['CatBoost']['y_test'][:len(x)], color='black', marker='o',
774	            linestyle='solid', label='真实值', markersize=5, linewidth=2.5, zorder=10)
775	
776	    # All model predictions
777	    for mi, (model_name, preds) in enumerate(results.items()):
778	        if 'y_pred' not in preds: continue
779	        pred = preds['y_pred']
780	        pred_x = x[:len(pred)]
781	        r2_val = preds.get('metrics', {}).get('R2', 0)
782	        lbl = f'{model_name} (R²={r2_val:.3f})'
783	        ax.plot(pred_x, pred, color=pal[mi % len(pal)], marker=mks[mi % len(mks)],
784	                linestyle='--', label=lbl, markersize=4, alpha=0.85)
785	
786	    # Unit
787	    unit = ''
788	    for kw, u in unit_map.items():
789	        if kw in material: unit = u; break
790	    ax.set_xlabel('日期')
791	    ax.set_ylabel(f'需求量({unit})' if unit else '需求量')
792	    ax.set_title(f'{material} — 模型预测对比', fontsize=13, fontweight='bold')
793	    ax.legend(fontsize=7, loc='upper left', bbox_to_anchor=(1.02, 1), framealpha=0.9)
794	    ax.tick_params(axis='x', rotation=30)
795	    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
796	    ax.spines['top'].set_visible(False); ax.spines['right'].set_visible(False)
797	
798	    plt.tight_layout()
799	    path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f'预测对比_{material}.png')
800	    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')
801	    plt.close()
802	    logger.info(f"  [图表] 预测对比图({material}) → {path}")
803

805	def plot_feature_importance(importance_dict, material):

836	def plot_metrics_comparison(all_metrics):
837	    """模型指标对比：分组柱状图（各物资各模型的四项指标）"""
838	    # 仅使用实际有预测结果的物资（跳过被SKIP_REACTOR_PROTECT等开关排除的）
839	    active_materials = [m for m in MATERIALS if m in all_metrics]
840	    if not active_materials:
841	        return
842	    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(16, 12))
843	    metric_names = ['MSE', 'RMSE', 'MAE', 'R2']
844	    # Dynamically collect all model names from results
845	    model_names = []
846	    for material in active_materials:
847	        for m in all_metrics[material]:
848	            if m not in model_names:
849	                model_names.append(m)
850	    colors = plt.cm.tab20(np.linspace(0, 1, max(len(model_names), 1)))
851	
852	    for ax_idx, metric in enumerate(metric_names):
853	        ax = axes[ax_idx // 2, ax_idx % 2]
854	        x = np.arange(len(active_materials))
855	        width = 0.8 / max(len(model_names), 1)
856	
857	        for i, model_name in enumerate(model_names):
858	            values = []
859	            for material in active_materials:
860	                vals = all_metrics[material].get(model_name, {})
861	                values.append(vals.get(metric, 0))
862	            bars = ax.bar(x + i * width, values, width, label=model_name,
863	                          color=colors[i], alpha=0.85, edgecolor='white')
864	
865	            # 数值标注
866	            for bar, val in zip(bars, values):
867	                ax.text(bar.get_x() + bar.get_width() / 2, bar.get_height() + 0.01,
868	                        f'{val:.4f}', ha='center', va='bottom', fontsize=7, rotation=90)
869	
870	        ax.set_title(metric, fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')
871	        ax.set_xticks(x + width * (len(model_names) - 1) / 2)
872	        ax.set_xticklabels([MATERIAL_LABELS[m] for m in active_materials])
873	        ax.legend(fontsize=8)
874	        ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3, axis='y')
875	
876	    plt.suptitle('模型评估指标对比', fontsize=16, fontweight='bold', y=1.01)
877	    plt.tight_layout()
878	    path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, '指标对比图.png')
879	    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')
880	    plt.close()
881	    logger.info(f"  [图表] 指标对比图 → {path}")

895	        ax.set_ylabel(f'{MATERIAL_LABELS[material]}\n需求量({unit})' if unit else f'{MATERIAL_LABELS[material]}\n需求量', fontsize=10)

904	    axes[0].set_title(f'配电网物资需求量 — {n_months}个月完整序列 ({date_range})', fontsize=13, fontweight='bold')

913	# ===================== log1p逆变换辅助 =====================
914	def _inverse_log1p(y_pred):
915	    """如果USE_LOG1P_TARGET启用, 将预测值从log空间逆变换回原始空间"""
916	    return np.expm1(y_pred) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_pred

918	
919	# ===================== 4. 两阶段预测框架 =====================
920	def _two_stage_fit_predict(X_tr, y_tr, X_te, stage2_regressor, material):
921	    """所有模型共享的两阶段预测: Stage1分类×Stage2回归 = P×Q"""
922	    y_bin = (y_tr > 0).astype(int)
923	    n_pos, n_neg = y_bin.sum(), len(y_bin) - y_bin.sum()
924	    if n_pos < 5 or n_neg < 5 or (y_tr > 0).sum() < 10:
925	        return None, None  # 回退信号
926	
927	    hp = get_hp('stage1_cls', material)
928	    cls = CatBoostClassifier(**hp,
929	        loss_function='Logloss', early_stopping_rounds=30, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
930	    nv = max(6, len(y_tr)//4)
931	    cls.fit(X_tr[:-nv], y_bin[:-nv], eval_set=(X_tr[-nv:], y_bin[-nv:]))
932	    prob = np.clip(cls.predict_proba(X_te)[:,1], 0, 1)
933	
934	    nz = y_tr > 0
935	    nv2 = min(6, nz.sum()//4)
936	    is_cb = hasattr(stage2_regressor, 'get_params')
937	    try:
938	        stage2_regressor.fit(X_tr[nz][:-nv2], y_tr[nz][:-nv2],
939	            eval_set=(X_tr[nz][-nv2:], y_tr[nz][-nv2:]))
940	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
941	        stage2_regressor.fit(X_tr[nz], y_tr[nz])
942	    return prob * np.maximum(stage2_regressor.predict(X_te), 0), cls
943	
944	
945	def run_catboost(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
946	    """CatBoost(朴素): 直接回归, 不使用两阶段框架(消融基线)"""
947	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
948	    hp = get_hp('catboost_direct', material)
949	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
950	        loss_function='RMSE', early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
951	    nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
952	    reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
953	    yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
954	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET: yp = np.expm1(yp)
955	    return yp, y_test, reg.get_feature_importance(), reg
956	
957	def run_catboost_tweedie(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
958	    """CatBoost-Tweedie(单阶段):  复合Poisson-Gamma损失函数直接回归。
959	
960	    关键差异: 不同于两阶段Tweedie(Stage1分类+Stage2的Tweedie loss), 这是单阶段——
961	    用全部69个月(含零值)训练, Tweedie损失天然建模"零=未发标+正=采购量"的生成机制。
962	    与RMSE直接回归的对比: Tweedie在零点有离散概率质量, 不会强迫模型预测非零。
963	
964	    预期: 波动数据(CV>1)上 Tweedie > RMSE(直接) >> 两阶段
965	    """
966	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
967	    hp = get_hp('catboost_tweedie', material)
968	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
969	        loss_function='Tweedie:variance_power=1.5',
970	        early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
971	    nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
972	    reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
973	    yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
974	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET: yp = np.expm1(yp)
975	    return yp, y_test, reg.get_feature_importance(), reg
976	
977	
978	# ===================== 实验4: 分位数回归 (可取消) =====================
979	def run_catboost_quantile(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
980	    """分位数回归: 训练 alpha=0.1/0.5/0.9 三个分位数模型。
981	
982	    中位数(0.5)作为点预测, (0.1, 0.9)作为80%预测区间。
983	    优势: 不做硬判决"有/无需求", 而是输出"不确定区间"。
984	    当下限>0 → 高置信度有需求; 上限≈0 → 高置信度没有。
985	    """
986	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
987	    hp = get_hp('quantile', material)
988	    preds = {}
989	    for alpha in [0.1, 0.5, 0.9]:
990	        reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
991	            loss_function=f'Quantile:alpha={alpha}',
992	            early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
993	        nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
994	        reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
995	        p = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
996	        if USE_LOG1P_TARGET: p = np.expm1(p)
997	        preds[alpha] = p
998	    yp = preds[0.5]  # 中位数点预测
999	    logger.info(f'  [分位数] 80%区间宽度均值={np.mean(preds[0.9]-preds[0.1]):.2f}')
1000	    return yp, y_test, None, (preds[0.1], preds[0.9])
1001	
1002	
1003	def run_catboost_2s(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
1004	    """TwoStage-CatBoost: 两阶段=分类×CatBoost回归"""
1005	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
1006	    hp = get_hp('catboost_2s', material)
1007	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
1008	        loss_function='RMSE', early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
1009	    yp, _ = _two_stage_fit_predict(X_train_factors, y_tr_log, X_test_factors, reg, material)
1010	    if yp is None:
1011	        logger.warning(f'  [CatBoost-2S] 回退到直接回归')
1012	        nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
1013	        reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
1014	        yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
1015	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET and yp is not None: yp = np.expm1(yp)
1016	    return yp, y_test, reg.get_feature_importance(), reg

1019	def run_conditional_catboost(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
1020	    """TwoStage-CondCatBoost: 两阶段=分类×更深CatBoost回归"""
1021	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
1022	    hp = get_hp('cond_catboost', material)
1023	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
1024	        loss_function='RMSE', early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
1025	    yp, _ = _two_stage_fit_predict(X_train_factors, y_tr_log, X_test_factors, reg, material)
1026	    if yp is None:
1027	        logger.warning(f'  [CondCatBoost-2S] 回退到直接回归')
1028	        nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
1029	        reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
1030	        yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
1031	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET and yp is not None: yp = np.expm1(yp)
1032	    return yp, y_test, reg.get_feature_importance(), reg

1035
