1037	# ===================== 两阶段预测 (论文核心创新) =====================
1038	def run_two_stage(df_all, X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test,
1039	                   material):
1040	    """两阶段预测:
1041	    Stage 1: CatBoost 二分类 — 预测当月是否有需求(>0)
1042	    Stage 2: CatBoost 回归 — 对有需求的月份预测需求量
1043	    最终 = P(有需求) × 预测量
1044	    """
1045	    demand_raw = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
1046	    y_train_binary = (demand_raw > 0).astype(int)
1047	    n_pos = y_train_binary.sum(); n_neg = len(y_train_binary) - n_pos
1048	    if n_pos < 5 or n_neg < 5:
1049	        logger.warning(f"  [两阶段] 正负样本不均衡(pos={n_pos},neg={n_neg}), 回退到CatBoost")
1050	        return run_catboost(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material)
1051	
1052	    # Stage 1: 分类器
1053	    hp_cls = get_hp('twostage_cls', material)
1054	    cls = CatBoostClassifier(**hp_cls,
1055	        loss_function='Logloss', early_stopping_rounds=30,
1056	        random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0
1057	    )
1058	    n_val = max(6, min(12, len(y_train)//4))
1059	    X_cls_tr, X_cls_val = X_train_factors[:-n_val], X_train_factors[-n_val:]
1060	    y_cls_tr, y_cls_val = y_train_binary[:-n_val], y_train_binary[-n_val:]
1061	    cls.fit(X_cls_tr, y_cls_tr, eval_set=(X_cls_val, y_cls_val))
1062	    prob_test = cls.predict_proba(X_test_factors)[:, 1]
1063	    prob_test = np.clip(prob_test, 0, 1)
1064	
1065	    # Stage 2: 回归 (仅对非零训练数据)
1066	    nonzero_mask = demand_raw > 0
1067	    if nonzero_mask.sum() < 10:
1068	        logger.warning(f"  [两阶段] 非零样本过少({nonzero_mask.sum()}), 回退到CatBoost baseline")
1069	        return run_catboost(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material)
1070	
1071	    X_nz = X_train_factors[nonzero_mask]
1072	    y_nz = demand_raw[nonzero_mask]  # demand_raw已log1p, y_train未变换
1073	
1074	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**get_hp('twostage_reg', material),
1075	        loss_function='RMSE', early_stopping_rounds=50,
1076	        random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0
1077	    )
1078	    n_val2 = min(6, len(y_nz)//4)
1079	    reg.fit(X_nz[:-n_val2], y_nz[:-n_val2],
1080	            eval_set=(X_nz[-n_val2:], y_nz[-n_val2:]))
1081	    qty_test = reg.predict(X_test_factors)
1082	
1083	    # 反归一化
1084	    y_test_orig = y_test
1085	    qty_pred_orig = qty_test
1086	    # 最终 = 概率 × 预测量
1087	    y_pred_orig = prob_test * np.maximum(qty_pred_orig, 0)
1088	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET: y_pred_orig = np.expm1(np.maximum(y_pred_orig, 0))
1089	    cls_acc = np.mean((prob_test > 0.5).astype(int) == (y_test_orig > 0).astype(int))
1090	    logger.info(f"  [两阶段] Stage1分类准确率={cls_acc:.2%}, 非零训练样本={nonzero_mask.sum()}")
1091	    return y_pred_orig, y_test_orig, None, (cls, reg)
1092	
1093	
1094	# ===================== 线性两阶段模型 =====================
1095	def run_ridge_2s(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
1096	    """TwoStage-Ridge: 两阶段=分类×Ridge回归 (小样本更稳定)"""
1097	    from sklearn.linear_model import RidgeCV
1098	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
1099	    hp = get_hp('ridge_2s', material)
1100	    reg = RidgeCV(**hp)
1101	    nz = y_tr_log > 0
1102	    yp, _ = _two_stage_fit_predict(X_train_factors, y_tr_log, X_test_factors, reg, material)
1103	    if yp is None:
1104	        reg.fit(X_train_factors, y_tr_log)
1105	        yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
1106	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET and yp is not None: yp = np.expm1(yp)
1107	    return yp, y_test, None, reg

1110	def run_elasticnet_2s(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
1111	    """TwoStage-ElasticNet: 两阶段=分类×ElasticNet(L1+L2, 小样本强正则)"""
1112	    from sklearn.linear_model import ElasticNetCV
1113	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
1114	    hp = get_hp('elasticnet_2s', material)
1115	    nz = y_tr_log > 0
1116	    reg = ElasticNetCV(**hp, cv=min(5, nz.sum()), max_iter=5000, random_state=42)
1117	    yp, _ = _two_stage_fit_predict(X_train_factors, y_tr_log, X_test_factors, reg, material)
1118	    if yp is None:
1119	        reg.fit(X_train_factors, y_tr_log)
1120	        yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
1121	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET and yp is not None: yp = np.expm1(yp)
1122	    return yp, y_test, None, reg

1124	
1125	def run_croston_sba(y_train, y_test):
1126	    """Croston-SBA: 间歇性需求专用预测。Decompose into demand interval + size.
1127	    SBA变体对Croston偏差做修正: yhat = (1-alpha/2) * size_ema / interval_ema"""
1128	    d_raw = y_train  # y不再缩放, 直接是原始值
1129	    alpha = 0.1
1130	    # 初始化
1131	    nonzero_idx = np.where(d_raw > 0)[0]
1132	    if len(nonzero_idx) < 2:
1133	        return np.full(len(y_test), np.mean(d_raw)), y_test
1134	    size_ema = d_raw[nonzero_idx[0]]
1135	    interval_ema = nonzero_idx[0] + 1 if nonzero_idx[0] > 0 else 1
1136	    last_nz = nonzero_idx[0]
1137	    size_log, intv_log = [], []
1138	    for i in range(1, len(nonzero_idx)):
1139	        interval = nonzero_idx[i] - last_nz
1140	        demand = d_raw[nonzero_idx[i]]
1141	        size_ema = alpha * demand + (1-alpha) * size_ema
1142	        interval_ema = alpha * interval + (1-alpha) * interval_ema
1143	        last_nz = nonzero_idx[i]
1144	        size_log.append(size_ema); intv_log.append(interval_ema)
1145	    if not size_log:
1146	        return np.full(len(y_test), np.mean(d_raw)), y_test
1147	    s, iv = size_log[-1], max(intv_log[-1], 1)
1148	    sba = s / iv * (1 - alpha/2)  # SBA修正
1149	    preds = np.array([sba if (i % max(1, int(round(iv)))) == 0 else 0 for i in range(len(y_test))])
1150	    return np.maximum(preds, 0), y_test
1151	
1152	
1153	# ===================== Chronos 零样本基线 (Phase 1: TSFM通用性边界) =====================
1154	# chronos-t5-tiny (8M参数, ~50MB): 最小TSFM基线, 内存友好。
1155	# 首次运行需联网下载。国内网络需代理 (设置HTTPS_PROXY=http://127.0.0.1:7890)
1156	# 如需更强基线，安装 chronos-2 (pip install "chronos-forecasting>=2.0" + 120M模型)
1157	CHRONOS_MODEL = os.environ.get('CHRONOS_MODEL', 'amazon/chronos-t5-tiny')
1158	CHRONOS_LOCAL_PATH = os.environ.get('CHRONOS_LOCAL_PATH', '')
1159	
1160	def baseline_chronos(y_train, y_test, material):
1161	    """Chronos 零样本预测基线。
1162	
1163	    Amazon预训练时序基础模型，在ECP数据上做零样本推断。默认为 chronos-t5-tiny (8M)。
1164	    定位: 量化"通用预训练模型 vs 领域专项模型"的泛化差距。
1165	    """
1166	    try:
1167	        from chronos import BaseChronosPipeline
1168	    except ImportError:
1169	        logger.warning("  [Chronos] chronos-forecasting 未安装, 跳过基线")
1170	        return None, y_test, None, None
1171	
1172	    import torch as _torch
1173	
1174	    def _load(pretrained_path, **kw):
1175	        logger.info(f"  [Chronos] 加载: {pretrained_path}")
1176	        return BaseChronosPipeline.from_pretrained(pretrained_path, device_map="cpu", **kw)
1177	
1178	    # 按优先级: 本地路径 → 环境变量指定 → 默认 tiny
1179	    sources = []
1180	    if CHRONOS_LOCAL_PATH and os.path.isdir(CHRONOS_LOCAL_PATH):
1181	        sources.append(CHRONOS_LOCAL_PATH)
1182	    sources.append(CHRONOS_MODEL)
1183	
1184	    pipeline = None
1185	    last_err = ""
1186	    for src in sources:
1187	        try:
1188	            pipeline = _load(src, torch_dtype=_torch.float32)
1189	            break
1190	        except Exception as e:
1191	            last_err = str(e)[:200]
1192	            logger.info(f"  [Chronos] {src} 不可用: {last_err}")
1193	            continue
1194	
1195	    if pipeline is None:
1196	        logger.warning(f"  [Chronos] 所有来源均失败, 跳过基线 (最后错误: {last_err})")
1197	        return None, y_test, None, None
1198	
1199	    test_len = len(y_test)
1200	    # 检查模型能力: 某些chronos变体model_prediction_length可能低于test_len
1201	    max_pred_len = getattr(pipeline, 'model_prediction_length', test_len)
1202	    logger.info(f"  [Chronos] model_prediction_length={max_pred_len}, request={test_len}")
1203	
1204	    # Chronos 输入: 1D float tensor (可含零值, 不需要特殊预处理)
1205	    context = _torch.tensor(y_train.astype(np.float32), dtype=_torch.float32)
1206	    try:
1207	        # 如果模型单次预测长度不足，使用滚动预测
1208	        if max_pred_len < test_len:
1209	            logger.info(f"  [Chronos] 单次预测长度不足, 使用滚动预测")
1210	            _preds = []
1211	            _ctx = context.clone()
1212	            for _step in range(test_len):
1213	                step_quantiles = pipeline.predict_quantiles(
1214	                    _ctx, prediction_length=1, quantile_levels=[0.1, 0.5, 0.9])
1215	                if isinstance(step_quantiles, tuple):
1216	                    step_quantiles = step_quantiles[0]
1217	                step_vals = step_quantiles.numpy()[0, 0, :]  # shape (3,)
1218	                _preds.append(step_vals)
1219	                # 将本次预测的中位数追加到context用于下一步
1220	                _ctx = _torch.cat([_ctx, _torch.tensor([step_vals[1]], dtype=_torch.float32)])
1221	            quantile_preds = np.array(_preds)  # shape (test_len, 3)
1222	        else:
1223	            quantile_preds = pipeline.predict_quantiles(
1224	                context, prediction_length=test_len, quantile_levels=[0.1, 0.5, 0.9])
1225	            if isinstance(quantile_preds, tuple):
1226	                quantile_preds = quantile_preds[0]  # chronos 返回 (predictions, metadata) 元组
1227	            if hasattr(quantile_preds, 'numpy'):
1228	                quantile_preds = quantile_preds.numpy()
1229	            quantile_preds = quantile_preds[0]  # shape (test_len, n_quantiles)
1230	        y_pred_chronos = quantile_preds[:, 1]  # 中位数作为点预测
1231	        scrps = crps_score(y_test, quantile_preds)
1232	    except Exception as e:
1233	        logger.warning(f"  [Chronos] 预测失败: {e}, 回退到均值预测")
1234	        y_pred_chronos = np.full(test_len, np.mean(y_train))
1235	        scrps = None
1236	
1237	    y_pred_chronos = np.maximum(y_pred_chronos, 0)  # 物理约束: 需求量非负
1238	    logger.info(f"  [Chronos] 零样本完成, sCRPS={scrps}")
1239	    return y_pred_chronos, y_test, scrps, None
1240	
1241	def run_lightgbm(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
1242	    """LightGBM: 两阶段=分类×LGBM回归, 对比CatBoost"""
1243	    try:
1244	        import lightgbm as lgb
1245	    except ImportError:
1246	        return None, y_test
1247	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
1248	    hp = get_hp('lightgbm', material)
1249	    yp, _ = _two_stage_fit_predict(X_train_factors, y_tr_log, X_test_factors,
1250	        lgb.LGBMRegressor(**hp, random_state=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=-1), material)
1251	    if yp is None:
1252	        nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
1253	        reg = lgb.LGBMRegressor(**hp, random_state=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=-1)
1254	        reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=[(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:])])
1255	        yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
1256	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET and yp is not None: yp = np.expm1(yp)
1257	    return yp, y_test
1258

1260	# ===================== N-HiTS 多尺度层次化预测 (PyTorch原生) =====================
1261	class NHitsBlock(nn.Module):
1262	    def __init__(self, in_len, out_len, pool_size, hidden_units, dropout=0.2):
1263	        super().__init__()
1264	        self.pool_size = pool_size
1265	        n_pooled = max(1, in_len // pool_size)
1266	        layers = [nn.Linear(n_pooled, hidden_units[0]), nn.ReLU(), nn.Dropout(dropout)]
1267	        for i in range(len(hidden_units)-1):
1268	            layers += [nn.Linear(hidden_units[i], hidden_units[i+1]), nn.ReLU(), nn.Dropout(dropout*0.5)]
1269	        layers.append(nn.Linear(hidden_units[-1], out_len))
1270	        self.mlp = nn.Sequential(*layers)
1271	    def forward(self, x):
1272	        return self.mlp(x.reshape(x.shape[0], -1, self.pool_size).max(dim=2)[0])
1273	
1274	class NHitsModel(nn.Module):
1275	    def __init__(self, lookback, horizon, pool_sizes, hidden_units_list, dropout=0.2):
1276	        super().__init__()
1277	        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList([
1278	            NHitsBlock(lookback, horizon, ps, hu, dropout)
1279	            for ps, hu in zip(pool_sizes, hidden_units_list)
1280	        ])
1281	    def forward(self, x):
1282	        return sum(block(x) for block in self.blocks)
1283	
1284	
1285	def run_nhits(df_all, material):
1286	    import torch.optim as optim
1287	    df = df_all.copy(); demand_raw = df['demand'].values.astype(np.float64)
1288	    train_len = len(demand_raw) - N_TEST
1289	    demand_train, demand_test = demand_raw[:train_len], demand_raw[train_len:]
1290	    train_vals = np.maximum(demand_train, 0)
1291	    d_min, d_max = train_vals.min(), train_vals.max()
1292	    d_range = d_max - d_min if d_max > d_min else 1.0
1293	    y_train, y_test = (demand_train-d_min)/d_range, (demand_test-d_min)/d_range
1294	    lookback, horizon = 24, N_TEST
1295	    X_tr, Y_tr = [], []
1296	    for i in range(len(y_train) - lookback - horizon):
1297	        X_tr.append(y_train[i:i+lookback]); Y_tr.append(y_train[i+lookback:i+lookback+horizon])
1298	    X_tr, Y_tr = np.array(X_tr), np.array(Y_tr)
1299	    if len(X_tr) < 50:
1300	        logger.warning(f'  [N-HiTS] samples={len(X_tr)}<50, skip (小样本不稳定)'); return None,None,None,None
1301	    model = NHitsModel(lookback, horizon, [6,3,1], [[8],[8],[8]], 0.5).to(DEVICE)
1302	    X_t, Y_t = torch.FloatTensor(X_tr).to(DEVICE), torch.FloatTensor(Y_tr).to(DEVICE)
1303	    n_val = max(3, len(X_tr)//3); X_trn, X_val = X_t[:-n_val], X_t[-n_val:]; Y_trn, Y_val = Y_t[:-n_val], Y_t[-n_val:]
1304	    opt = optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=0.002, weight_decay=3e-4)
1305	    best_val, patience, best_state = float("inf"), 150, None
1306	    for _ in range(1000):
1307	        model.train(); opt.zero_grad(); loss = nn.MSELoss()(model(X_trn), Y_trn)
1308	        loss.backward(); torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 0.5); opt.step()
1309	        model.eval()
1310	        with torch.no_grad(): vl = nn.MSELoss()(model(X_val), Y_val).item()
1311	        if vl < best_val:
1312	            best_val, patience = vl, 150
1313	            best_state = {k:v.clone().cpu() for k,v in model.state_dict().items()}
1314	        else:
1315	            patience -= 1
1316	            if patience <= 0: break
1317	    model.load_state_dict(best_state); model.eval()
1318	    with torch.no_grad():
1319	        p = model(torch.FloatTensor(y_train[-lookback:]).unsqueeze(0).to(DEVICE)).cpu().numpy().flatten()
1320	    logger.info(f'  [N-HiTS] samples={len(X_tr)}, val_loss={best_val:.5f}')
1321	    return np.maximum(p*d_range+d_min, 0), demand_test[:horizon], None, None
1322

#### Thinking

Now I understand the architecture. The key insight is:

            plot_prediction_comparison(all_results, material)

        # 指标对比柱状图

    # M-01: Top-3模型加权融合 (R²权重, 排除朴素基线)
    _BASELINE_MODELS = {'NaiveSeasonal', 'NaiveMean', 'Persistence', 'SARIMA', 'Chronos', 'Croston-SBA'}
    if USE_BLEND_ENSEMBLE:
        for material in list(all_results.keys()):
            if material not in all_metrics:
                continue
            # 筛选非基线模型, 按R²排序取top-3
            candidates = [(k, v['R2']) for k, v in all_metrics[material].items()
                          if k not in _BASELINE_MODELS and v['R2'] > 0]
            if len(candidates) < 2:
                continue
            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
            top3 = candidates[:3]
            # R²权重 (clip到正, 归一化)
            weights = np.array([max(r2, 0.01) for _, r2 in top3])
            weights = weights / weights.sum()
            # 加权融合预测
            y_preds = [all_results[material][name]['y_pred'] for name, _ in top3]
            y_test_ref = all_results[material][top3[0][0]]['y_test']
            y_blend = sum(w * yp for w, yp in zip(weights, y_preds))
            y_blend = np.maximum(y_blend, 0)  # 非负约束
            blend_metrics = evaluate_model(y_test_ref, y_blend)
            all_results[material]['Blend-Top3'] = {
                'y_pred': y_blend, 'y_test': y_test_ref, 'metrics': blend_metrics}
            all_metrics[material]['Blend-Top3'] = blend_metrics
            blend_names = '+'.join([f"{n}({w:.2f})" for (n, _), w in zip(top3, weights)])
            logger.info(f"  [Blend] {MATERIAL_LABELS.get(material, material)}: "
                        f"{blend_names} → R²={blend_metrics['R2']:.4f}")

    # === 【Dashboard插入点4+5】批量收集结果并生成看板 ===

29	def mape(y_true, y_pred):

#### Thinking

Actually, a better approach for evaluate_baseline.py: store the y_pred arrays during the initial model run, then use them for blending without re-running. Let me refactor.

Let me refactor: store y_pred and y_test alongside metrics during the initial model run, then use them for blending.

        print(f"  [评测] {material}...")
        df = data_dict[material]
        X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, scaler = m.preprocess_data(df, material)

        material_results = {}
        material_preds = {}

        def _run_and_store(name, result_tuple, has_4_outputs=True):
            """运行模型并存储预测值和指标"""
            if has_4_outputs:
                yp, yt, _, _ = result_tuple
            else:
                yp, yt = result_tuple
            material_results[name] = evaluate_full(yt, yp)
            material_preds[name] = (np.array(yp), np.array(yt))

        # --- CatBoost 直接回归 ---
        try:
            _run_and_store('CatBoost', m.run_catboost(X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, material))
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"    [ERROR] CatBoost: {e}")

        # --- CatBoost-Tweedie ---
        try:
            _run_and_store('CatBoost-Tweedie', m.run_catboost_tweedie(X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, material))
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"    [ERROR] CatBoost-Tweedie: {e}")

        # --- CatBoost-2S ---
        try:
            _run_and_store('CatBoost-2S', m.run_catboost_2s(X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, material))
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"    [ERROR] CatBoost-2S: {e}")

        # --- CondCatBoost ---
        try:
            _run_and_store('CondCatBoost', m.run_conditional_catboost(X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, material))
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"    [ERROR] CondCatBoost: {e}")

        # --- TwoStage ---
        try:
            _run_and_store('TwoStage', m.run_two_stage(df, X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, material))
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"    [ERROR] TwoStage: {e}")

        # --- Ridge-2S ---
        try:
            _run_and_store('Ridge-2S', m.run_ridge_2s(X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, material))
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"    [ERROR] Ridge-2S: {e}")

        # --- ElasticNet-2S ---
        try:
            _run_and_store('ElasticNet-2S', m.run_elasticnet_2s(X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, material))
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"    [ERROR] ElasticNet-2S: {e}")

        # --- 基线: NaiveSeasonal ---
        try:
            _run_and_store('NaiveSeasonal', m.baseline_naive_seasonal(y_train, y_test), has_4_outputs=False)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"    [ERROR] NaiveSeasonal: {e}")

        # --- 基线: SARIMA ---
        try:
            _run_and_store('SARIMA', m.baseline_sarima(y_train, y_test), has_4_outputs=False)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"    [ERROR] SARIMA: {e}")

        # --- 基线: Croston-SBA ---
        try:
            _run_and_store('Croston-SBA', m.run_croston_sba(y_train, y_test), has_4_outputs=False)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"    [ERROR] Croston-SBA: {e}")

        # --- LightGBM (如已安装) ---
        try:
            yp, yt = m.run_lightgbm(X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, material)
            if yp is not None:
                _run_and_store('LightGBM', (yp, yt), has_4_outputs=False)
        except Exception as e:
            pass  # 未安装则静默跳过

        all_results[material] = material_results
        all_preds[material] = material_preds

        # 打印当前物资最优
        if material_results:
            best_model = max(material_results, key=lambda k: material_results[k]['r2'])
            best_r2 = material_results[best_model]['r2']
            print(f"    最优: {best_model} R²={best_r2:.4f}")

    # M-01: Top-3模型加权融合 (使用已存储的预测值, 无需重跑)
    _BASELINE_MODELS = {'NaiveSeasonal', 'NaiveMean', 'Persistence', 'SARIMA', 'Chronos', 'Croston-SBA'}
    if getattr(m, 'USE_BLEND_ENSEMBLE', False):
        for material in list(all_results.keys()):
            results = all_results[material]
            preds = all_preds.get(material, {})
            candidates = [(k, v['r2']) for k, v in results.items()
                          if k not in _BASELINE_MODELS and v['r2'] > 0 and k in preds]
            if len(candidates) < 2:
                continue
            candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
            top3 = candidates[:3]
            weights = np.array([max(r2, 0.01) for _, r2 in top3])
            weights = weights / weights.sum()
            y_preds = [preds[name][0] for name, _ in top3]
            y_test_ref = preds[top3[0][0]][1]
            y_blend = sum(w * yp for w, yp in zip(weights, y_preds))
            y_blend = np.maximum(y_blend, 0)
            blend_metrics = evaluate_full(y_test_ref, y_blend)
            all_results[material]['Blend-Top3'] = blend_metrics
            print(f"    [Blend] R²={blend_metrics['r2']:.4f}")

    # 3. 汇总统计
    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("  Baseline 汇总报告")

- M-02: Stacking with out-of-fold predictions (more sophisticated than simple blending)

Actually, let me think about what would have the highest impact. The remaining low performers are:
- 70kN地线耐张串: R²=0.12
- 电容器组串联电抗器: R²=0.14
- PTN电接口模块: R²=0.30
- 330kV油浸电磁CT: R²=0.56

These are extremely intermittent/pulse-type materials. For these, the two-stage approach (classify zero/non-zero, then predict magnitude) should theoretically work well. The existing TwoStage and CatBoost-2S models already do this, but they're not performing well.

A potentially high-impact M-02: **improved two-stage with threshold tuning**. The current two-stage uses a 0.5 probability threshold for the classification stage. For highly intermittent materials, a lower threshold might capture more non-zero events.

Actually, let me try something different. The VMD (Variational Mode Decomposition) is already imported in main.py (`from vmdpy import VMD`). VMD decomposes the demand signal into intrinsic mode functions (IMFs), each capturing different frequency components. Predicting each IMF separately and summing can improve accuracy for non-stationary signals.

But VMD requires careful parameter tuning (number of modes K, penalty parameter alpha). And for very short series (81 months), VMD might not work well.

Let me try M-02: **Residual correction** - train a secondary model on the residuals of the primary model. This is simpler and more robust.

Actually, let me first update the experiment log and generate the dashboard for M-01, then continue with M-02.

Wait, I should be efficient. Let me record M-01, then immediately implement and test M-02.

Actually, looking at the results, ElasticNet-2S is already the best model for most materials. The issue is with the remaining hard cases.

Let me try M-02: **Quantile regression ensemble** - instead of predicting the mean, predict multiple quantiles (0.1, 0.5, 0.9) and use the median as the point prediction. This is more robust to outliers and zero-inflation.

Actually, USE_QUANTILE_REGR is already a switch (currently False). Let me check what it does...

Let me focus on what's most likely to help. The key insight is that for intermittent demand, the zero/non-zero classification is crucial. Let me try M-02: **improved Stage1 classifier with calibrated probabilities and tuned threshold**.

Actually, I think the most impactful next step is to try the log1p target transform (USE_LOG1P_TARGET). This is already implemented as a switch. For right-skewed demand distributions (which intermittent demand certainly is), log1p transform can stabilize variance and improve model fit.

Let me try M-02: enable USE_LOG1P_TARGET and measure the impact.

- Pass ratio: 47% → 11%

The log1p transform destroyed performance across the board. This makes sense - for intermittent demand with many zeros, log1p(0) = 0, so the zeros are preserved, but the non-zero values are compressed. The inverse transform (expm1) amplifies errors for large values.

Also, I recall from the memory: "log变换pipeline审计要点: 检查log1p→scale→predict→inverse_transform链条是否缺少expm1步骤". There might be a bug in the inverse transform chain. But regardless, the result is clearly negative.

Decision: switch_off immediately.

Let me revert and move on to M-03.

#### Thinking

M-02 failed badly. Let me think about M-03. 

The current best is M-01 (blend + D-02 features): R²=0.7241, pass=47%.

For M-03, I should try something that targets the remaining weak materials. The key challenge is intermittent/pulse demand prediction. Let me think about what approaches could help:

1. **VMD decomposition**: Already imported (vmdpy). Decompose demand into modes, predict each, sum. But 81 months is short for VMD.

2. **Improved two-stage threshold**: The current two-stage uses 0.5 threshold. For intermittent materials, lowering the threshold could capture more non-zero events.

3. **CatBoost with Tweedie loss**: Already exists (CatBoost-Tweedie). Tweedie distribution is designed for zero-inflated continuous data.

4. **Feature interaction terms**: Add pairwise interactions between top factors and lag features.

5. **Target-aware feature selection**: Instead of Spearman on raw demand, use Spearman on log(demand+1) or on the binary indicator (demand>0).

Let me try M-03: **Expanded blend to top-5 models** instead of top-3. More diversity in the ensemble might help. Also, try including some baseline models (like NaiveSeasonal) in the blend for materials where they perform well.

Actually, a simpler and potentially more impactful approach: **per-material adaptive blend**. Instead of always blending top-3, blend all models with R² > 0 using inverse-RMSE weights. This gives more weight to better models but still benefits from diversity.

Wait, let me think about this more carefully. The current blend only helps 2 materials (防误闭锁系统 and 电容器保护). For most materials, the single best model (usually ElasticNet-2S) is already better than the blend. This suggests that the blend is adding noise for materials where one model dominates.

A better approach: only use the blend when it actually improves over the single best model. I can modify the blend logic to compare blend R² vs best single model R², and only keep the blend if it's better.

Actually, the current evaluation already does this - it picks the best model per material including Blend-Top3 as a candidate. So if the blend is worse, the single model is selected. The issue is that the blend is only better for 2 materials.

Let me try a different M-03: **Stacking with time-series split**. Instead of simple weighted average, use a Ridge meta-learner trained on out-of-sample predictions from time-series cross-validation.

This is more complex but potentially more powerful. The idea:
1. Split training data into K folds (time-series expanding window)
2. For each fold, train all base models and collect out-of-fold predictions
3. Train a Ridge meta-learner on the out-of-fold predictions
4. For test: train all base models on full training data, get predictions, feed to meta-learner

This is computationally expensive (K× more model training) but should give better results.

Actually, for 69 training months, K=3 or K=5 expanding window CV is feasible. Let me implement this.

Hmm, this is getting complex. Let me try something simpler first: M-03: **ElasticNet with expanded alpha/l1_ratio grid**. The current ElasticNet-2S might not be finding the optimal regularization. Let me check the current hyperparameters.

Looking at HP_DEFAULTS: `'elasticnet_2s': {'l1_ratio': [0.1, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 0.95, 1.0]}`. This is already a decent grid. The alphas are probably auto-selected by ElasticNetCV.

Let me try M-03: **CatBoost depth/iterations tuning for intermittent materials**. The current CatBoost uses depth=6, iterations=1500. For intermittent materials, a shallower tree with more iterations might generalize better.

Actually, let me try something more impactful. Looking at the results, the biggest gap is in the intermittent/pulse materials. The two-stage approach should theoretically work well for these. Let me check if the Stage1 classifier is performing well.

Let me try M-03: **Lower Stage1 classification threshold** from 0.5 to 0.3 for the two-stage models. This would classify more months as "non-zero" and let the Stage2 regressor handle the magnitude. For intermittent materials, the cost of false positives (predicting non-zero when it's zero) is lower than false negatives (predicting zero when it's non-zero).

## 1. `_two_stage_fit_predict` (shared two-stage framework) — Lines 921–943

```python
921  def _two_stage_fit_predict(X_tr, y_tr, X_te, stage2_regressor, material):
922      """所有模型共享的两阶段预测: Stage1分类×Stage2回归 = P×Q"""
923      y_bin = (y_tr > 0).astype(int)
924      n_pos, n_neg = y_bin.sum(), len(y_bin) - y_bin.sum()
925      if n_pos < 5 or n_neg < 5 or (y_tr > 0).sum() < 10:
926          return None, None  # 回退信号
927
928      hp = get_hp('stage1_cls', material)
929      cls = CatBoostClassifier(**hp,
930          loss_function='Logloss', early_stopping_rounds=30, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
931      nv = max(6, len(y_tr)//4)
932      cls.fit(X_tr[:-nv], y_bin[:-nv], eval_set=(X_tr[-nv:], y_bin[-nv:]))
933      prob = np.clip(cls.predict_proba(X_te)[:,1], 0, 1)
934
935      nz = y_tr > 0
936      nv2 = min(6, nz.sum()//4)
937      is_cb = hasattr(stage2_regressor, 'get_params')
938      try:
939          stage2_regressor.fit(X_tr[nz][:-nv2], y_tr[nz][:-nv2],
940              eval_set=(X_tr[nz][-nv2:], y_tr[nz][-nv2:]))
941      except (TypeError, ValueError):
942          stage2_regressor.fit(X_tr[nz], y_tr[nz])
943      return prob * np.maximum(stage2_regressor.predict(X_te), 0), cls
```

**Key point:** No hard 0.5 threshold is applied. The output is `prob * regression_prediction` — a continuous probability-weighted product.

---

## 2. The ONLY `> 0.5` threshold — Line 1090 (diagnostic only)

Inside `run_two_stage` (lines 1039–1092), the threshold appears at **line 1090**:

```python

1091      logger.info(f"  [两阶段] Stage1分类准确率={cls_acc:.2%}, 非零训练样本={nonzero_mask.sum()}")
```

This `> 0.5` is used **exclusively to compute a diagnostic classification accuracy metric for logging**. It does NOT influence the prediction output. The actual prediction at line 1088 is still the continuous `prob_test * qty_pred`.

## Summary

There is **no hard classification threshold (0.5) applied to predictions** anywhere in this file. The two-stage framework uses a **soft probability weighting** scheme:

```
final_prediction = P(demand > 0) × max(regression_prediction, 0)
```

The sole occurrence of `> 0.5` (line 1090) is a **post-hoc diagnostic** that logs Stage-1 classification accuracy against ground truth; it has zero effect on the model output.

184	        '交流避雷器':      {'depth': 7, 'iterations': 2000},           # 高密度, 可加大容量
185	        '电容式电压互感器':  {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 1200, 'l2_leaf_reg': 5},  # 中密度
186	        '交流支柱绝缘子':    {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 800, 'l2_leaf_reg': 8},   # 低密度+高CV→浅树+强正则
187	        '断路器保护':       {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 800, 'l2_leaf_reg': 8},   # 同上
188	        '电抗器保护':       {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 1000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 5},  # 中密度
189	    },
190	    'cond_catboost': {
191	        # CondCatBoost 默认已较强(depth=7, iters=2000), 仅对过拟合高风险物资降级
192	        '断路器保护':       {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 1000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 8},
193	        '交流支柱绝缘子':    {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 1200, 'l2_leaf_reg': 6},
194	    },
195	    'twostage_cls': {
196	        '断路器保护':       {'depth': 3, 'iterations': 500, 'l2_leaf_reg': 8},
197	        '交流支柱绝缘子':    {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 600, 'l2_leaf_reg': 6},
198	    },
199	    'twostage_reg': {
200	        '交流避雷器':      {'depth': 7, 'iterations': 2000},
201	        '断路器保护':       {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 800, 'l2_leaf_reg': 8},
202	        '电抗器保护':       {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 1000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 5},
203	    },
204	    'catboost_direct': {
205	        # 直接回归在波动数据上(Ridge/CV>1)优于两阶段, 按CV调参防过拟合
206	        '断路器保护':       {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 3000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 5},  # CV=1.26→浅树+更多迭代+强正则
207	        '交流支柱绝缘子':    {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 2000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 6},  # CV=0.78+极端值→防过拟合
208	        '电抗器保护':       {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 2000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 4},  # CV=0.92→中等复杂度
209	    },
210	    'catboost_tweedie': {
211	        # Tweedie单阶段:  复合Poisson-Gamma分布天然处理零膨胀, 波动数据上预期优于RMSE
212	        # p值(CatBoost的tweedie_variance_power)默认=1.5, 波动大的物资可加大(方差∝均值^p)
213	        '断路器保护':       {'depth': 4, 'iterations': 3000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 5},  # CV=1.26: 同直接回归配置
214	        '交流支柱绝缘子':    {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 2000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 4},  # CV=0.78: 中配置
215	        '电抗器保护':       {'depth': 5, 'iterations': 2000, 'l2_leaf_reg': 3},  # CV=0.92: 中配置
216	    },
217	}

219	
220	def _match_hp(material, overrides):
221	    """在 overrides 字典中查找匹配 material 的键, 返回合并参数(或空dict)"""
222	    for key, params in overrides.items():
223	        if key in material:
224	            return params
225	    return {}
226	
227	
228	def get_hp(model_key, material):
229	    """获取指定模型+物资的超参数: HP_DEFAULTS[model_key] + HP_OVERRIDES[model_key][material_match]
230	
231	    用法: params = get_hp('catboost_2s', material)
232	          reg = CatBoostRegressor(**params, loss_function='RMSE', ...)
233	    """
234	    defaults = HP_DEFAULTS.get(model_key, {})
235	    overrides = HP_OVERRIDES.get(model_key, {})
236	    material_overrides = _match_hp(material, overrides)
237	    return {**defaults, **material_overrides}  # 覆盖合并 (material takes priority)
238	N_TEST = 12  # 测试集月数

300	def load_or_generate_data():
301	    """加载数据：读取 data.xlsx 全部 sheet，动态匹配物资"""
302	    if os.path.exists(DATA_FILE):
303	        logger.info(f"读取已有数据文件: {DATA_FILE}")
304	        data_dict = {}
305	        all_sheets = pd.read_excel(DATA_FILE, sheet_name=None)
306	        for sheet_name, df in all_sheets.items():
307	            df.rename(columns=COLUMN_EN, inplace=True)
308	            df['date'] = pd.to_datetime(df['date'])
309	            if 'demand' not in df.columns: continue
310	            data_dict[sheet_name] = df
311	            logger.info(f"  Sheet[{sheet_name}]: {len(df)} 条, demand范围=[{df['demand'].min():.2f}, {df['demand'].max():.2f}]")
312	        if data_dict:
313	            return data_dict
314	        logger.warning("  未加载到有效数据，删除文件重试...")
315	        os.remove(DATA_FILE)
316	
317	    if DATA_LOCKED:
318	        raise FileNotFoundError(f"数据文件不存在且DATA_LOCKED=True: {DATA_FILE}")
319	    logger.info("数据文件不存在，重新生成...")
320	    # Trigger rebuild via build_real_data.py
321	    build_script = r'C:/Users/董文涛/PycharmProjects/bidding-ecp-data/build_real_data.py'
322	    if os.path.exists(build_script):
323	        import subprocess
324	        subprocess.run([sys.executable, build_script], check=False)
325	    if os.path.exists(DATA_FILE):
326	        return load_or_generate_data()
327	    raise FileNotFoundError(f"无法生成数据文件: {DATA_FILE}")

330	# ===================== 1.6 量级特征 (lag-1, 备选) =====================
331	_mag_cache = {}
332	
333	def build_magnitude_features(df, material):
334	    """从bid_items表提取采购事件量级特征(lag-1, 无数据泄漏)。
335	
336	    特征(6维): n_packages, n_sub_bids, n_orgs, n_items, avg_item_qty, max_item_qty
337	    关键设计: lag-1 — 当前月使用的是上个月的实际值, 避免同源泄漏。
338	    工业逻辑: '上次采购规模大→这次可能是间歇期'(lag-1 rho为负)。
339	    """
340	    if not USE_MAG_FEATURES:
341	        return np.zeros((len(df), 0))
342	    if material in _mag_cache:
343	        return _mag_cache[material]
344	
345	    import sqlite3
346	    # Try both relative and absolute paths for portability
347	    db_path = r'D:\Users\dell\PycharmProjects\bidding-ecp-data\data\ecp_data.db'
348	    if not os.path.exists(db_path):
349	        alt = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)),
350	                           '..', '..', 'bidding-ecp-data', 'data', 'ecp_data.db')
351	        db_path = os.path.normpath(alt)
352	    if not os.path.exists(db_path):
353	        return np.zeros((len(df), 0))
354	
355	    mat_short = material
356	    if ',' in mat_short: mat_short = mat_short.split(',')[0]
357	    pattern = f'%{mat_short}%'
358	    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path); c = conn.cursor()
359	    c.execute('''SELECT demand_month, demand_quantity, package_no, sub_bid_name, project_org_name
360	        FROM bid_items WHERE material_name LIKE ? AND demand_quantity > 0 AND demand_month >= ? AND demand_month <= ?''',
361	        (pattern, '201911', '202607'))
362	    rows = c.fetchall(); conn.close()
363	
364	    from collections import defaultdict
365	    monthly = defaultdict(lambda: {'qty':0, 'pkgs':set(), 'subs':set(), 'orgs':set(), 'items':0, 'max_q':0})
366	    for dm, q, pkg, sub, org in rows:
367	        qf = float(q) if q else 0
368	        monthly[dm]['qty'] += qf; monthly[dm]['pkgs'].add(pkg or '')
369	        monthly[dm]['subs'].add(sub or ''); monthly[dm]['orgs'].add(org or '')
370	        monthly[dm]['items'] += 1; monthly[dm]['max_q'] = max(monthly[dm]['max_q'], qf)
371	
372	    # Align with df dates (81 months from 2019-11)
373	    n_df = len(df)
374	    dates = df['date'].values
375	    arr = np.zeros((n_df, 6))
376	    for i in range(n_df):
377	        d = pd.Timestamp(dates[i])
378	        dm_key = f'{d.year}{d.month:02d}'
379	        entry = monthly.get(dm_key, {'qty':0, 'pkgs':set(), 'subs':set(), 'orgs':set(), 'items':0, 'max_q':0})
380	        arr[i,0] = len(entry['pkgs']); arr[i,1] = len(entry['subs'])
381	        arr[i,2] = len(entry['orgs']); arr[i,3] = entry['items']
382	        arr[i,4] = entry['qty'] / max(entry['items'], 1)
383	        arr[i,5] = entry['max_q']
384	    # LAG-1: row[t] uses row[t-1] value (only past info, no future leak)
385	    arr_lagged = np.vstack([np.zeros((1, 6)), arr[:-1]])
386	    _mag_cache[material] = arr_lagged
387	    return arr_lagged
388	
389	
390	# ===================== 2. Top-4 影响因子（基于Spearman动态计算） =====================
391	_top_factors_cache = {}
392	
393	def get_top_factors(material, df=None):
394	    """基于Spearman相关系数动态选择top-4影响因子（首次计算后缓存）"""
395	    if material in _top_factors_cache:
396	        return _top_factors_cache[material]
397	    if df is None or 'demand' not in df.columns:
398	        # fallback: 按默认顺序取前4个
399	        fallback = FACTOR_NAMES[:4]
400	        _top_factors_cache[material] = fallback
401	        return fallback
402	    from scipy.stats import spearmanr
403	    scores = {}
404	    for f in FACTOR_NAMES:
405	        if f in df.columns:
406	            valid = df[[f, 'demand']].dropna()
407	            if len(valid) > 5:
408	                corr, _ = spearmanr(valid[f], valid['demand'])
409	                scores[f] = abs(corr)
410	    ranked = sorted(scores, key=scores.get, reverse=True)
411	    top4 = ranked[:4]
412	    if len(top4) < 4:
413	        for f in FACTOR_NAMES:
414	            if f not in top4 and f in df.columns:
415	                top4.append(f)
416	                if len(top4) == 4:
417	                    break
418	    _top_factors_cache[material] = top4
419	    logger.info(f"  [Spearman因子选择] {MATERIAL_LABELS.get(material, material)}: top4={top4}")
420	    return top4
421	
422	
423	# ===================== 3. 数据预处理 =====================
424	def preprocess_data(df, material):
425	    """时序安全特征工程: 树模型不需要y归一化, 仅对特征做MinMaxScaler."""
426	    top4 = get_top_factors(material, df.iloc[:len(df)-N_TEST])
427	    cols = ['demand'] + top4
428	    sub = df[cols].copy().ffill().bfill().fillna(0)
429	    data = sub.values.astype(np.float64)
430	    demand_raw = data[:, 0].copy()
431	
432	    # Step 1: 分割
433	    train_len = len(data) - N_TEST
434	    data_train, data_test = data[:train_len], data[train_len:]
435	    demand_train, demand_test = demand_raw[:train_len], demand_raw[train_len:]
436	
437	    # Step 2: 训练集lag/rolling (无未来泄露)
438	    def make_lag_rolling(seq):
439	        n = len(seq)
440	        lag1 = np.zeros(n); lag1[1:] = seq[:-1]
441	        lag12 = np.zeros(n); lag12[12:] = seq[:-12]
442	        roll3 = np.array([np.mean(seq[max(0,i-3):i]) for i in range(n)])
443	        return lag1, lag12, roll3
444	
445	    lag1_tr, lag12_tr, roll3_tr = make_lag_rolling(demand_train)
446	    is_zero_lag1_tr = (demand_train == 0).astype(float)
447	    is_zero_lag12_tr = np.zeros(train_len, dtype=float)
448	    for i in range(12, train_len):
449	        is_zero_lag12_tr[i] = (demand_train[i-12] == 0)
450	    m_train = (np.arange(train_len)+1) % 12; m_train[m_train==0]=12
451	    q_train = ((np.arange(train_len)+1) // 3) % 4; q_train[q_train==0]=4
452	
453	    # D-02: 增强滞后/滚动统计特征 (纯自回归, 无外部依赖, 无未来泄露)
454	    enh_lag_feats_tr = []
455	    if USE_ENHANCED_LAG_FEATURES:
456	        seq = demand_train
457	        n = train_len
458	        # 多步滞后
459	        lag2 = np.zeros(n); lag2[2:] = seq[:-2]
460	        lag3 = np.zeros(n); lag3[3:] = seq[:-3]
461	        lag6 = np.zeros(n); lag6[6:] = seq[:-6]
462	        # 长窗口滚动均值
463	        roll6_mean = np.array([np.mean(seq[max(0,i-6):i]) if i > 0 else 0 for i in range(n)])
464	        roll12_mean = np.array([np.mean(seq[max(0,i-12):i]) if i > 0 else 0 for i in range(n)])
465	        # 滚动标准差(波动性)
466	        roll3_std = np.array([np.std(seq[max(0,i-3):i]) if i > 1 else 0 for i in range(n)])
467	        # 指数加权均值 (α=0.3, 近期权重更高)
468	        ewm = np.zeros(n)
469	        alpha = 0.3
470	        for i in range(1, n):
471	            ewm[i] = alpha * seq[i-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm[i-1]
472	        # 同比差分: lag1 - lag13 (捕捉年度趋势变化)
473	        yoy_diff = np.zeros(n)
474	        for i in range(13, n):
475	            yoy_diff[i] = seq[i-1] - seq[i-13]
476	        enh_lag_feats_tr = [lag2, lag3, lag6, roll6_mean, roll12_mean, roll3_std, ewm, yoy_diff]
477	
478	    # D-04: 批次采购附加特征 (附加模式, 不参与Spearman竞争, lag-1避免泄露)
479	    batch_feats_tr = []
480	    if USE_BATCH_FEATURES:
481	        for col in ['has_batch', 'digital_bids']:
482	            if col in df.columns:
483	                vals = df[col].values.astype(np.float64)
484	                # lag-1: 用上月批次信号预测当月需求
485	                lagged = np.zeros(train_len)
486	                lagged[1:] = vals[:train_len-1]
487	                batch_feats_tr.append(lagged)
488	
489	    # D-05: 月度季节剖面 (同月历史扩展均值, 训练集计算, 无泄露)
490	    seasonal_profile_tr = []
491	    if USE_SEASONAL_PROFILE and 'date' in df.columns:
492	        cal_m = df['date'].dt.month.values  # 实际日历月 1-12
493	        sp = np.zeros(train_len)
494	        for i in range(train_len):
495	            same_month_past = [j for j in range(i) if cal_m[j] == cal_m[i]]
496	            if same_month_past:
497	                sp[i] = np.mean(demand_train[same_month_past])
498	            else:
499	                sp[i] = 0  # 首次出现该月, 无历史
500	        seasonal_profile_tr = [sp]
501	
502	    # 实验1: 事件保持特征 (SHOS-based)
503	    evt_feats_tr = []
504	    if USE_EVENT_FEATURES:
505	        d = demand_train
506	        n = train_len
507	        # Stop类: 距上次事件的间隔
508	        gap_since_last = np.zeros(n)
509	        last_event = -999
510	        for i in range(n):
511	            if d[i] > 0: last_event = i
512	            gap_since_last[i] = i - last_event if last_event >= 0 else n
513	        # Hoover类: 事件密度
514	        evt_cnt_6m = np.zeros(n); evt_cnt_12m = np.zeros(n)
515	        cumul_12m = np.zeros(n)
516	        for i in range(n):
517	            evt_cnt_6m[i] = np.sum(d[max(0,i-5):i+1] > 0)
518	            evt_cnt_12m[i] = np.sum(d[max(0,i-11):i+1] > 0)
519	            cumul_12m[i] = np.sum(d[max(0,i-11):i+1])
520	        # Occurrence类: 月份事件频率(平滑)
521	        month_freq = np.zeros(n)
522	        cal_month = np.array([(4+i)%12+1 for i in range(n)])
523	        for i in range(12, n):
524	            past_same_month = [j for j in range(i) if cal_month[j]==cal_month[i]]
525	            if past_same_month:
526	                month_freq[i] = np.mean(d[past_same_month] > 0)
527	        # 注: last_evt_mag/evt_trend/evt_cv 在测试集为常数(仅复制训练集最后值), 已移除
528	        evt_feats_tr = [gap_since_last, evt_cnt_6m, evt_cnt_12m, cumul_12m, month_freq]
529	
530	    # 方案A: 季度末哑变量
531	    qtr_end_cols = []
532	    if USE_QUARTER_DUMMIES:
533	        cal_month_tr = np.array([(4 + i) % 12 + 1 for i in range(train_len)])
534	        is_q1_end = (cal_month_tr == 3).astype(float)
535	        is_q2_end = (cal_month_tr == 6).astype(float)
536	        is_q3_q4_end = ((cal_month_tr == 9) | (cal_month_tr == 12)).astype(float)
537	        qtr_end_cols = [is_q1_end, is_q2_end, is_q3_q4_end]
538	
539	    X_train_raw = np.column_stack([
540	        data_train[:,1:], lag1_tr, lag12_tr, roll3_tr,
541	        is_zero_lag1_tr, is_zero_lag12_tr,
542	        np.sin(2*np.pi*m_train/12), np.cos(2*np.pi*m_train/12),
543	        np.sin(2*np.pi*q_train/4), np.cos(2*np.pi*q_train/4),
544	    ] + evt_feats_tr + qtr_end_cols + enh_lag_feats_tr + batch_feats_tr + seasonal_profile_tr)
545	
546	    # 实验6: 量级特征(lag-1) → 追加到特征矩阵末尾
547	    mag_all = build_magnitude_features(df, material)
548	    if USE_MAG_FEATURES and mag_all.shape[1] > 0:
549	        X_train_raw = np.column_stack([X_train_raw, mag_all[:train_len]])
550	
551	    # Step 3: 防御性NaN清洗 + 特征Scaler仅对训练集fit
552	    X_train_raw = np.nan_to_num(X_train_raw, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
553	    feature_scaler = MinMaxScaler()
554	    X_train = feature_scaler.fit_transform(X_train_raw)
555	    y_train = demand_train.copy()
556	
557	    # Step 4: 测试集特征
558	    lag1_te = np.zeros(N_TEST); lag12_te = np.zeros(N_TEST); roll3_te = np.zeros(N_TEST)
559	    for i in range(N_TEST):
560	        idx = train_len + i
561	        lag1_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-1] if idx>0 else 0
562	        lag12_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-12] if idx>=12 else 0
563	        roll3_te[i] = np.mean(demand_raw[max(0,idx-3):idx])
564	    is_zero_lag1_te = (lag1_te == 0).astype(float)
565	    is_zero_lag12_te = (lag12_te == 0).astype(float)
566	    m_test = (np.arange(train_len+1, train_len+N_TEST+1)) % 12; m_test[m_test==0]=12
567	    q_test = ((np.arange(train_len+1, train_len+N_TEST+1)) // 3) % 4; q_test[q_test==0]=4
568	
569	    # D-02: 测试集增强滞后/滚动特征 (使用demand_raw的历史真实值, 无未来泄露)
570	    enh_lag_feats_te = []
571	    if USE_ENHANCED_LAG_FEATURES:
572	        nt = N_TEST; tl = train_len
573	        lag2_te = np.zeros(nt); lag3_te = np.zeros(nt); lag6_te = np.zeros(nt)
574	        roll6_te = np.zeros(nt); roll12_te = np.zeros(nt); roll3std_te = np.zeros(nt)
575	        ewm_te = np.zeros(nt); yoy_te = np.zeros(nt)
576	        # EWM需要训练集末尾状态作为初始值
577	        alpha = 0.3
578	        ewm_state = 0.0
579	        for i in range(1, tl):
580	            ewm_state = alpha * demand_raw[i-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm_state
581	        for i in range(nt):
582	            idx = tl + i
583	            lag2_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-2] if idx >= 2 else 0
584	            lag3_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-3] if idx >= 3 else 0
585	            lag6_te[i] = demand_raw[idx-6] if idx >= 6 else 0
586	            roll6_te[i] = np.mean(demand_raw[max(0,idx-6):idx]) if idx > 0 else 0
587	            roll12_te[i] = np.mean(demand_raw[max(0,idx-12):idx]) if idx > 0 else 0
588	            roll3std_te[i] = np.std(demand_raw[max(0,idx-3):idx]) if idx > 1 else 0
589	            ewm_state = alpha * demand_raw[idx-1] + (1 - alpha) * ewm_state
590	            ewm_te[i] = ewm_state
591	            yoy_te[i] = (demand_raw[idx-1] - demand_raw[idx-13]) if idx >= 13 else 0
592	        enh_lag_feats_te = [lag2_te, lag3_te, lag6_te, roll6_te, roll12_te, roll3std_te, ewm_te, yoy_te]
593	
594	    # D-04: 测试集批次采购附加特征 (lag-1)
595	    batch_feats_te = []
596	    if USE_BATCH_FEATURES:
597	        for col in ['has_batch', 'digital_bids']:
598	            if col in df.columns:
599	                vals = df[col].values.astype(np.float64)
600	                lagged_te = np.zeros(N_TEST)
601	                for i in range(N_TEST):
602	                    idx = train_len + i
603	                    lagged_te[i] = vals[idx-1] if idx >= 1 else 0
604	                batch_feats_te.append(lagged_te)
605	
606	    # D-05: 测试集月度季节剖面 (用训练集同月均值)
607	    seasonal_profile_te = []
608	    if USE_SEASONAL_PROFILE and 'date' in df.columns:
609	        cal_m_all = df['date'].dt.month.values
610	        # 训练集各月均值
611	        month_means = {}
612	        for mo in range(1, 13):
613	            idxs = [j for j in range(train_len) if cal_m_all[j] == mo]
614	            month_means[mo] = np.mean(demand_train[idxs]) if idxs else 0
615	        sp_te = np.array([month_means.get(cal_m_all[train_len + i], 0) for i in range(N_TEST)])
616	        seasonal_profile_te = [sp_te]
617	
618	    # 实验1: 测试集事件特征
619	    evt_feats_te = []
620	    if USE_EVENT_FEATURES:
621	        d_all = demand_raw; tl = train_len; nt = N_TEST
622	        gap_sl_te = np.zeros(nt); last_ev = -999
623	        for i in range(tl):
624	            if d_all[i] > 0: last_ev = i
625	        for i in range(nt):
626	            idx = tl + i
627	            if d_all[idx] > 0: last_ev = idx
628	            gap_sl_te[i] = idx - last_ev if last_ev >= 0 else tl
629	        ec6_te = np.zeros(nt); ec12_te = np.zeros(nt); cu12_te = np.zeros(nt)
630	        for i in range(nt):
631	            seq = d_all[max(0,tl+i-5):tl+i+1]
632	            seq12 = d_all[max(0,tl+i-11):tl+i+1]
633	            ec6_te[i] = np.sum(np.array(seq) > 0)
634	            ec12_te[i] = np.sum(np.array(seq12) > 0)
635	            cu12_te[i] = np.sum(seq12)
636	        mf_te = np.zeros(nt); cal_m_all = np.array([(4+i)%12+1 for i in range(tl+nt)])
637	        for i in range(nt):
638	            past_same = [j for j in range(tl+i) if cal_m_all[j]==cal_m_all[tl+i]]
639	            if past_same: mf_te[i] = np.mean(d_all[past_same] > 0)
640	        evt_feats_te = [gap_sl_te, ec6_te, ec12_te, cu12_te, mf_te]
641	
642	    qtr_end_cols_te = []
643	    if USE_QUARTER_DUMMIES:
644	        cal_month_te = np.array([(4 + (train_len + i)) % 12 + 1 for i in range(N_TEST)])
645	        is_q1_end_te = (cal_month_te == 3).astype(float)
646	        is_q2_end_te = (cal_month_te == 6).astype(float)
647	        is_q3_q4_end_te = ((cal_month_te == 9) | (cal_month_te == 12)).astype(float)
648	        qtr_end_cols_te = [is_q1_end_te, is_q2_end_te, is_q3_q4_end_te]
649	
650	    X_test_raw = np.column_stack([
651	        data_test[:,1:], lag1_te, lag12_te, roll3_te,
652	        is_zero_lag1_te, is_zero_lag12_te,
653	        np.sin(2*np.pi*m_test/12), np.cos(2*np.pi*m_test/12),
654	        np.sin(2*np.pi*q_test/4), np.cos(2*np.pi*q_test/4),
655	    ] + evt_feats_te + qtr_end_cols_te + enh_lag_feats_te + batch_feats_te + seasonal_profile_te)
656	    if USE_MAG_FEATURES and mag_all.shape[1] > 0:
657	        X_test_raw = np.column_stack([X_test_raw, mag_all[train_len:train_len+N_TEST]])
658	    X_test_raw = np.nan_to_num(X_test_raw, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
659	    X_test = feature_scaler.transform(X_test_raw)
660	    y_test = demand_test.copy()
661	
662	    logger.info(f"  [特征工程] top4={top4}, n_feat={X_train.shape[1]}维, 训练={train_len}月"
663	                + (" [事件特征ON]" if USE_EVENT_FEATURES else "")
664	                + (" [量级特征ON]" if USE_MAG_FEATURES else "")
665	                + (" [增强滞后ON]" if USE_ENHANCED_LAG_FEATURES else "")
666	                + (" [log1p]" if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else ""))
667	    return X_train, y_train, X_test, y_test, feature_scaler
668	
669	def baseline_naive_seasonal(y_train, y_test, period=12):
670	    """季节性朴素预测: yhat_t = y_{t-period} (抄去年同期)"""
671	    preds = np.array([y_train[-period + (i % period)] for i in range(len(y_test))])
672	    y_test_orig = y_test
673	    y_pred_orig = preds
674	    return y_pred_orig, y_test_orig
675	
676	def baseline_persistence(y_train, y_test):
677	    """持久性预测: yhat_{t+1} = y_t (抄上月)"""
678	    preds = np.full(len(y_test), y_train[-1])
679	    y_test_orig = y_test
680	    y_pred_orig = preds
681	    return y_pred_orig, y_test_orig
682	
683	def baseline_sarima(y_train, y_test):
684	    """SARIMA(1,0,1)(1,0,1,12) 基线预测"""
685	    try:
686	        from statsmodels.tsa.statespace.sarimax import SARIMAX
687	        y_train_orig = y_train
688	        model = SARIMAX(y_train_orig, order=(1,0,1), seasonal_order=(1,0,1,12),
689	                        enforce_stationarity=False, enforce_invertibility=False)
690	        fit = model.fit(disp=False)
691	        y_pred_orig = fit.forecast(steps=len(y_test))
692	        y_test_orig = y_test
693	        return np.maximum(y_pred_orig, 0), y_test_orig
694	    except Exception:
695	        # Fallback to Naive Seasonal
696	        return baseline_naive_seasonal(y_train, y_test)
697	
698	
699	def baseline_naive_mean(y_train, y_test):
700	    """历史均值预测: 所有测试月预测为训练集均值"""
701	    preds = np.full(len(y_test), np.mean(y_train))
702	    return preds, y_test
703	
704	def evaluate_model(y_true, y_pred, y_train=None):
705	    """计算 MSE/RMSE/MAE/R²/sMAPE/MASE/WRMSSE
706	
707	    WRMSSE (M5竞赛标准): 分母为训练集季节性naive误差，对零值不敏感。
708	    """
709	    mse = mean_squared_error(y_true, y_pred)
710	    rmse = np.sqrt(mse)
711	    mae = mean_absolute_error(y_true, y_pred)
712	    r2 = r2_score(y_true, y_pred)
713	    smape = np.mean(2*np.abs(y_pred-y_true)/(np.abs(y_pred)+np.abs(y_true)+1e-10))*100
714	    mase_denom = np.mean(np.abs(y_true[1:]-y_true[:-1])) if len(y_true)>1 else 1
715	    mase = mae / max(mase_denom, 1e-10) if mase_denom > 0 else 999
716	    # WRMSSE: 分母是训练集季节性naive的RMSE (M5标准)
717	    wmsse = None
718	    if y_train is not None and len(y_train) >= 12:
719	        seas_naive_pred = np.array([y_train[-12 + (i % 12)] for i in range(len(y_true))])
720	        seas_rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((seas_naive_pred - y_true)**2))
721	        seas_naive_train_err = np.sqrt(np.mean((y_train[12:] - y_train[:-12])**2))
722	        wmsse = round(seas_rmse / max(seas_naive_train_err, 1e-10), 4)
723	    return {'MSE': round(mse,4), 'RMSE': round(rmse,4), 'MAE': round(mae,4),
724	            'R2': round(r2,4), 'sMAPE': round(smape,2), 'MASE': round(mase,4),
725	            'WRMSSE': wmsse,
726	            'zero_acc': round(np.mean((y_true==0)==(y_pred==0)),4)}
727	
728	
729	def crps_score(y_true, pred_quantiles):
730	    """sCRPS: 缩放连续排位概率分数。评估概率预测分布质量。
731	
732	    Args:
733	        y_true: shape (n,) 真实值
734	        pred_quantiles: shape (n, n_quantiles) 每个时间步的各分位数预测
735	    Returns:
736	        sCRPS 值 (数值积分近似)
737	    """
738	    n = len(y_true)
739	    yt = np.array(y_true).reshape(-1)
740	    pq = np.array(pred_quantiles)
741	    if pq.ndim != 2 or pq.shape[0] != n:
742	        return None
743	    q_levels = np.linspace(0, 1, pq.shape[1])  # [0, 1/(k-1), 2/(k-1), ..., 1]
744	    crps_vals = np.zeros(n)
745	    for i in range(n):
746	        fi = pq[i]
747	        indicator = np.array(fi <= yt[i], dtype=float)
748	        integrand = (q_levels - indicator)**2
749	        # 梯形积分: np.trapz removed in numpy 2.0
750	        crps_vals[i] = np.sum((integrand[:-1] + integrand[1:]) / 2 * np.diff(q_levels))
751	    crps = np.mean(crps_vals)
752	    # 缩放因子: 真实值绝对均值
753	    scale = np.mean(np.abs(yt)) if np.mean(np.abs(yt)) > 0 else 1
754	    return round(crps / scale, 4)
755

758	def plot_prediction_comparison(all_results, material):
759	    """预测对比曲线：所有模型预测 vs 真实值"""
760	    fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 1, figsize=(14, 7))
761	    pal = ['#FF0000','#2196F3','#4CAF50','#FF9800','#8E24AA','#00BCD4','#795548','#E91E63']
762	    mks = ['o','^','D','v','s','p','*','h']
763	    unit_map = {'交流避雷器': '台/只', '电容式电压互感器': '台', '交流支柱绝缘子': '只/支',
764	                '断路器保护': '套', '电抗器保护': '套',
765	                '线路保护': '套', '10kV变压器': '台', '变压器保护': '套',
766	                '母线保护': '套', '500kVGIS组合电器': '套'}
767	
768	    results = all_results[material]
769	    test_len = len(results['CatBoost']['y_test'])
770	    end_date = pd.Timestamp.today().replace(day=1) - pd.DateOffset(months=1)
771	    x = pd.date_range(end=end_date, periods=test_len, freq='MS')
772	
773	    # Truth line
774	    ax.plot(x, results['CatBoost']['y_test'][:len(x)], color='black', marker='o',
775	            linestyle='solid', label='真实值', markersize=5, linewidth=2.5, zorder=10)
776	
777	    # All model predictions
778	    for mi, (model_name, preds) in enumerate(results.items()):
779	        if 'y_pred' not in preds: continue
780	        pred = preds['y_pred']
781	        pred_x = x[:len(pred)]
782	        r2_val = preds.get('metrics', {}).get('R2', 0)
783	        lbl = f'{model_name} (R²={r2_val:.3f})'
784	        ax.plot(pred_x, pred, color=pal[mi % len(pal)], marker=mks[mi % len(mks)],
785	                linestyle='--', label=lbl, markersize=4, alpha=0.85)
786	
787	    # Unit
788	    unit = ''
789	    for kw, u in unit_map.items():
790	        if kw in material: unit = u; break
791	    ax.set_xlabel('日期')
792	    ax.set_ylabel(f'需求量({unit})' if unit else '需求量')
793	    ax.set_title(f'{material} — 模型预测对比', fontsize=13, fontweight='bold')
794	    ax.legend(fontsize=7, loc='upper left', bbox_to_anchor=(1.02, 1), framealpha=0.9)
795	    ax.tick_params(axis='x', rotation=30)
796	    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
797	    ax.spines['top'].set_visible(False); ax.spines['right'].set_visible(False)
798	
799	    plt.tight_layout()
800	    path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f'预测对比_{material}.png')
801	    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')
802	    plt.close()
803	    logger.info(f"  [图表] 预测对比图({material}) → {path}")

837	def plot_metrics_comparison(all_metrics):
838	    """模型指标对比：分组柱状图（各物资各模型的四项指标）"""
839	    # 仅使用实际有预测结果的物资（跳过被SKIP_REACTOR_PROTECT等开关排除的）
840	    active_materials = [m for m in MATERIALS if m in all_metrics]
841	    if not active_materials:
842	        return
843	    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(16, 12))
844	    metric_names = ['MSE', 'RMSE', 'MAE', 'R2']
845	    # Dynamically collect all model names from results

847	    for material in active_materials:
848	        for m in all_metrics[material]:
849	            if m not in model_names:
850	                model_names.append(m)
851	    colors = plt.cm.tab20(np.linspace(0, 1, max(len(model_names), 1)))
852	
853	    for ax_idx, metric in enumerate(metric_names):
854	        ax = axes[ax_idx // 2, ax_idx % 2]
855	        x = np.arange(len(active_materials))
856	        width = 0.8 / max(len(model_names), 1)
857	
858	        for i, model_name in enumerate(model_names):
859	            values = []
860	            for material in active_materials:
861	                vals = all_metrics[material].get(model_name, {})
862	                values.append(vals.get(metric, 0))
863	            bars = ax.bar(x + i * width, values, width, label=model_name,
864	                          color=colors[i], alpha=0.85, edgecolor='white')
865	
866	            # 数值标注
867	            for bar, val in zip(bars, values):
868	                ax.text(bar.get_x() + bar.get_width() / 2, bar.get_height() + 0.01,
869	                        f'{val:.4f}', ha='center', va='bottom', fontsize=7, rotation=90)
870	
871	        ax.set_title(metric, fontsize=14, fontweight='bold')
872	        ax.set_xticks(x + width * (len(model_names) - 1) / 2)
873	        ax.set_xticklabels([MATERIAL_LABELS[m] for m in active_materials])
874	        ax.legend(fontsize=8)
875	        ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3, axis='y')
876	
877	    plt.suptitle('模型评估指标对比', fontsize=16, fontweight='bold', y=1.01)
878	    plt.tight_layout()
879	    path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, '指标对比图.png')
880	    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')
881	    plt.close()
882	    logger.info(f"  [图表] 指标对比图 → {path}")

895	        unit = ''; [unit := u for kw,u in {'交流避雷器':'台','电容式电压互感器':'台','交流支柱绝缘子':'只','断路器保护':'套','电抗器保护':'套','线路保护':'套','10kV变压器':'台'}.items() if kw in material]

914	# ===================== log1p逆变换辅助 =====================
915	def _inverse_log1p(y_pred):
916	    """如果USE_LOG1P_TARGET启用, 将预测值从log空间逆变换回原始空间"""
917	    return np.expm1(y_pred) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_pred
918	
919	
920	# ===================== 4. 两阶段预测框架 =====================
921	def _two_stage_fit_predict(X_tr, y_tr, X_te, stage2_regressor, material):
922	    """所有模型共享的两阶段预测: Stage1分类×Stage2回归 = P×Q"""
923	    y_bin = (y_tr > 0).astype(int)
924	    n_pos, n_neg = y_bin.sum(), len(y_bin) - y_bin.sum()
925	    if n_pos < 5 or n_neg < 5 or (y_tr > 0).sum() < 10:
926	        return None, None  # 回退信号
927	
928	    hp = get_hp('stage1_cls', material)
929	    cls = CatBoostClassifier(**hp,
930	        loss_function='Logloss', early_stopping_rounds=30, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
931	    nv = max(6, len(y_tr)//4)
932	    cls.fit(X_tr[:-nv], y_bin[:-nv], eval_set=(X_tr[-nv:], y_bin[-nv:]))
933	    prob = np.clip(cls.predict_proba(X_te)[:,1], 0, 1)
934	
935	    nz = y_tr > 0
936	    nv2 = min(6, nz.sum()//4)
937	    is_cb = hasattr(stage2_regressor, 'get_params')
938	    try:
939	        stage2_regressor.fit(X_tr[nz][:-nv2], y_tr[nz][:-nv2],
940	            eval_set=(X_tr[nz][-nv2:], y_tr[nz][-nv2:]))
941	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
942	        stage2_regressor.fit(X_tr[nz], y_tr[nz])
943	    return prob * np.maximum(stage2_regressor.predict(X_te), 0), cls
944	
945	
946	def run_catboost(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
947	    """CatBoost(朴素): 直接回归, 不使用两阶段框架(消融基线)"""
948	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
949	    hp = get_hp('catboost_direct', material)
950	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
951	        loss_function='RMSE', early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
952	    nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
953	    reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
954	    yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
955	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET: yp = np.expm1(yp)
956	    return yp, y_test, reg.get_feature_importance(), reg
957	
958	def run_catboost_tweedie(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
959	    """CatBoost-Tweedie(单阶段):  复合Poisson-Gamma损失函数直接回归。
960	
961	    关键差异: 不同于两阶段Tweedie(Stage1分类+Stage2的Tweedie loss), 这是单阶段——
962	    用全部69个月(含零值)训练, Tweedie损失天然建模"零=未发标+正=采购量"的生成机制。
963	    与RMSE直接回归的对比: Tweedie在零点有离散概率质量, 不会强迫模型预测非零。
964	
965	    预期: 波动数据(CV>1)上 Tweedie > RMSE(直接) >> 两阶段
966	    """
967	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
968	    hp = get_hp('catboost_tweedie', material)
969	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
970	        loss_function='Tweedie:variance_power=1.5',
971	        early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
972	    nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
973	    reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
974	    yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
975	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET: yp = np.expm1(yp)
976	    return yp, y_test, reg.get_feature_importance(), reg
977	
978	
979	# ===================== 实验4: 分位数回归 (可取消) =====================
980	def run_catboost_quantile(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
981	    """分位数回归: 训练 alpha=0.1/0.5/0.9 三个分位数模型。
982	
983	    中位数(0.5)作为点预测, (0.1, 0.9)作为80%预测区间。
984	    优势: 不做硬判决"有/无需求", 而是输出"不确定区间"。
985	    当下限>0 → 高置信度有需求; 上限≈0 → 高置信度没有。
986	    """
987	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
988	    hp = get_hp('quantile', material)
989	    preds = {}
990	    for alpha in [0.1, 0.5, 0.9]:
991	        reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
992	            loss_function=f'Quantile:alpha={alpha}',
993	            early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
994	        nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
995	        reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
996	        p = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
997	        if USE_LOG1P_TARGET: p = np.expm1(p)
998	        preds[alpha] = p
999	    yp = preds[0.5]  # 中位数点预测
1000	    logger.info(f'  [分位数] 80%区间宽度均值={np.mean(preds[0.9]-preds[0.1]):.2f}')
1001	    return yp, y_test, None, (preds[0.1], preds[0.9])
1002	
1003	
1004	def run_catboost_2s(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):

1007	    hp = get_hp('catboost_2s', material)
1008	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**hp,
1009	        loss_function='RMSE', early_stopping_rounds=50, random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0)
1010	    yp, _ = _two_stage_fit_predict(X_train_factors, y_tr_log, X_test_factors, reg, material)
1011	    if yp is None:
1012	        logger.warning(f'  [CatBoost-2S] 回退到直接回归')
1013	        nv = min(12, len(y_tr_log)//4)
1014	        reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))
1015	        yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
1016	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET and yp is not None: yp = np.expm1(yp)
1017	    return yp, y_test, reg.get_feature_importance(), reg

1030	        reg.fit(X_train_factors[:-nv], y_tr_log[:-nv], eval_set=(X_train_factors[-nv:], y_tr_log[-nv:]))

1038	# ===================== 两阶段预测 (论文核心创新) =====================
1039	def run_two_stage(df_all, X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test,
1040	                   material):
1041	    """两阶段预测:
1042	    Stage 1: CatBoost 二分类 — 预测当月是否有需求(>0)
1043	    Stage 2: CatBoost 回归 — 对有需求的月份预测需求量
1044	    最终 = P(有需求) × 预测量
1045	    """
1046	    demand_raw = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
1047	    y_train_binary = (demand_raw > 0).astype(int)
1048	    n_pos = y_train_binary.sum(); n_neg = len(y_train_binary) - n_pos
1049	    if n_pos < 5 or n_neg < 5:
1050	        logger.warning(f"  [两阶段] 正负样本不均衡(pos={n_pos},neg={n_neg}), 回退到CatBoost")
1051	        return run_catboost(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material)
1052	
1053	    # Stage 1: 分类器
1054	    hp_cls = get_hp('twostage_cls', material)
1055	    cls = CatBoostClassifier(**hp_cls,
1056	        loss_function='Logloss', early_stopping_rounds=30,
1057	        random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0
1058	    )
1059	    n_val = max(6, min(12, len(y_train)//4))
1060	    X_cls_tr, X_cls_val = X_train_factors[:-n_val], X_train_factors[-n_val:]
1061	    y_cls_tr, y_cls_val = y_train_binary[:-n_val], y_train_binary[-n_val:]
1062	    cls.fit(X_cls_tr, y_cls_tr, eval_set=(X_cls_val, y_cls_val))
1063	    prob_test = cls.predict_proba(X_test_factors)[:, 1]
1064	    prob_test = np.clip(prob_test, 0, 1)
1065	
1066	    # Stage 2: 回归 (仅对非零训练数据)
1067	    nonzero_mask = demand_raw > 0
1068	    if nonzero_mask.sum() < 10:
1069	        logger.warning(f"  [两阶段] 非零样本过少({nonzero_mask.sum()}), 回退到CatBoost baseline")
1070	        return run_catboost(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material)
1071	
1072	    X_nz = X_train_factors[nonzero_mask]
1073	    y_nz = demand_raw[nonzero_mask]  # demand_raw已log1p, y_train未变换
1074	
1075	    reg = CatBoostRegressor(**get_hp('twostage_reg', material),
1076	        loss_function='RMSE', early_stopping_rounds=50,
1077	        random_seed=RANDOM_SEED, verbose=0
1078	    )
1079	    n_val2 = min(6, len(y_nz)//4)
1080	    reg.fit(X_nz[:-n_val2], y_nz[:-n_val2],
1081	            eval_set=(X_nz[-n_val2:], y_nz[-n_val2:]))
1082	    qty_test = reg.predict(X_test_factors)
1083	
1084	    # 反归一化
1085	    y_test_orig = y_test
1086	    qty_pred_orig = qty_test
1087	    # 最终 = 概率 × 预测量
1088	    y_pred_orig = prob_test * np.maximum(qty_pred_orig, 0)
1089	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET: y_pred_orig = np.expm1(np.maximum(y_pred_orig, 0))
1090	    cls_acc = np.mean((prob_test > 0.5).astype(int) == (y_test_orig > 0).astype(int))
1091	    logger.info(f"  [两阶段] Stage1分类准确率={cls_acc:.2%}, 非零训练样本={nonzero_mask.sum()}")
1092	    return y_pred_orig, y_test_orig, None, (cls, reg)
1093	
1094

1096	def run_ridge_2s(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
1097	    """TwoStage-Ridge: 两阶段=分类×Ridge回归 (小样本更稳定)"""
1098	    from sklearn.linear_model import RidgeCV
1099	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
1100	    hp = get_hp('ridge_2s', material)
1101	    reg = RidgeCV(**hp)
1102	    nz = y_tr_log > 0
1103	    yp, _ = _two_stage_fit_predict(X_train_factors, y_tr_log, X_test_factors, reg, material)
1104	    if yp is None:
1105	        reg.fit(X_train_factors, y_tr_log)
1106	        yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
1107	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET and yp is not None: yp = np.expm1(yp)
1108	    return yp, y_test, None, reg

1111	def run_elasticnet_2s(X_train_factors, y_train, X_test_factors, y_test, material):
1112	    """TwoStage-ElasticNet: 两阶段=分类×ElasticNet(L1+L2, 小样本强正则)"""
1113	    from sklearn.linear_model import ElasticNetCV
1114	    y_tr_log = np.log1p(y_train) if USE_LOG1P_TARGET else y_train
1115	    hp = get_hp('elasticnet_2s', material)
1116	    nz = y_tr_log > 0
1117	    reg = ElasticNetCV(**hp, cv=min(5, nz.sum()), max_iter=5000, random_state=42)
1118	    yp, _ = _two_stage_fit_predict(X_train_factors, y_tr_log, X_test_factors, reg, material)
1119	    if yp is None:
1120	        reg.fit(X_train_factors, y_tr_log)
1121	        yp = np.maximum(reg.predict(X_test_factors), 0)
1122	    if USE_LOG1P_TARGET and yp is not None: yp = np.expm1(yp)
1123	    return yp, y_test, None, reg
